📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本周多篇登革热研究覆盖媒介生物防控、预测模型、临床生物标志物、疫苗免疫学及快速诊断技术。一项荟萃分析显示全区域释放Wolbachia蚊可使发病率下降约85%;微流控与RT-LAMP等快速检测工具持续优化,以支持资源有限地区的现场分型需求。
  • Wolbachia蚊全区域释放与登革热发病率显著下降相关,合并IRR为0.15(95%CI 0.05–0.39),对应估计下降85% [10.1093/trstmh/trag063]。
  • 在泄漏安全的时间序列设计中,基于监测数据的LSTM模型在2025年外部时间验证中表现最佳,MAE为7.951,R2为0.698 [10.21203/rs.3.rs-10307931/v1]。
  • 泰国2005–2024年监测显示25岁以上成人病例占比从19.56%升至36.16%,Kitagawa分解提示主要驱动力为流行病学因素而非人口老龄化 [10.1371/journal.pntd.0014697]。
  • 印度尼西亚坦格朗县全年监测中DENV-2占比32.8%,继发感染与重症显著相关(调整后OR=10.25,95%CI 1.71–197.90) [10.7883/yoken.jjid.2025.232]。
  • Recent dengue literature spans vector biocontrol, forecasting models, clinical biomarkers, vaccine immunology, and rapid diagnostics.
  • Full-area Wolbachia mosquito releases were associated with a pooled incidence rate ratio of 0.15 (95% CI 0.05–0.39), an estimated 85% reduction in dengue incidence, outperforming partial-area deployments [10.1093/trstmh/trag063].
  • Under a leakage-safe temporal design, an LSTM forecasting model achieved the strongest 2025 out-of-time holdout performance (MAE 7.951, R2 0.698), exceeding classical baselines in Marikina City [10.21203/rs.3.rs-10307931/v1].
  • Thailand surveillance (2005–2024) shows the share of adult cases aged ≥25 years nearly doubled from 19.56% to 36.16%, with Kitagawa decomposition attributing +178.7% of the shift to epidemiological rather than demographic factors [10.1371/journal.pntd.0014697].
  • Year-round surveillance in Tangerang, Indonesia identified DENV-2 as the dominant serotype (32.8%) and found secondary infection strongly associated with severe disease (adjusted OR 10.25, 95% CI 1.71–197.90) [10.7883/yoken.jjid.2025.232].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期登革病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 皮内拉斯县加大了蚊虫控制力度,以限制 登革热 的传播。皮尼拉斯县政府报告了这一行动,作为其公共卫生举措的一部分。 [news-ee852165561b8592]。
  • 在一项 2 期临床试验中测试了一种实验性单克隆抗体 (Dengue-mAb) 对 登革病毒 的治疗效果,结果显示,与安慰剂相比,所有剂量水平的病毒血症和发烧都能在 24 小时内快速清除。该研究还指出,没有记录到因果相关的严重不良事件。
  • 奥兰治县已确诊一例本地获得性 登革热 病例,佛罗里达州已有 40 多例病例。卫生官员报告了这一确认。 [news-9e35db79b33ea660]。
  • 一项研究发现,与未接受抗血栓治疗 (ATT) 的患者相比,接受双重抗血小板治疗 (DAPT) 或口服抗凝治疗 (OAC) 的登革热患者在 诊断 治疗 7 和 30 天内出现大出血的几率更高。这些发现是由李宝增领导的研究人员报告的。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Dengue virus news developments.
  • Pinellas County has increased mosquito control efforts to limit the spread of Dengue Fever. This action was reported by the Pinellas County government as part of their public health initiatives. [news-ee852165561b8592].
  • An experimental monoclonal antibody (Dengue-mAb) was tested in a phase 2 clinical trial for its therapeutic effect against dengue virus, showing rapid clearance of viremia and fever within 24 hours at all dose levels compared to placebo.
  • A locally-acquired case of dengue fever has been confirmed in Orange County, and more than 40 cases across Florida. Health officials reported the confirmation. [news-9e35db79b33ea660].
  • A study found that dengue patients on dual antiplatelet therapy (DAPT) or oral anticoagulant therapy (OAC) had higher rates of major bleeding within 7 and 30 days of diagnosis compared to those not receiving antithrombotic therapy (ATT).

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: PLOS Neglected Tropical Diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-31  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.pntd.0014027  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 20, 8, e0014027
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

布基纳法索 登革热 的知识、态度和实践 (KAP):布基纳法索全国家庭调查的结果。

Knowledge, Attitudes and Practices (KAP) on Dengue Fever in Burkina Faso: Findings from the national household survey in Burkina Faso.
作者： Nicolas Ouédraogo, Siaka Débé, Harouna Soré, Farida Tiendrébégo, Gérard Wendyam Nonkani, Guillaume Sylvestre Sanou, Réné Kinda, Adama Ganou, Casimire Wendlamita Tarama, Sonia Ilboudo
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 登革热 是布基纳法索一个新兴的公共卫生问题,随着 暴发 的增加和人口意识方面的数据差距不断扩大。本研究评估了全国所有卫生区域与 登革热 相关的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。方法/主要结果 2022 年 5 月,采用分层两阶段整群抽样设计进行了全国性横断面家庭调查。每个地区选择 1 个农村地区和 1 个城市地区。使用结构化调查问卷对户主或其代表进行了访谈。数据以电子方式收集并使用 Stata 进行分析。共有 1,568 名参与者参加(52.0% 男性;48.0% 女性)。总体而言,66.3%的人听说过登革热,城市知晓率高于农村。只有49.0%的人正确识别蚊虫叮咬为传播的模式,29.9%的人不知道什么是登革热。大多数受访者(88.3%)表示登革热会影响每个人。在预防方面,80.2%的人表示睡觉时使用蚊帐,49.0%的人消除积水,45.4%的人使用驱蚊剂。实际上,67.6% 的人一直在蚊帐下睡觉,82.6% 的人使用驱虫剂。几乎所有受访者(98.6%)表示,如果出现登革热症状,他们会咨询卫生专业人员。然而,对治疗和疫苗接种的了解有限,分别有46.5%和56.6%的人表示不知道是否存在药物或疫苗。结论/意义 本研究强调了布基纳法索对登革热相关 KAP 的认识中等,但存在巨大的知识差距和城乡差异。加强社区教育和综合 媒介 控制策略对于改善预防和减少登革热 媒介 至关重要。作者摘要 登革热 是一种蚊媒疾病,正在世界许多地区传播,包括西非。在布基纳法索,近年来有 暴发 的报道,但人们对社区对这种疾病的了解以及他们如何保护自己知之甚少。在这项研究中,我们进行了一项覆盖全国所有卫生区域的全国家庭调查,以了解人们对登革热的相关知识、看法和预防实践。我们发现,虽然许多人听说过登革热,但仍然存在重大差距。很大一部分人不知道该疾病是如何传播的,也不知道 治疗 或 疫苗 是否存在。据报道,人们采取了睡在蚊帐下和清除积水等预防措施,但这些做法并非各地都一致,特别是在城市和农村地区之间。令人鼓舞的是,几乎所有受访者都表示,如果出现症状,他们会寻求医疗专业人员的护理。我们的工作提供了布基纳法索与登革热相关的社区意识和行为的第一个全国性图片。这些发现强调需要加强健康教育和社区参与,以改善预防并支持控制蚊媒疾病的持续努力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 是布基纳法索的一个新兴公共卫生问题,暴发 和人口意识方面的数据差距不断增加。本研究旨在评估所有卫生区域与 登革热 相关的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。
设计与对象
2022 年 5 月,采用分层两阶段整群抽样设计进行了全国横断面住户调查。每个地区选择 1 个农村地区和 1 个城市地区。共有 1,568 名参与者参加(52.0% 男性;48.0% 女性)。
核心方法
使用结构化调查问卷对户主或其代表进行了访谈。数据以电子方式收集并使用 Stata 进行分析。该研究在全国范围内进行了家庭调查,覆盖全国所有卫生区域,以了解人们对登革热的相关知识、看法和预防做法。
主要结果
总体而言,66.3%的人听说过登革热,城市知晓率高于农村。只有49.0%的人正确识别蚊虫叮咬为传播的模式,29.9%的人不知道什么是登革热。据报道,人们采取了睡在蚊帐下和清除积水等预防措施,但这些做法并非各地都一致,特别是在城市和农村地区之间。几乎所有受访者(98.6%)表示,如果出现症状,他们会寻求医疗专业人员的护理。
解释与创新
该研究强调了布基纳法索对登革热相关 KAP 的认识中等,但存在巨大的知识差距和城乡差异。它提供了布基纳法索与登革热相关的社区意识和行为的第一张全国图片。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要加强健康教育和社区参与,以改善预防并支持控制蚊媒疾病的持续努力。该研究为设计有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供了重要见解,以解决登革热相关实践中的知识差距和差异。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。由于依赖自我报告的数据以及城乡差异可能影响普遍性,因此证据强度中等。

Knowledge, Attitudes and Practices (KAP) on Dengue Fever in Burkina Faso: Findings from the national household survey in Burkina Faso.

Authors: Nicolas Ouédraogo, Siaka Débé, Harouna Soré, Farida Tiendrébégo, Gérard Wendyam Nonkani, Guillaume Sylvestre Sanou, Réné Kinda, Adama Ganou, Casimire Wendlamita Tarama, Sonia Ilboudo
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Dengue fever is an emerging public health concern in Burkina Faso, with increasing outbreaks and data gaps in population awareness. This study assessed knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) related to dengue fever in all health regions of the country. Methodology/Principal Findings A nationwide cross-sectional household survey was conducted in May 2022 using a stratified two-stage cluster sampling design. One rural and one urban area were selected per region. Heads of households or their representatives were interviewed using a structured questionnaire. Data were collected electronically and analyzed using Stata. A total of 1,568 participants were enrolled (52.0% male; 48.0% female). Overall, 66.3% had heard of dengue, with higher awareness in urban than rural areas. Only 49.0% correctly identified mosquito bites as the mode of transmission, and 29.9% did not know what dengue is. Most respondents (88.3%) stated that dengue can affect everyone. Regarding prevention, 80.2% reported sleeping under a mosquito net, 49.0% eliminated stagnant water, and 45.4% used mosquito repellents. In practice, 67.6% consistently slept under mosquito nets and 82.6% used repellents. Almost all respondents (98.6%) reported that they would consult a health professional if they had symptoms of dengue. However, knowledge about treatment and vaccination was limited, with 46.5% and 56.6% respectively reporting not knowing whether drugs or vaccines exist. Conclusions/Significance This study highlights moderate awareness but substantial knowledge gaps and urban–rural disparities in dengue-related KAP in Burkina Faso. Strengthening community-based education and integrated vector control strategies is essential to improve prevention and reduce dengue transmission. Author Summary Dengue fever is a mosquito-borne disease that is spreading in many parts of the world, including West Africa. In Burkina Faso, outbreaks have been reported in recent years, but little is known about what communities understand about the disease or how they protect themselves. In this study, we conducted a nationwide household survey covering all health regions of the country to explore people’s knowledge, perceptions, and prevention practices related to dengue. We found that while many people had heard about dengue, important gaps remain. A significant proportion did not know how the disease is transmitted or whether treatments or vaccines exist. Preventive actions such as sleeping under mosquito nets and removing standing water were reported, but these practices were not consistent everywhere, especially between urban and rural areas. Encouragingly, almost all respondents said they would seek care from a health professional if they developed symptoms. Our work provides the first national picture of community awareness and behaviors related to dengue in Burkina Faso. These findings highlight the need for strengthened health education and community engagement to improve prevention and support ongoing efforts to control mosquito-borne diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is an emerging public health concern in Burkina Faso, with increasing outbreaks and data gaps in population awareness. This study aimed to assess knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) related to dengue fever across all health regions.
Design and population
A nationwide cross-sectional household survey was conducted in May 2022 using a stratified two-stage cluster sampling design. One rural and one urban area were selected per region. A total of 1,568 participants were enrolled (52.0% male; 48.0% female).
Core methods
Heads of households or their representatives were interviewed using a structured questionnaire. Data were collected electronically and analyzed using Stata. The study conducted a nationwide household survey covering all health regions of the country to explore people’s knowledge, perceptions, and prevention practices related to dengue.
Main results
Overall, 66.3% had heard of dengue, with higher awareness in urban than rural areas. Only 49.0% correctly identified mosquito bites as the mode of transmission, and 29.9% did not know what dengue is. Preventive actions such as sleeping under mosquito nets and removing standing water were reported, but these practices were not consistent everywhere, especially between urban and rural areas. Almost all respondents (98.6%) reported that they would seek care from a health professional if they developed symptoms.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights moderate awareness but substantial knowledge gaps and urban–rural disparities in dengue-related KAP in Burkina Faso. It provides the first national picture of community awareness and behaviors related to dengue in Burkina Faso.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need for strengthened health education and community engagement to improve prevention and support ongoing efforts to control mosquito-borne diseases. The study provides essential insights for designing targeted public health interventions to address knowledge gaps and disparities in dengue-related practices.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on self-reported data and the potential for urban–rural disparities to affect generalizability.

Journal: Vector borne and zoonotic diseases (Larchmont, N.Y.)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-31  |  DOI: 10.1177/15303667261484025  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 15303667261484025
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

将微流控芯片集成到 媒介 传播的疾病 监测:技术突破和持续的障碍。

Integrating Microfluidics Chips into Vector-Borne Disease Surveillance: Technological Breakthroughs and Persistent Hurdles.
作者： Jun Feng, Lin Ai, Min Chen, Huan-Yu Wu
摘要中文翻译背景:媒介 传播的疾病(VBD),如疟疾、登革热和寨卡病毒感染,仍然是全球严重的健康负担,特别是在资源有限的地区。传统的诊断方法,包括显微镜检查、酶联免疫吸附测定和聚合酶链反应,由于依赖集中实验室、处理时间长和运营成本高而面临很大的局限性,阻碍了偏远或服务不足地区的及时疾病管理。目标:微流控诊断已成为一种变革性解决方案,通过小型化流体控制和多重检测功能提供便携性、系统集成和高灵敏度。这些设备通过将样品制备、目标放大和信号读出结合到单个芯片中,实现快速现场 诊断,满足了对即时检测不断增长的需求。方法:本综述是在对 2010 年 1 月至 2026 年 6 月期间 PubMed、Web of Science 和 Scopus 数据库进行系统文献检索后进行的。检索词包括“微流体”、“芯片实验室”、“护理点”、“媒介 传播疾病”、“疟疾”、“登革热”、“寨卡”、“西尼罗河病毒”、“莱姆病”、和“CRISPR”。纳入标准为(1)同行评审的文章,报告微流控芯片在VBD病原体检测中的应用或媒介 监测;(2) 描述可现场部署或经过临床验证的平台的研究;(3) 英文出版物。排除标准包括未经实验验证的纯理论建模研究和基于非芯片的微流体系统。经过标题/摘要筛选和全文评估后,总共选择了 87 项研究进行定性综合。我们严格评估现实世界的应用场景,包括乌干达疟疾 监测 的现场部署和巴西和东南亚 暴发 对虫媒病毒感染的反应,同时解决持续存在的转化障碍,例如制造标准化、样品预处理复杂性和成本效益。结果:主要研究结果表明,与传统实验室方法相比,在小型化平台上成功集成了样本到答案工作流程,显着减少了检测时间和操作依赖性。确定的关键挑战包括资源有限环境下的制造可扩展性、复杂的样品预处理要求以及降低成本以确保在流行地区可持续部署的迫切需要。结论:本综述旨在探索传统诊断之外的新兴整合策略,重点关注基于 CRISPR 的检测、人工智能驱动的图像分析和可扩展的制造工艺等方法,以加强全球 VBD 控制网络并改善服务不足人群的临床结果。结论:本综述旨在探索传统诊断之外的新兴整合策略,重点关注基于 CRISPR 的检测、简化的图像分析和可扩展的制造工艺等方法,以加强全球 VBD 控制网络并改善服务不足人群的临床结果。提出了在流行地区分阶段部署的具体建议,包括试点测试计划和技术转让伙伴关系,以加速转化影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热等 媒介 传播疾病给全球带来了巨大的健康负担,特别是在资源有限的环境中,传统诊断在及时性、便携性和成本方面面临严重限制。该研究强调需要创新的诊断方法来克服这些障碍并实现有效的疾病管理。
设计与对象
本系统综述综合了 2010 年 1 月至 2026 年 6 月发表的有关 媒介 传播疾病病原体检测的微流控芯片应用和 媒介 监测 的同行评审文献。纳入的研究报告了用于检测病原体(包括 登革病毒)的可现场部署或临床验证的微流控平台。
核心方法
使用与微流体、即时检测和 Z​​XQTERMAEQXZ 传播疾病相关的目标术语在 PubMed、Web of Science 和 Scopus 中进行了系统的文献检索。纳入标准需要对基于微流控芯片的检测平台进行实验验证,而排除标准则删除了纯粹的理论建模研究和非基于芯片的微流控系统。
主要结果
该审查确定了 87 项研究,这些研究证明了在小型化微流体平台上成功集成了样本到答案工作流程,用于 媒介 传播的疾病检测,与传统实验室方法相比,显着减少了检测时间和操作依赖性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明微流体诊断可以通过集成的样品制备、扩增和检测实现快速现场 诊断,从而改变 媒介 传播疾病的现场检测。新颖之处在于综合现实世界的应用场景并识别特定于现场部署的转化障碍。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持微流体诊断通过减少诊断延迟和运营成本来加强流行地区登革热和其他虫媒病毒感染的 监测 和临床管理的潜力。审查提出了分阶段部署战略和技术转让伙伴关系,以加速实际实施。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏针对登革热特定平台的定量性能指标以及依赖于异质性研究的定性综合,证据基础受到限制。由于已确定的转化障碍(包括制造可扩展性、样品预处理复杂性和成本效益问题),证据的强度是中等的。

Integrating Microfluidics Chips into Vector-Borne Disease Surveillance: Technological Breakthroughs and Persistent Hurdles.

Authors: Jun Feng, Lin Ai, Min Chen, Huan-Yu Wu
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Vector-borne diseases (VBDs), such as malaria, dengue, and Zika virus infections, remain a critical global health burden, particularly in resource-limited regions. Conventional diagnostic methods, including microscopy, enzyme-linked immunosorbent assays, and polymerase chain reaction, face significant limitations due to their reliance on centralized laboratories, lengthy processing times, and high operational costs, hindering timely disease management in remote or underserved areas. OBJECTIVE: Microfluidic diagnostics have emerged as a transformative solution, offering portability, system integration, and high sensitivity through miniaturized fluidic control and multiplexed detection capabilities. These devices enable rapid, on-site diagnosis by combining sample preparation, target amplification, and signal readout into a single chip, aligning with the growing demand for point-of-care testing. METHODS: This review was conducted following a systematic literature search of PubMed, Web of Science, and Scopus databases from January 2010 to June 2026. Search terms included combinations of "microfluidic," "lab-on-a-chip," "point-of-care," "vector-borne disease," "malaria," "dengue," "Zika," "West Nile virus," "Lyme disease," and "CRISPR." Inclusion criteria were (1) peer-reviewed articles reporting microfluidic chip applications for VBD pathogen detection or vector surveillance; (2) studies describing field-deployable or clinically validated platforms; and (3) publications in English. Exclusion criteria included purely theoretical modeling studies without experimental validation and nonchip-based microfluidic systems. A total of 87 studies were selected for qualitative synthesis after title/abstract screening and full-text evaluation. We critically evaluate real-world application scenarios, including field deployments for malaria surveillance in Uganda and outbreak responses to arboviral infections in Brazil and Southeast Asia, while addressing persistent translational barriers such as manufacturing standardization, sample pretreatment complexity, and cost-effectiveness. RESULTS: Key findings demonstrate the successful integration of sample-to-answer workflows on miniaturized platforms, achieving significant reductions in detection time and operational dependencies compared with conventional laboratory methods. Critical challenges identified include manufacturing scalability for resource-limited settings, complex sample pretreatment requirements, and the pressing need for cost reduction to ensure sustainable deployment in endemic regions. CONCLUSION: This review aims to explore emerging integration strategies beyond conventional diagnostics, focusing on approaches such as CRISPR-based assays, artificial intelligence-driven image analysis, and scalable manufacturing processes to strengthen global VBD control networks and improve clinical outcomes in underserved populations. CONCLUSION: This review aims to explore emerging integration strategies beyond conventional diagnostics, focusing on approaches such as CRISPR-based assays, simplified image analysis, and scalable manufacturing processes to strengthen global VBD control networks and improve clinical outcomes in underserved populations. Specific recommendations for phased deployment in endemic regions, including pilot beta-testing programs and technology transfer partnerships, are proposed to accelerate translational impact.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Vector-borne diseases such as dengue impose a substantial global health burden, particularly in resource-limited settings where conventional diagnostics face critical limitations in timeliness, portability, and cost. The study highlights the need for innovative diagnostic approaches to overcome these barriers and enable effective disease management.
Design and population
This systematic review synthesizes peer-reviewed literature on microfluidic chip applications for vector-borne disease pathogen detection and vector surveillance published between January 2010 and June 2026. Included studies reported field-deployable or clinically validated microfluidic platforms for detecting pathogens including dengue virus.
Core methods
A systematic literature search was conducted in PubMed, Web of Science, and Scopus using targeted terms related to microfluidics, point-of-care testing, and vector-borne diseases. Inclusion criteria required experimental validation of microfluidic chip-based detection platforms, while exclusion criteria removed purely theoretical modeling studies and non-chip-based microfluidic systems.
Main results
The review identified 87 studies demonstrating successful integration of sample-to-answer workflows on miniaturized microfluidic platforms for vector-borne disease detection, achieving significant reductions in detection time and operational dependencies compared with conventional laboratory methods.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that microfluidic diagnostics can transform point-of-care testing for vector-borne diseases by enabling rapid, on-site diagnosis through integrated sample preparation, amplification, and detection. The novelty lies in synthesizing real-world application scenarios and identifying translational barriers specific to field deployment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the potential of microfluidic diagnostics to strengthen surveillance and clinical management of dengue and other arboviral infections in endemic regions by reducing diagnostic delays and operational costs. The review proposes phased deployment strategies and technology transfer partnerships to accelerate practical implementation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence base is limited by the absence of quantitative performance metrics for dengue-specific platforms and reliance on qualitative synthesis of heterogeneous studies. The strength of evidence is moderate due to identified translational barriers including manufacturing scalability, sample pretreatment complexity, and cost-effectiveness concerns.

印度尼西亚坦格朗县全年登革热 监测(2024 年):临床和病毒学特征及生物标志物评估。

Year-Round Dengue Surveillance in Tangerang Regency, Indonesia (2024): Clinical and Virological Features with Biomarker Evaluation.
作者： Dewi Lokida, Herman Kosasih, Susilowati Susilowati, Nathania Nadia Budiman, Salmawati Salmawati, Adhella Menur Naysilla, Ivana Yulian Hendarsin, Cintya Naya Danastri, Atindriya Tri Iswari, Sylvia Victoria
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热仍然是印度尼西亚的一个主要公共卫生问题。本研究旨在描述印度尼西亚坦格朗县 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染的 流行病学、临床特征、免疫应答 和基因型特征。
设计与对象
A year-round prospective 监测 was conducted from January 2024 to January 2025 at a district hospital and two primary health centers.在 404 名参与者中,240 人 (59.4%) 确诊登革热。
核心方法
使用登革热NS1、IgM/IgG快速检测、ELISA和RT-PCR对年龄≥4岁的发热患者进行检测,并对血清生物标志物(血管生成素-2、IL-6、铁蛋白、sCD163)进行定量。测序鉴定出DENV-1基因型I和IV、DENV-2 Cosmopolitan、DENV-3基因型I和DENV-4基因型IIa;一种 DENV-4 分离株携带 EDIII E384 D→H 取代,值得进一步研究。
主要结果
Among RT-PCR-serotyped cases (n=192), the distribution was DENV-2 63/192 (32.8%), DENV-1 60/192 (31.3%), DENV-3 47/192 (24.5%), and DENV-4 22/192 (11.5%). Among hospitalized dengue cases (n=144), secondary infection (131/144, 91.0%) was associated with severe disease (adjusted OR=10.25; 95% CI: 1.71-197.90, p=0.034).
解释与创新
该研究深入了解了 登革病毒 感染的血清型分布和临床特征,强调了继发感染与严重疾病之间的关联。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 监测 策略提供信息,并强调开发 疫苗 的必要性,以诱导平衡的血清型特异性免疫来支持登革热控制。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖单一地区医院和初级卫生中心而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。由于观察设计和潜在的混杂因素,证据的强度中等。

Year-Round Dengue Surveillance in Tangerang Regency, Indonesia (2024): Clinical and Virological Features with Biomarker Evaluation.

Authors: Dewi Lokida, Herman Kosasih, Susilowati Susilowati, Nathania Nadia Budiman, Salmawati Salmawati, Adhella Menur Naysilla, Ivana Yulian Hendarsin, Cintya Naya Danastri, Atindriya Tri Iswari, Sylvia Victoria
Original abstract (English)
Dengue remains a major public health concern in Indonesia. This study aimed to describe the epidemiology, clinical characteristics, immune responses, and genotype characterization of dengue virus (DENV) infections in Tangerang Regency, Indonesia. A year-round prospective surveillance was conducted from January 2024-January 2025 at a district hospital and two primary health centers. Febrile patients aged ≥4 years were tested using dengue NS1, IgM/IgG rapid tests, ELISA, and RT-PCR, and serum biomarkers (angiopoietin-2, IL-6, ferritin, sCD163) were quantified. Of 404 participants, 240 (59.4%) had confirmed dengue. Among RT-PCR-serotyped cases (n=192), the distribution was DENV-2 63/192 (32.8%), DENV-1 60/192 (31.3%), DENV-3 47/192 (24.5%), and DENV-4 22/192 (11.5%). Among hospitalized dengue cases (n=144), secondary infection (131/144, 91.0%) was associated with severe disease (adjusted OR=10.25; 95% CI: 1.71-197.90, p=0.034). Biomarker levels were elevated in dengue cases but did not discriminate severity, indicating the need for validation in larger cohorts using standardized definitions. Sequencing identified DENV-1 genotypes I and IV, DENV-2 Cosmopolitan, DENV-3 genotype I, and DENV-4 genotype IIa; one DENV-4 isolate carried an EDIII E384 D→H substitution warranting further investigation. Continued genomic surveillance and vaccine development that induces balanced, serotype-specific immunity are needed to support national and global dengue control.

Question and background
Dengue remains a major public health concern in Indonesia. This study aimed to describe the epidemiology, clinical characteristics, immune responses, and genotype characterization of dengue virus (DENV) infections in Tangerang Regency, Indonesia.
Design and population
A year-round prospective surveillance was conducted from January 2024 to January 2025 at a district hospital and two primary health centers. Of 404 participants, 240 (59.4%) had confirmed dengue.
Core methods
Febrile patients aged ≥4 years were tested using dengue NS1, IgM/IgG rapid tests, ELISA, and RT-PCR, and serum biomarkers (angiopoietin-2, IL-6, ferritin, sCD163) were quantified. Sequencing identified DENV-1 genotypes I and IV, DENV-2 Cosmopolitan, DENV-3 genotype I, and DENV-4 genotype IIa; one DENV-4 isolate carried an EDIII E384 D→H substitution warranting further investigation.
Main results
Among RT-PCR-serotyped cases (n=192), the distribution was DENV-2 63/192 (32.8%), DENV-1 60/192 (31.3%), DENV-3 47/192 (24.5%), and DENV-4 22/192 (11.5%). Among hospitalized dengue cases (n=144), secondary infection (131/144, 91.0%) was associated with severe disease (adjusted OR=10.25; 95% CI: 1.71-197.90, p=0.034).
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides insights into the serotype distribution and clinical features of dengue virus infections, highlighting the association between secondary infection and severe disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance strategies and emphasize the need for vaccine development that induces balanced, serotype-specific immunity to support dengue control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a single district hospital and primary health centers, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational design and potential for confounding factors.

儿童中性粒细胞淋巴细胞比率、血小板淋巴细胞比率和 登革热 之间的相关性 - 横断面研究。

CORRELATION BETWEEN NEUTROPHIL LYMPHOCYTE RATIO, PLATELET LYMPHOCYTE RATIO AND DENGUE FEVER IN CHILDREN-A CROSS-SECTIONAL STUDY.
作者： Divya Lakshmi, Ramya Ramanathan, Sundari Subramanian
摘要中文翻译登革热是一种重新出现的由蚊子传播的热带疾病。这项横断面研究是在印度南部一家三级护理中心的儿科进行的,历时两年多。本研究纳入了出现发烧症状并经血清学检测证实患有登革热的0-15岁儿童和青少年。在患病第 3 天采集血液并送去进行全血细胞计数 (CBC)。得出中性粒细胞-淋巴细胞比率(NLR)和血小板-淋巴细胞比率(PLR)。NLR和PLR的敏感性分别为68.4%和96.8%,NLR和PLR的特异性分别为41.7%和66.7%。尽管发现 PLR 具有更高的敏感性和特异性,但发现 NLR 具有更高的阳性预测价值。血小板-淋巴细胞比率在总体准确性、特异性和敏感性方面表现出优越性,被证明是识别 重症登革热 病例的更有效标记。NLR 和 PLR 显示出很强的正相关性(r = 0.83,p <0.001)。这些比率的临床效用已在脓毒症和 COVID-19 等其他疾病中得到了广泛研究,尤其是在成人中,但由于缺乏知识和研究有限,在儿童中尚未得到充分利用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨儿童中性粒细胞-淋巴细胞比值(NLR)和血小板-淋巴细胞比值(PLR)与登革热之间的相关性,因为这些比值已在其他疾病中进行了研究,但在儿科登革热病例中的研究还不够充分。这项研究的动机是需要确定有效的生物标志物以早期检测儿童登革热。
设计与对象
印度南部一家三级护理中心的儿科进行了一项为期两年的横断面研究。该研究纳入了出现发烧症状并经血清学检测证实患有登革热的0-15岁儿童和青少年。
核心方法
在患病第 3 天收集血样并进行全血细胞计数 (CBC) 分析。NLR 和 PLR 根据 CBC 结果计算。该研究使用这些比率来评估它们作为儿童 登革热 生物标志物的潜力。
主要结果
NLR 和 PLR 显示出很强的正相关性(r = 0.83,p < 0.001)。NLR和PLR的敏感性分别为68.4%和96.8%,NLR和PLR的特异性分别为41.7%和66.7%。
解释与创新
研究发现,虽然PLR具有更高的敏感性和特异性,但NLR表现出更高的阳性预测价值。这表明 NLR 在识别儿童 重症登革热 病例方面可能更有用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于开发更有效的生物标志物,用于儿童 重症登革热 的早期检测和风险评估,从而有可能改善临床决策和公共卫生 监测。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。由于依赖血清学确认且缺乏纵向数据,证据强度中等。

CORRELATION BETWEEN NEUTROPHIL LYMPHOCYTE RATIO, PLATELET LYMPHOCYTE RATIO AND DENGUE FEVER IN CHILDREN-A CROSS-SECTIONAL STUDY.

Authors: Divya Lakshmi, Ramya Ramanathan, Sundari Subramanian
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is a re-emerging tropical disease transmitted by mosquitoes. This cross-sectional study was conducted in the Department of Paediatrics of a tertiary care centre in South India over two years. Children and adolescents aged 0-15 years who presented with fever and were confirmed to have dengue by serological tests were included in this study. Blood was collected and sent for complete blood count (CBC) on day 3 of illness. Neutrophil-lymphocyte ratio (NLR) and platelet-lymphocyte ratio (PLR) were derived. The sensitivity of NLR and PLR was 68.4% and 96.8% respectively, while the specificity of NLR and PLR were 41.7% and 66.7% respectively. Although PLR was found to have higher sensitivity and specificity, NLR was found to have an increased positive predictive value. The platelet-lymphocyte ratio demonstrates superiority by overall accuracy, specificity, and sensitivity, proving to be a more effective marker for identifying severe dengue cases. NLR and PLR demonstrated a strong positive correlation (r = 0.83, p <0.001). The clinical utility of these ratios has been extensively studied in other diseases, such as sepsis and COVID-19, especially in adults, but has been underutilized in children due to the lack of knowledge and limited research.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the correlation between neutrophil-lymphocyte ratio (NLR) and platelet-lymphocyte ratio (PLR) with dengue fever in children, as these ratios have been studied in other diseases but not sufficiently in pediatric dengue cases. The research was motivated by the need to identify effective biomarkers for early detection of dengue in children.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted over two years in the Department of Paediatrics at a tertiary care centre in South India. Children and adolescents aged 0-15 years who presented with fever and were confirmed to have dengue by serological tests were included in the study.
Core methods
Blood samples were collected and analyzed for complete blood count (CBC) on day 3 of illness. NLR and PLR were calculated from the CBC results. The study used these ratios to assess their potential as biomarkers for dengue fever in children.
Main results
NLR and PLR demonstrated a strong positive correlation (r = 0.83, p < 0.001). The sensitivity of NLR and PLR was 68.4% and 96.8% respectively, while the specificity of NLR and PLR were 41.7% and 66.7% respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that although PLR had higher sensitivity and specificity, NLR showed an increased positive predictive value. This suggests that NLR may be more useful in identifying severe dengue cases in children.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of more effective biomarkers for early detection and risk assessment of severe dengue in children, potentially improving clinical decision-making and public health surveillance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on serological confirmation and the lack of longitudinal data.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

在随机、开放标签、I 期研究中评估灭活和减毒活四价登革热 疫苗 初免加强策略:对了解保护的免疫相关性的影响。

Evaluation of inactivated and live attenuated, tetravalent dengue vaccine prime-boost strategies in a randomized, open-label, phase I study: implications for understanding immune correlates of protection.
作者： Michael Koren, Simon Pollett, Keisha Akerele, Christine Lee, Kristin Mills, James E. Moon, Paul B. Keiser, Jack Hutter, Melinda Hamer, Justin M Curley
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
需要针对所有四种 登革病毒 (DENV) 类型的登革热 疫苗 产生平衡免疫力,以减轻登革热疾病的全球负担。实现这一目标对于登革热 疫苗 开发人员来说是一个挑战,特别是在对登革热免疫学上未接触过的个体进行免疫接种时。
设计与对象
This study was a randomized, phase I, open-label trial involving 40 healthy adults. The study evaluated a heterologous, tetravalent purified inactivated vaccine (PIV) prime and a tetravalent live attenuated vaccine (LAV) boost regimen. The researchers compared two schedules: 0, 90 day and 0, 180 day.
核心方法
该研究通过定量 RT-PCR 在急性样本中使用病毒 基因组 检测,并使用血清抗体消耗测量暴露后的类型特异性抗体反应。这些方法是已知的保护相关方法。然而,目前测量类型特异性抗体的方法对于在小型临床试验中评估 疫苗 并不理想。
主要结果
两种 PIV/LAV 方案引发与 DENV 1-4 类似、高且平衡的细胞和体液 免疫应答。然而,浓缩的 0 天、90 天时间表与更严重的不良事件相关。在随后的临床试验中,与未接种疫苗的对照组相比,疫苗 没有受到保护,并且经历了更明显的 登革热 样疾病。
解释与创新
结果凸显了在理解登革热 疫苗 介导的保护和疾病风险的机制相关性方面的关键差距。该研究强调需要改进方法来评估 免疫应答 并确定保护的相关性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果强调了了解保护的免疫相关性以及与不同 疫苗接种 方案相关的潜在风险的重要性,为开发更有效的登革热 疫苗 提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量较小以及对特定 免疫应答 测量的依赖可能会限制普遍性。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于单次试验和随后的临床挑战,所使用的测量技术存在一些局限性。

Evaluation of inactivated and live attenuated, tetravalent dengue vaccine prime-boost strategies in a randomized, open-label, phase I study: implications for understanding immune correlates of protection.

Authors: Michael Koren, Simon Pollett, Keisha Akerele, Christine Lee, Kristin Mills, James E. Moon, Paul B. Keiser, Jack Hutter, Melinda Hamer, Justin M Curley
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: Vaccines that elicit balanced, protective immunity to all four dengue virus (DENV) types are needed to reduce the global burden of dengue disease. Achieving this goal has been a challenge for dengue vaccine developers, particularly when immunizing dengue immunologically naïve individuals. In this randomized, phase I, open-label study of 40 healthy adults, we evaluated a heterologous, tetravalent purified inactivated vaccine (PIV) prime and a tetravalent live attenuated vaccine (LAV) boost. We compared a 0, 90 day PIV/LAV prime-boost schedule to a 0, 180 day schedule. These two PIV/LAV regimens elicited similar, high, and balanced cellular and humoral immune responses to DENV 1-4, although the condensed schedule was associated with more severe adverse events. In a subsequent clinical trial [K. Friberg, et al., Lancet Infect Dis 24:896-908, 2024, https://doi.org/10.1016/S1473-3099(24)00100-2], the PIV/LAV vaccinees were challenged with DENV-1 and most were unprotected and experienced more pronounced dengue fever-like illness than unvaccinated controls. These outcomes underscore critical gaps in our understanding of mechanistic correlates of dengue vaccine-mediated protection and disease risk. IMPORTANCE: Dengue vaccine research and development has been complicated by several factors, including an incomplete understanding of immunologic correlates of protection and immune-mediated risk of severe disease. High and balanced immunity elicited by the tetravalent purified inactivated vaccine (PIV)/live attenuated vaccine (LAV) prime-boost vaccine strategy was predicted to provide protection from dengue virus type 1 (DENV-1) challenge in a subsequent clinical study, but vaccinees were unprotected and had an unexpected increase in symptom severity compared to unvaccinated controls. Evidence of exposure to a specific DENV type by viral genome detection (e.g., by quantitative RT-PCR) in acute samples or measurement of post-exposure, type-specific antibody responses (e.g., by serum antibody depletion) are known correlates of protection. However, current methods of measuring type-specific antibody are not ideal for evaluating groups of vaccinees, even in relatively small clinical trials. Newer, high-throughput methods of determining DENV type-specific immune responses and discovery of other correlates of protection are needed to more reliably evaluate dengue vaccine candidates.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue vaccines that elicit balanced immunity against all four dengue virus (DENV) types are needed to reduce the global burden of dengue disease. Achieving this goal has been a challenge for dengue vaccine developers, particularly when immunizing dengue immunologically naïve individuals.
Design and population
This study was a randomized, phase I, open-label trial involving 40 healthy adults. The study evaluated a heterologous, tetravalent purified inactivated vaccine (PIV) prime and a tetravalent live attenuated vaccine (LAV) boost regimen. The researchers compared two schedules: 0, 90 day and 0, 180 day.
Core methods
The study used viral genome detection via quantitative RT-PCR in acute samples and measured post-exposure, type-specific antibody responses using serum antibody depletion. These methods are known correlates of protection. However, current methods for measuring type-specific antibodies are not ideal for evaluating vaccinees in small clinical trials.
Main results
The two PIV/LAV regimens elicited similar, high, and balanced cellular and humoral immune responses to DENV 1-4. However, the condensed 0, 90 day schedule was associated with more severe adverse events. In a subsequent clinical trial, vaccinees were unprotected and experienced more pronounced dengue fever-like illness compared to unvaccinated controls.
Interpretation and novelty
The outcomes highlight critical gaps in understanding the mechanistic correlates of dengue vaccine-mediated protection and disease risk. The study underscores the need for improved methods to evaluate immune responses and identify correlates of protection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of more effective dengue vaccines by emphasizing the importance of understanding immune correlates of protection and the potential risks associated with different vaccination schedules.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's small sample size and reliance on specific immune response measurements may limit generalizability. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on a single trial and subsequent clinical challenges, with some limitations in the measurement techniques used.

泰国甘烹碧登革热 传播 的空间结构。

The spatial structure of dengue transmission in Kamphaeng Phet Thailand.
作者： Sarah H. Ooi, Angkana T. Huang, Surachai Kaewhiran, Aaron Farmer, Darunee Buddhari, Tao Li, Jaykumar Gandhi, Adam R. Pollio, Jun Hang, Matthew A. Conte
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 是一种由蚊子传播的虫媒病毒,对全球人口构成严重威胁。DENV 的四种血清型通常在地方性流行,导致在空间和时间上产生许多离散但重叠的 传播 链,有效隐藏 传播 的潜在模式,以及对当地人群免疫力的长期影响。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,将来自泰国甘烹碧省一家省级医院的个人的地理定位病例数据 (N = 7,487) 与 25 年期间感染他们的病毒序列子集 (N = 302) 结合起来。
核心方法
我们使用血清型和序列数据估计了不同时间和空间窗口内病例对之间的空间依赖性,并使用逻辑回归来确定 宿主 群体规模或流行病规模是否与病毒多样性相关。
主要结果
We found there was spatial dependence between cases at up to 5 km, resulting in localised serotype-specific patterns of immunity in subsequent years. Within a 78.5 km2 area there were 9.6 distinct transmission chains at any time and that each 100,000 increase in population size was associated with 9.8 additional chains. While local viral diversity was also correlated with the size of the epidemic within any month, we found diversity only had moderate predictive power to accurately recover epidemic dynamics.
解释与创新
这些发现强调了具有大量免疫力的高度地方性病原体的微妙且空间可变的生态学。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过强调 监测 的空间模式和人口规模在塑造病毒多样性中的作用,为登革热流行地区的针对性 监测 和干预策略的设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究将血清分型病例数据与 25 年来单一环境的序列数据结合起来,这可能会限制普遍性。由于病毒多样性与流行病规模之间的相关性仅为中等,因此证据强度为中等。

The spatial structure of dengue transmission in Kamphaeng Phet Thailand.

Authors: Sarah H. Ooi, Angkana T. Huang, Surachai Kaewhiran, Aaron Farmer, Darunee Buddhari, Tao Li, Jaykumar Gandhi, Adam R. Pollio, Jun Hang, Matthew A. Conte
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue virus (DENV) is a mosquito transmitted arbovirus that represents a severe threat to global populations. The four serotypes of DENV often circulate endemically resulting in many discrete but overlapping transmission chains in space and time, effectively hiding the underlying patterns of transmission, and longer term consequences on local population immunity. Here we combined serotyped case data with sequence data from a single setting over 25 years to characterise the spatial dependence between cases, the number of distinct transmission chains circulating at any time and whether community transmission was followed by serotype-specific local herd immunity. METHODOLOGY/PRINCIPAL FINDINGS: In this study, we combined geolocated case data (N = 7,487) from individuals that presented at a provincial hospital in Kamphaeng Phet Province, Thailand alongside a subset of the sequences of the viruses (N = 302) that infected them. We estimated the spatial dependence between case pairs over different temporal and spatial windows, using both serotype and sequence data, and used logistic regression to identify whether host population size or epidemic size were correlated with viral diversity. We found there was spatial dependence between cases at up to 5 km, resulting in localised serotype-specific patterns of immunity in subsequent years. Within a 78.5 km2 area there were 9.6 distinct transmission chains at any time and that each 100,000 increase in population size was associated with 9.8 additional chains. While local viral diversity was also correlated with the size of the epidemic within any month, we found diversity only had moderate predictive power to accurately recover epidemic dynamics. CONCLUSIONS/SIGNIFICANCE: These findings highlight the nuanced and spatially variable ecology of a highly endemic pathogen with substantial immunity. This study also illustrates the strengths of combining multiple data sources to shed light on the nature of DENV transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) is a mosquito transmitted arbovirus that represents a severe threat to global populations. The four serotypes of DENV often circulate endemically resulting in many discrete but overlapping transmission chains in space and time, effectively hiding the underlying patterns of transmission, and longer term consequences on local population immunity.
Design and population
In this study, geolocated case data (N = 7,487) from individuals that presented at a provincial hospital in Kamphaeng Phet Province, Thailand were combined with a subset of the sequences of the viruses (N = 302) that infected them over a 25-year period.
Core methods
We estimated the spatial dependence between case pairs over different temporal and spatial windows, using both serotype and sequence data, and used logistic regression to identify whether host population size or epidemic size were correlated with viral diversity.
Main results
We found there was spatial dependence between cases at up to 5 km, resulting in localised serotype-specific patterns of immunity in subsequent years. Within a 78.5 km2 area there were 9.6 distinct transmission chains at any time and that each 100,000 increase in population size was associated with 9.8 additional chains. While local viral diversity was also correlated with the size of the epidemic within any month, we found diversity only had moderate predictive power to accurately recover epidemic dynamics.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings highlight the nuanced and spatially variable ecology of a highly endemic pathogen with substantial immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform the design of targeted surveillance and intervention strategies in dengue-endemic regions by highlighting the spatial patterns of transmission and the role of population size in shaping viral diversity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study combined serotyped case data with sequence data from a single setting over 25 years, which may limit generalisability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the correlation between viral diversity and epidemic size being only moderate.

2005-2024 年泰国登革热年龄分布的流行病学变化

Epidemiological shift in age distribution of Dengue in Thailand, 2005–2024
作者： Kittipong Sornlorm, Roshan Kumar Mahato, Sarayu Muntaphan, Kanit Hnuploy, Rajitra Nawawonganun
摘要中文翻译登革热历来被认为是一种儿童疾病,但年龄分布变化的证据正在增加。我们分析了泰国 20 年(2005 年至 2024 年)的国家登革热 监测 数据,其中包括 1,639,442 例病例,以检查特定年龄 发病率 的时间趋势,使用 发病率 比率 (RIRR) 量化年龄变化幅度,并使用 Kitakawa 分解法分解观察到的变化。25岁以上成人病例比例从19.56%增长近一倍,从19.56%增至36.16%,而儿童比例则从54.49%下降至41.63%。25-44 岁成年人的 RIRR 为 1.78 (95% CI 1.76-1.80),≥45 岁老年人的 RIRR 为 2.19 (95% CI 2.15-2.24)。Kitakawa 分解将 +178.7% 的变化归因于流行病学因素,将 -78.7% 的变化归因于人口变化,支持感染力下降而不是人口老龄化作为主要驱动因素。这些发现需要将登革热预防扩大到学校项目之外,并提高对成人登革热的临床警惕性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热历来被认为是一种儿童疾病,但年龄分布变化的证据正在增加。该研究旨在调查泰国登革热特定年龄 发病率 的时间趋势。
设计与对象
我们分析了泰国 20 年(2005 年至 2024 年)包含 1,639,442 例病例的国家登革热 监测 数据,以检查特定年龄 监测 的时间趋势。
核心方法
该研究使用北川分解来量化年龄变化幅度并分解观察到的变化,将变化归因于流行病学因素和人口变化。
主要结果
25岁以上成人病例比例从19.56%增长近一倍,从19.56%增至36.16%,而儿童比例则从54.49%下降至41.63%。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,观察到的年龄分布变化主要是由流行病学因素驱动的,而不是人口老龄化。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现需要将登革热预防扩大到学校项目之外,并提高对成人登革热的临床警惕性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖国家 监测 数据而受到限制,该数据可能无法涵盖所有​​病例或解释区域差异。证据有力,评估可信度高。

Epidemiological shift in age distribution of Dengue in Thailand, 2005–2024

Authors: Kittipong Sornlorm, Roshan Kumar Mahato, Sarayu Muntaphan, Kanit Hnuploy, Rajitra Nawawonganun
Original abstract (English)
Dengue has historically been characterized as a childhood disease, but evidence of shifting age distribution is increasing. We analyzed 20 years (2005–2024) of national dengue surveillance data from Thailand comprising 1,639,442 cases to examine temporal trends in age-specific incidence, quantify age shift magnitude using the ratio of incidence rate ratios (RIRR), and decompose observed changes using Kitagawa decomposition. The proportion of cases among adults ≥25 years nearly doubled from 19.56% to 36.16%, while the children's proportion declined from 54.49% to 41.63%. RIRR for adults 25–44 years was 1.78 (95% CI 1.76-1.80), and for older adults ≥45 years was 2.19 (95% CI 2.15-2.24). Kitagawa decomposition attributed +178.7% of change to epidemiological factors and -78.7% to demographic shifts, supporting a declining force of infection rather than population aging as the primary driver. These findings necessitate expanding dengue prevention beyond school-based programs and enhancing clinical vigilance for adult dengue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue has historically been characterized as a childhood disease, but evidence of shifting age distribution is increasing. The study aimed to examine temporal trends in age-specific incidence of dengue in Thailand.
Design and population
We analyzed 20 years (2005–2024) of national dengue surveillance data from Thailand comprising 1,639,442 cases to examine temporal trends in age-specific incidence.
Core methods
The study used Kitagawa decomposition to quantify age shift magnitude and decompose observed changes, attributing the change to epidemiological factors and demographic shifts.
Main results
The proportion of cases among adults ≥25 years nearly doubled from 19.56% to 36.16%, while the children's proportion declined from 54.49% to 41.63%.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the observed shift in age distribution is primarily driven by epidemiological factors rather than population aging.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings necessitate expanding dengue prevention beyond school-based programs and enhancing clinical vigilance for adult dengue.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on national surveillance data, which may not capture all cases or account for regional variations. The evidence is strong, with a high confidence assessment.

巴拉圭的蚊子多样性和虫媒病毒检测:野外人群中登革热和基孔肯雅病毒感染的第一个证据。

Mosquito diversity and arbovirus detection in Paraguay: first evidence of dengue and chikungunya virus infection in field populations.
作者： Darian Cáceres Roche, Luis Ferreira, Cynthia Bernal, Lívia Sacchetto, Sofía Echeverria, Patricia Ayala, Adriana Valenzuela, Laura Patricia Mendoza, Ana K Ibarrola-Vannucci, José Montiel
摘要中文翻译虫媒病毒是蚊媒病毒,对公共卫生具有重要意义,特别是在热带和亚热带地区。在巴拉圭,登革热 (DENV) 和基孔肯雅 (CHIKV) 病毒属于流行病学最相关的病毒;然而,整合蚊子和虫媒病毒 监测 的研究仍然很少。本研究旨在表征不同生态环境下的蚊子组合组成,并研究现场收集的蚊子中的虫媒病毒循环。2013 年至 2024 年间,从巴拉圭的城市、农村和大西洋森林环境中总共收集了 4,595 只蚊子(609 个池)。已鉴定出 26 种蚊子,其中致倦库蚊 (55.3%) 数量最多,其次是肩胛伊蚊 (Ochlerotatus) (13.3%)。埃及伊蚊(7.3%)主要与城市地区有关。多样性分析显示,大西洋森林的α多样性和伽玛多样性较高,而农村地区的值较低,而高的β多样性表明不同地点之间存在大量物种更替。通过实时 RT-PCR 进行的分子筛查未发现西尼罗河病毒、圣路易斯脑炎、委内瑞拉马脑炎、马亚罗病毒、黄热病、寨卡病毒或奥罗普切病毒的证据。埃及伊蚊池检测出 DENV(n=3;2021 年和 2023 年)和 CHIKV(n=3;2023 年)呈阳性。系统发育重建证实了DENV-4基因型II(谱系B.1.1)、DENV-2基因型II(F.1.1.2)和CHIKV东/中/南非(ECSA)谱系在巴拉圭的循环,所有这些谱系都与人类暴发相关序列密切相关。这些发现提供了巴拉圭现场采集的蚊子中虫媒病毒感染的第一个分子证据,并强调了综合昆虫学和病毒 监测 的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
蚊子传播的虫媒病毒对热带和亚热带地区的公共卫生构成重大威胁,但巴拉圭缺乏将蚊子多样性与虫媒病毒传播联系起来的综合研究。该研究通过调查巴拉圭不同生态环境中现场采集的蚊子中虫媒病毒的存在情况,解决了知识差距。
设计与对象
2013 年至 2024 年间,从巴拉圭的城市、农村和大西洋森林环境中总共收集了 4,595 只蚊子(609 个池)。已鉴定出 26 种蚊子,其中致倦库蚊数量最多,其次是肩胛伊蚊 (Ochlerotatus) 和埃及伊蚊。
核心方法
通过实时 RT-PCR 进行分子筛查,用于检测蚊子库中的虫媒病毒。进行系统发育重建来表征病毒谱系。
主要结果
在三个埃及伊蚊库(2021 年和 2023 年)中检测到 登革病毒(DENV),在三个库(2023 年)中检测到基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)。系统发育分析鉴定出DENV-4基因型II(谱系B.1.1)、DENV-2基因型II(F.1.1.2)和CHIKV东/中/南非(ECSA)谱系,所有这些谱系都与人类暴发相关序列密切相关。
解释与创新
在现场收集的蚊子中检测到 DENV 和 CHIKV 提供了巴拉圭虫媒病毒传播的第一个分子证据,强调需要综合昆虫学和病毒 监测 来监测新出现和重新出现的虫媒病毒。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持巴拉圭的针对性 媒介 控制和 监测 战略,特别是在埃及伊蚊占主导地位的城市地区,以减轻登革热和基孔肯雅热 传播 风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于现场采集的蚊子和有限的时间采样,可能无法充分体现病毒多样性和 监测 动态,因此需要更广泛的纵向 监测 来加强证据。

Mosquito diversity and arbovirus detection in Paraguay: first evidence of dengue and chikungunya virus infection in field populations.

Authors: Darian Cáceres Roche, Luis Ferreira, Cynthia Bernal, Lívia Sacchetto, Sofía Echeverria, Patricia Ayala, Adriana Valenzuela, Laura Patricia Mendoza, Ana K Ibarrola-Vannucci, José Montiel
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses are mosquito-borne viruses of major public health importance, particularly in tropical and subtropical regions. In Paraguay, dengue (DENV) and chikungunya (CHIKV) viruses are among the most epidemiologically relevant; however, studies integrating mosquito and arbovirus surveillance remain scarce. This study aimed to characterize mosquito assemblage composition across different ecological settings and investigate arbovirus circulation in field-collected mosquitoes. A total of 4,595 mosquitoes (609 pools) were collected between 2013 and 2024 from urban, rural, and Atlantic Forest environments in Paraguay. Twenty-six mosquito species were identified, with Culex quinquefasciatus (55.3%) as the most abundant, followed by Aedes (Ochlerotatus) scapularis (13.3%). Aedes aegypti (7.3%) was mainly associated with urban areas. Diversity analyses showed higher alpha diversity and greater gamma diversity in the Atlantic Forest and lower values in rural areas, whereas high beta diversity indicated substantial species turnover among locations. Molecular screening by real-time RT-PCR detected no evidence of West Nile, Saint Louis encephalitis, Venezuelan equine encephalitis, Mayaro, yellow fever, Zika, or Oropouche viruses. aegypti pools tested positive for DENV (n=3; 2021 and 2023) and CHIKV (n=3; 2023). Phylogenetic reconstruction confirmed the circulation of DENV-4 Genotype II (lineage B.1.1), DENV-2 Genotype II (F.1.1.2), and CHIKV East/Central/South African (ECSA) lineages in Paraguay, all closely related to human outbreak-associated sequences. These findings provide the first molecular evidence of arbovirus infection in field-collected mosquitoes in Paraguay and emphasize the importance of integrated entomological and viral surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Arboviruses transmitted by mosquitoes pose significant public health threats in tropical and subtropical regions, yet Paraguay lacked integrated studies linking mosquito diversity with arbovirus circulation. The study addressed the knowledge gap by investigating arbovirus presence in field-collected mosquitoes across diverse ecological settings in Paraguay.
Design and population
A total of 4,595 mosquitoes (609 pools) were collected between 2013 and 2024 from urban, rural, and Atlantic Forest environments in Paraguay. Twenty-six mosquito species were identified, with Culex quinquefasciatus as the most abundant, followed by Aedes (Ochlerotatus) scapularis and Aedes aegypti.
Core methods
Molecular screening by real-time RT-PCR was used to detect arboviruses in mosquito pools. Phylogenetic reconstruction was performed to characterize viral lineages.
Main results
Dengue virus (DENV) was detected in three Aedes aegypti pools (2021 and 2023), and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) in three pools (2023). Phylogenetic analysis identified DENV-4 Genotype II (lineage B.1.1), DENV-2 Genotype II (F.1.1.2), and CHIKV East/Central/South African (ECSA) lineages, all closely related to human outbreak-associated sequences.
Interpretation and novelty
The detection of DENV and CHIKV in field-collected mosquitoes provides the first molecular evidence of arbovirus circulation in Paraguay, highlighting the need for integrated entomological and viral surveillance to monitor emerging and re-emerging arboviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support targeted vector control and surveillance strategies in Paraguay, particularly in urban areas where Aedes aegypti predominates, to mitigate dengue and chikungunya transmission risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s reliance on field-collected mosquitoes and limited temporal sampling may underrepresent viral diversity and transmission dynamics, warranting broader longitudinal surveillance to strengthen evidence.

马里基纳市每周登革热病例的时间建模:经典、机器学习和深度时间模型的时间尊重比较

Temporal Modeling of Weekly Dengue Cases in Marikina City: A Time-Respecting Comparison of Classical, Machine Learning, and Deep Temporal Models
作者： Kal-el John C. Bautista, Luis Arnold N. Respecio, Cyril Troy C. Vinuya, John Paul Q. Tomas, Karl C. Ablola, Bonifacio T. Doma, Bautista KJC, Respecio LAN, Vinuya CTC, Tomas JPQ
摘要中文翻译<title>Abstract</title> <p>背景 短期登革热预报可以支持当地的准备工作,但模型比较可能会因随机分裂、信息泄漏、有限的基线测试和不充分的过时评估而受到削弱。这项研究开发了一个仅 监测 的框架,用于预测马里基纳市未来两周的每周登革热 监测。方法 每周记录将 2016-2020 年经马里基纳过滤的卫生部 –流行病学 局数据与 2023-2025 年马里基纳 流行病学 监测 单位数据相结合。2021-2022 年被排除在外,因为现有记录分别包含 0 例和 3 例报告病例。将八种经过训练的模型(ARIMA、SARIMA、ETS、决策树、随机森林、梯度提升、LSTM 和时态 CNN)与朴素参考基线和移动平均参考基线进行了比较。目标年份划分使用 2024 年进行内部验证和模型选择,并使用 2025 年作为不合时宜的时间保留。当早期观察到的 2025 年病例数发生在相关预测原点之前时,允许将其作为预测 2025 年后期目标的因果输入。平均绝对误差是主要指标,由均方根误差、确定系数、特征重要性和 发病率 类误差分析支持。结果 清理后的数据集包含 417 个每周观察值和 8,263 个报告病例。尽管两周移动平均值和朴素基线产生的误差略低,但 ARIMA 是 2024 年内部验证期间训练最强的模型。在 2025 年的时间保留中,LSTM 实现了最有利的指标,MAE 为 7.951,RMSE 为 10.651,R2 为 0.698,其次是 ARIMA,MAE 为 8.652,RMSE 为 11.686,R2 为 0.636。两者的 MAE 均低于初始基线。基于树的模型主要依赖于当前的 发病率 和两周滚动摘要。所有评估的方法都倾向于低估 暴发 级别的周数。结论 在泄漏安全时间设计下,仅 监测 预测是可行的。LSTM 产生了最强的 2025 年保留指标,而 ARIMA 仍然是一个可解释且计算效率高的替代方案。操作使用应保留简单的基线,传达不确定性,并优先改进对 暴发 快速升级的检测。</p>
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过比较经典、机器学习和深度时间模型来改进短时程登革热预测,解决现有方法中随机分割、信息泄漏和过时评估不足的局限性。
设计与对象
该研究使用 2016-2020 年和 2023-2025 年的数据开发了一个仅 监测 的框架,用于预测马里基纳市每周登革热 监测,其中 2024 年用于内部验证,2025 年作为过时的时间保留。
核心方法
每周记录将马里基纳过滤后的卫生部 流行病学 局数据与马里基纳 流行病学 监测 单位数据以及八个经过训练的模型(ARIMA、SARIMA、ETS、决策树、随机森林、梯度提升、LSTM 和时间 CNN)与朴素和移动平均参考基线进行比较。
主要结果
清理后的数据集包含 417 个每周观察值和 8,263 个报告病例。2021-2022 年因报告病例数分别为 0 例和 3 例而被排除在外。在 2025 年的时间保留中,LSTM 实现了最有利的指标,MAE 为 7.951,RMSE 为 10.651,R2 为 0.698,其次是 ARIMA,MAE 为 8.652,RMSE 为 11.686,R2 为 0.636。
解释与创新
作者解释说,LSTM 产生了最强的 2025 年保留指标,而 ARIMA 仍然是一种可解释且计算高效的替代方案,强调了在泄漏安全时间设计下仅进行 监测 预测的可行性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,LSTM 和 ARIMA 模型可以改进登革热预测,通过更准确地预测每周 发病率 和更好地检测 暴发 快速升级来支持当地的准备工作。
局限与证据强度
由于病例数较少以及 暴发 级别周的潜在预测不足,该研究的证据被排除在外,因此排除了 2021-2022 年的数据,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Temporal Modeling of Weekly Dengue Cases in Marikina City: A Time-Respecting Comparison of Classical, Machine Learning, and Deep Temporal Models

Authors: Kal-el John C. Bautista, Luis Arnold N. Respecio, Cyril Troy C. Vinuya, John Paul Q. Tomas, Karl C. Ablola, Bonifacio T. Doma, Bautista KJC, Respecio LAN, Vinuya CTC, Tomas JPQ
Original abstract (English)
<title>Abstract</title> <p>Background Short-horizon dengue forecasting can support local preparedness, but model comparisons may be weakened by random splitting, information leakage, limited baseline testing, and insufficient out-of-time evaluation. This study developed a surveillance-only framework for forecasting weekly dengue incidence in Marikina City two weeks ahead. Methods Weekly records combined Marikina-filtered Department of Health–Epidemiology Bureau data from 2016–2020 with Marikina Epidemiology Surveillance Unit data from 2023–2025. The years 2021–2022 were excluded because the available records contained zero and three reported cases, respectively. Eight trained models—ARIMA, SARIMA, ETS, Decision Tree, Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, LSTM, and Temporal CNN—were compared with naïve and moving-average reference baselines. Target-year splits used 2024 for internal validation and model selection and 2025 as an out-of-time temporal holdout. Earlier observed 2025 case counts were permitted as causal inputs for forecasting later 2025 targets when they occurred before the relevant forecast origin. Mean absolute error was the primary metric, supported by root mean square error, coefficient of determination, feature importance, and incidence-class error analysis. Results The cleaned dataset contained 417 weekly observations and 8,263 reported cases. ARIMA was the strongest trained model during 2024 internal validation, although the two-week moving average and naïve baselines produced slightly lower errors. In the 2025 temporal holdout, LSTM achieved the most favorable metrics, with an MAE of 7.951, RMSE of 10.651, and R2 of 0.698, followed by ARIMA with an MAE of 8.652, RMSE of 11.686, and R2 of 0.636. Both achieved lower MAE than the naïve baseline. Tree-based models relied primarily on current incidence and two-week rolling summaries. All evaluated methods tended to underpredict outbreak-level weeks. Conclusions Surveillance-only forecasting was feasible under a leakage-safe temporal design. LSTM produced the strongest 2025 holdout metrics, while ARIMA remained an interpretable and computationally efficient alternative. Operational use should retain simple baselines, communicate uncertainty, and prioritize improved detection of rapid outbreak escalation.</p>
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to improve short-horizon dengue forecasting by comparing classical, machine learning, and deep temporal models, addressing the limitations of random splitting, information leakage, and insufficient out-of-time evaluation in existing methods.
Design and population
The study developed a surveillance-only framework for forecasting weekly dengue incidence in Marikina City using data from 2016–2020 and 2023–2025, with 2024 used for internal validation and 2025 as an out-of-time temporal holdout.
Core methods
Weekly records combined Marikina-filtered Department of Health–Epidemiology Bureau data with Marikina Epidemiology Surveillance Unit data, and eight trained models—ARIMA, SARIMA, ETS, Decision Tree, Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, LSTM, and Temporal CNN—were compared with naïve and moving-average reference baselines.
Main results
The cleaned dataset contained 417 weekly observations and 8,263 reported cases. The years 2021–2022 were excluded due to zero and three reported cases, respectively. In the 2025 temporal holdout, LSTM achieved the most favorable metrics, with an MAE of 7.951, RMSE of 10.651, and R2 of 0.698, followed by ARIMA with an MAE of 8.652, RMSE of 11.686, and R2 of 0.636.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that LSTM produced the strongest 2025 holdout metrics, while ARIMA remained an interpretable and computationally efficient alternative, highlighting the feasibility of surveillance-only forecasting under a leakage-safe temporal design.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that LSTM and ARIMA models can improve dengue forecasting, supporting local preparedness by enabling more accurate predictions of weekly incidence and better detection of rapid outbreak escalation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by the exclusion of 2021–2022 data due to low case counts and the potential for underprediction of outbreak-level weeks, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

登革热 在免疫功能低下中的应用:留尼汪岛的 队列研究 回顾性研究

Dengue fever in the immunocompromised: a retrospective cohort study on Reunion Island
作者： Maud Lekieffre, Loïc Raffray, Quentin Cabrera, Catherine Mohr, Aurélie Foucher, Cécile Saint-Pastou-terrier, Yatrika Koumar, Patrick Gerardin, Olivier Maillard, Antoine Bertolotti
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 登革热 (DF) 是世界范围内最流行的虫媒病毒相关疾病。我们的研究旨在描述留尼汪岛免疫功能低下患者的 DF,并将其与非免疫功能低下患者的数据进行比较。方法 在一项回顾性 队列研究 研究中,将 2019 年留尼汪大学医院收治的所有逆转录酶链反应 (rt-PCR) 阳性患者纳入确诊为 DF 的患者,我们确定了免疫功能低下患者,以将其社会人口统计学、临床和生物学特征与非免疫功能低下患者进行比较。结果 847 例确诊 DF 的患者中,41 例免疫功能低下。他们的年龄明显更大 (p=0,001),查尔森评分也明显更高 (p=0,001)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 (DF) 是世界范围内最流行的虫媒病毒相关疾病。我们的研究旨在描述留尼汪岛免疫功能低下患者的 DF,并将其与非免疫功能低下患者的数据进行比较。
设计与对象
在一项回顾性 队列研究 研究中,纳入了 2019 年留尼汪大学医院收治的所有确诊 DF 的患者。该研究确定了免疫功能低下的患者,并将他们的社会人口统计学、临床和生物学特征与非免疫功能低下的患者进行了比较。
核心方法
该研究采用回顾性 队列研究 设计,通过逆转录酶链反应 (rt-PCR) 检测招募确诊的 DF 病例。Immunocompromised patients were identified and compared with non-immunocompromised patients based on socio-demographic,clinical and biological characteristics.
主要结果
在 847 名确诊 DF 的患者中,有 41 名免疫功能低下。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了免疫功能低下患者中 DF 的独特临床特征,这可能为有针对性的管理策略提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为免疫功能低下人群的 监测 和 DF 管理策略提供信息,从而有可能改善临床结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制,但由于方法明确以及对免疫功能低下和非免疫功能低下患者的直接比较,证据充足。

Dengue fever in the immunocompromised: a retrospective cohort study on Reunion Island

Authors: Maud Lekieffre, Loïc Raffray, Quentin Cabrera, Catherine Mohr, Aurélie Foucher, Cécile Saint-Pastou-terrier, Yatrika Koumar, Patrick Gerardin, Olivier Maillard, Antoine Bertolotti
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Dengue fever (DF) is the most worldwide prevalent arbovirus associated diseases. Our study aimed to describe DF in immunocompromised patients on Reunion island and to compare it with data from non-immunocompromised patients. Methods In a retrospective cohort study that enrolled as confirmed DF all patients with positive reverse transcriptase chain reaction (rt-PCR) admitted to the University Hospital of Reunion in 2019, we identified immunocompromised patients to compare their socio-demographic, clinical and biological characteristics to non-immunocompromised. Results From the 847 patients with confirmed DF, 41 were immunocompromised. They were significantly older (p=0,001), presented significantly higher Charlson score (p
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever (DF) is the most worldwide prevalent arbovirus associated diseases. Our study aimed to describe DF in immunocompromised patients on Reunion island and to compare it with data from non-immunocompromised patients.
Design and population
In a retrospective cohort study, all patients with confirmed DF admitted to the University Hospital of Reunion in 2019 were enrolled. The study identified immunocompromised patients and compared their socio-demographic, clinical and biological characteristics to non-immunocompromised patients.
Core methods
The study used a retrospective cohort design, enrolling confirmed DF cases through reverse transcriptase chain reaction (rt-PCR) testing. Immunocompromised patients were identified and compared with non-immunocompromised patients based on socio-demographic, clinical and biological characteristics.
Main results
From the 847 patients with confirmed DF, 41 were immunocompromised.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the distinct clinical profile of DF in immunocompromised patients, which may inform targeted management strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and management strategies for DF in immunocompromised populations, potentially improving clinical outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias, but the evidence is strong due to the clear methodology and direct comparison of immunocompromised and non-immunocompromised patients.

印度的登革热 媒介 动态:使用基于机器学习的最大熵模型在 SSP 场景下当前和未来的多模型适用性

Dengue Vector Dynamics in India: Present and Future Multi-Model Suitability under SSP Scenarios Using a Machine Learning-Based Maximum Entropy Model
作者： Yogeshwar Singh Rathore, Martand Mani Mishra, Anil Kumar, Ankita Chakraborty, Anisha Choudhury, Sonal Kumari, Netrananda Sahu
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热 近年来在印度成为一种令人担忧的气候敏感疾病。本研究使用物种分布模型 (MaxEnt) 检查登革热 传播 当前和未来的气候适宜性。使用 CHELSA v2.1 数据集的高分辨率生物气候数据(约 1 公里分辨率)和来自不同来源的埃及伊蚊的地理参考发生数据。通过空间细化、多重共线性消除和采样偏差校正来特别关注数据质量,以提高模型的稳健性。未来预测是使用三个共享社会经济路径 (SSP)(SSP126、SSP370、SSP585)下的五个一般循环模型(GFDL-ESM4、IPSL-CM6A-LR、MPI-ESM1-2-HR、MRI-ESM2-0、UKESM1-0-LL)生成的,涵盖近(2011-2040)、中期(2041-2070)和遥远的未来(2071-2100)时期。该模型显示出中等的预测性能(AUC = 0.79,TSS = 0.53),而高博伊斯指数(0.9)表明在识别合适的栖息地方面具有很强的生态一致性。结果清楚地表明,印度一半以上的气候适合登革热 传播。西高止山脉、东部沿海地区和东北部各州的适宜性较高。未来的预测表明,变化可能是通过空间重新分配而不是均匀扩张而发生的,其中包括逐渐北移和高排放情景下适宜性的局部降低。与报告的登革热 发病率 的比较进一步表明,即使在被归类为中等适宜性的地区,也存在大量的 传播,这表明仅较高的适宜性并不能完全决定疾病负担。总体而言,该研究强调印度的登革热风险不仅是未来的担忧,而且是持续的挑战。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
近年来,登革热 在印度已成为一种令人担忧的气候敏感疾病。该研究旨在评估当前和未来不同社会经济情景下登革热 传播 的多模型适用性。
设计与对象
本研究使用物种分布建模 (MaxEnt) 方法检查登革热 传播 当前和未来的气候适宜性。使用 CHELSA v2.1 数据集的高分辨率生物气候数据(约 1 公里分辨率)和来自不同来源的埃及伊蚊的地理参考发生数据。
核心方法
该研究使用基于机器学习的最大熵模型,根据五个大气循环模型(GFDL-ESM4、IPSL-CM6A-LR、MPI-ESM1-2-HR、MRI-ESM2-0、UKESM1-0-LL)和三个共享社会经济路径(SSP126、SSP370、SSP585)生成近(2011-2040)中期的未来预测。(2041-2070)和遥远的未来(2071-2100)时期。
主要结果
结果显示,印度一半以上的地区气候适合登革热传播。西高止山脉、东部沿海地区和东北部各州的适宜性较高。未来的预测表明,变化是通过空间重新分配而不是统一扩张来实现的,包括逐渐北移和高排放情景下适宜性的局部降低。
解释与创新
该模型显示出中等的预测性能(AUC = 0.79,TSS = 0.53),而高博伊斯指数(0.9)表明在识别合适的栖息地方面具有很强的生态一致性。与报告的登革热 发病率 的比较进一步表明,即使在被归类为中等适宜性的地区,也存在大量的 传播,这表明仅较高的适宜性并不能完全决定疾病负担。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,印度的登革热风险不仅是未来的担忧,也是持续的挑战。该研究提供了对登革热 监测 空间动态的见解,可以为有针对性的 监测 和干预策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,该模型的中等预测性能(AUC = 0.79,TSS = 0.53)表明其准确性存在一些局限性。结果基于气候数据和发生记录,可能无法完全捕获影响登革热 传播 的所有因素。

Dengue Vector Dynamics in India: Present and Future Multi-Model Suitability under SSP Scenarios Using a Machine Learning-Based Maximum Entropy Model

Authors: Yogeshwar Singh Rathore, Martand Mani Mishra, Anil Kumar, Ankita Chakraborty, Anisha Choudhury, Sonal Kumari, Netrananda Sahu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue Fever has emerged as an alarming climate-sensitive disease in India in recent time. This study examines the present and future climatic suitability of dengue transmission using a species distribution modelling (MaxEnt). High-resolution bioclimatic data of CHELSA v2.1 dataset (~ 1 km resolution) and georeferenced occurrence data of Aedes aegypti from various sources are used. Particular attention is given to data quality through spatial thinning, multicollinearity removal, and correction for sampling bias to improve model robustness. Future projections are generated using five General Circulation Models (GFDL-ESM4, IPSL-CM6A-LR, MPI-ESM1-2-HR, MRI-ESM2-0, UKESM1-0-LL) under three Shared Socioeconomic Pathways (SSP) (SSP126, SSP370, SSP585) across near (2011–2040), mid (2041–2070), and far-future (2071–2100) periods. The model has shown moderate predictive performance (AUC = 0.79, TSS = 0.53), while high Boyce Index (0.9) which is indicating strong ecological consistency in identifying suitable habitats. The results are clearly showing that more than half of India is climatically suitable for dengue transmission. Western Ghats, eastern coastal regions, and the northeastern states have higher suitability. Future projections are showing that changes are likely to occur through spatial redistribution rather than uniform expansion, which includes a gradual northward shift and localized reductions in suitability under high-emission scenarios. A comparison with reported dengue incidence further shows that substantial transmission is occuring even in areas categorised as moderately suitable, which shows that the higher suitability alone does not fully determine disease burden. Overall, the study highlights that dengue risk in India is not only a future concern but an ongoing challenge.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue Fever has emerged as an alarming climate-sensitive disease in India in recent time. The study aims to assess the present and future multi-model suitability for dengue transmission under different socioeconomic scenarios.
Design and population
This study examines the present and future climatic suitability of dengue transmission using a species distribution modelling (MaxEnt) approach. High-resolution bioclimatic data of CHELSA v2.1 dataset (~ 1 km resolution) and georeferenced occurrence data of Aedes aegypti from various sources are used.
Core methods
The study uses a machine learning-based maximum entropy model to generate future projections under five General Circulation Models (GFDL-ESM4, IPSL-CM6A-LR, MPI-ESM1-2-HR, MRI-ESM2-0, UKESM1-0-LL) and three Shared Socioeconomic Pathways (SSP126, SSP370, SSP585) across near (2011–2040), mid (2041–2070), and far-future (2071–2100) periods.
Main results
The results show that more than half of India is climatically suitable for dengue transmission. Western Ghats, eastern coastal regions, and the northeastern states have higher suitability. Future projections indicate changes through spatial redistribution rather than uniform expansion, including a gradual northward shift and localized reductions in suitability under high-emission scenarios.
Interpretation and novelty
The model has shown moderate predictive performance (AUC = 0.79, TSS = 0.53), while the high Boyce Index (0.9) indicates strong ecological consistency in identifying suitable habitats. A comparison with reported dengue incidence further shows that substantial transmission is occurring even in areas categorised as moderately suitable, which suggests that higher suitability alone does not fully determine disease burden.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight that dengue risk in India is not only a future concern but an ongoing challenge. The study provides insights into the spatial dynamics of dengue transmission, which can inform targeted surveillance and intervention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, and the model's moderate predictive performance (AUC = 0.79, TSS = 0.53) suggests some limitations in its accuracy. The results are based on climatic data and occurrence records, which may not fully capture all factors influencing dengue transmission.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

来自巴基斯坦的 DENV-1 分离物的分子表征揭示了针对 CprM 区域的植物化学物质的免疫原性变异和 抗病毒药物 潜力。

Molecular Characterization of DENV-1 Isolates From Pakistan Reveals Immunogenic Variability and Antiviral Potential of Phytochemicals Targeting the CprM Region.
作者： Sajid Ali Shah, Syed Ahsan Shahid, Sidra Rahman, Muhammad Ali
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 在全球造成大量发病,但有效的 抗病毒药物 仍然缺乏,特别是在多种血清型共同传播的巴基斯坦等流行地区。该研究解决了有关循环 DENV-1 分离株的分子特征及其对免疫和 抗病毒药物 发育的潜在影响的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究筛查了来自巴基斯坦白沙瓦的 45 名患者样本,在 20-40 岁的个体中观察到感染率最高(49%)。它重点关注 DENV-1 分离株,检查血清分型、CprM 连接区域的遗传变异性以及植物化学物质的 抗病毒药物 潜力。
核心方法
进行系统发育分析以评估 DENV-1 分离株的遗传变异性和区域 进化。针对 抗病毒药物 潜力筛选了 60 种植物化学物质的库,并使用对接分数和 ADMET 分析评估了与 CprM 区域的结合亲和力。
主要结果
系统发育分析发现了 6 个 DENV-1 分离株与来自中国、印度、孟加拉国和巴基斯坦的区域毒株紧密聚集。在 CprM 区域检测到 6 个氨基酸取代(Q47H、G48E、P49H、K51E/N、L52M、M57I)。九种植物化学物质对 CprM 区域表现出高结合亲和力(对接分数 < -100),其中水飞蓟宁表现出最强的相互作用(-159.81)。
解释与创新
在 CprM 区域观察到的氨基酸取代可能会影响病毒结构、免疫逃避和抗原特性,突出了 抗病毒药物 设计的潜在目标。水飞蓟宁作为 CprM 区域高亲和力结合剂的鉴定代表了 DENV-1 抗病毒药物 开发的新计算线索。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现通过表征巴基斯坦循环的 DENV-1 变体为 监测 策略提供信息,并支持探索植物源化合物作为 抗病毒药物 疗法的候选药物,有待体外验证。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于计算对接和计算机 ADMET 分析,而没有体外或体内验证,这限制了 抗病毒药物 功效的证据强度。小样本量(n = 45)和关注单一区域可能会限制系统发育和替代研究结果的普遍性。

Molecular Characterization of DENV-1 Isolates From Pakistan Reveals Immunogenic Variability and Antiviral Potential of Phytochemicals Targeting the CprM Region.

Authors: Sajid Ali Shah, Syed Ahsan Shahid, Sidra Rahman, Muhammad Ali
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV), a mosquito-borne arbovirus, remains a critical global health concern, causing approximately 390 million infections annually. Dengue fever poses significant challenges to healthcare systems in endemic regions like Pakistan due to the presence of four established serotypes (DENV-1-DENV-4) and the potential emergence of a fifth serotype (DENV-5). The current study aimed to investigate molecular characteristics of DENV-1 isolates from Peshawar, Pakistan, focusing on serotyping, genetic variability in the CprM junction region, and identification of potential antiviral phytochemicals. A total of 45 patient samples were screened, with the highest infection rate (49%) observed among individuals aged 20-40 years. Phylogenetic analysis revealed that six isolates clustered within the DENV-1 clade and grouped closely related to isolates from China, India, Bangladesh, and Pakistan, suggesting a regional evolution and potentially cross-border transmission. The prominent amino acid substitutions were Q47H, G48E, P49H, K51 E/N, L52 M, and M57I, which may influence viral structure, immune evasion, and antigenic properties. To address the lack of effective antivirals, a library of 60 phytochemicals was screened. Nine compounds demonstrated favorable ADMET profiles and high binding affinity (docking scores < -100) with the CprM region. Silydianin exhibited the strongest interaction (-159.81), indicating promising inhibitory potential. This integrative molecular and computational approach enhances our understanding of circulating DENV-1 strains and highlights plant-derived compounds as candidates for future antiviral development, pending in vitro validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) causes substantial global morbidity, yet effective antivirals remain lacking, particularly in endemic regions such as Pakistan where multiple serotypes co-circulate. The study addressed the knowledge gap regarding molecular characteristics of circulating DENV-1 isolates and their potential impact on immunity and antiviral development.
Design and population
The study screened 45 patient samples from Peshawar, Pakistan, with the highest infection rate (49%) observed among individuals aged 20–40 years. It focused on DENV-1 isolates, examining serotyping, genetic variability in the CprM junction region, and antiviral potential of phytochemicals.
Core methods
Phylogenetic analysis was performed to assess genetic variability and regional evolution of DENV-1 isolates. A library of 60 phytochemicals was screened for antiviral potential, with binding affinity to the CprM region evaluated using docking scores and ADMET profiling.
Main results
Phylogenetic analysis identified six DENV-1 isolates clustering closely with regional strains from China, India, Bangladesh, and Pakistan. Six amino acid substitutions (Q47H, G48E, P49H, K51E/N, L52M, M57I) were detected in the CprM region. Nine phytochemicals showed high binding affinity (docking scores < -100) to the CprM region, with silydianin exhibiting the strongest interaction (-159.81).
Interpretation and novelty
The observed amino acid substitutions in the CprM region may influence viral structure, immune evasion, and antigenic properties, highlighting potential targets for antiviral design. The identification of silydianin as a high-affinity binder to the CprM region represents a novel computational lead for DENV-1 antiviral development.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform surveillance strategies by characterizing circulating DENV-1 variants in Pakistan and support the exploration of plant-derived compounds as candidates for antiviral therapy, pending in vitro validation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on computational docking and in silico ADMET profiling without in vitro or in vivo validation, which constrains the strength of evidence for antiviral efficacy. The small sample size (n=45) and focus on a single region may limit generalizability of phylogenetic and substitution findings.
