哥伦比亚安蒂奥基亚省乌拉巴分区患者肿瘤坏死因子α (TNFα) 基因启动子-308 G/A 多态性的分子检测及其与 重症登革热 发展的关系

Molecular Detection of the -308 G/A Polymorphism in the Tumor Necrosis Factor Alpha (TNFα) Gene Promoter and Its Relationship with the Development of Severe Dengue in Patients from Urabá Subregion, Antioquia-Colombia
作者： Jorge Emilio Salazar Flórez, Ronald Guillermo Peláez Sánchez, Luz Stella Giraldo Cardona, Leci Camila Ariza Salas, Juliana María Martínez Garro, Marco Torres-Castro, Fernando P. Monroy, Luis Ernesto López-Rojas, Katerine Marín Velasquez, Berta Nelly Restrepo Jaramillo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 是由 登革病毒 (DENV) 引起的病毒感染,登革病毒 是黄病毒科的正链 RNA 病毒。人类感染可引起三种与严重程度相关的临床形式:无警告信号的登革热、有警告信号的登革热和 重症登革热。
设计与对象
本研究探讨了 TNF-α 启动子基因多态性与登革热严重程度之间的联系。我们重新招募了 92 例实验室确诊的登革热病例,其中 30 例没有警告标志的登革热病例、43 例有警告标志的登革热病例和 19 例 重症登革热。
核心方法
通过 PCR(聚合酶链式反应)扩增、Sanger 测序和 TNF-α 基因启动子 突变 的生物信息学分析完成基因分型。逻辑回归评估了 -308 G/A TNF-α 突变 与登革热严重程度之间的关联。
主要结果
在10.5%的重症病例中检测到突变基因型-308 G/A TNF-α。与有警告标志的患者相比,严重病例拥有这种 突变 的可能性是其两倍,但没有发现统计学上的显着差异。基因型频率与 Hardy-Weinberg 平衡一致 (p = 0.112)。
解释与创新
虽然没有检测到统计学上显着的关联,但 TNF-α -308G/A 多态性显示出对 重症登革热 的易感性增加的趋势,严重病例中的频率高于有或没有警告迹象的患者。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,TNF-α -308G/A 多态性可能是预测 重症登革热 的潜在生物标志物,这可以为未来对该疾病的免疫发病机制及其临床管理的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
估计的事后统计功效为 11.3%,表明证据强度有限。该研究可能缺乏足够的样本量来检测显着的关联,因此应谨慎解释结果。

Molecular Detection of the -308 G/A Polymorphism in the Tumor Necrosis Factor Alpha (TNFα) Gene Promoter and Its Relationship with the Development of Severe Dengue in Patients from Urabá Subregion, Antioquia-Colombia

Authors: Jorge Emilio Salazar Flórez, Ronald Guillermo Peláez Sánchez, Luz Stella Giraldo Cardona, Leci Camila Ariza Salas, Juliana María Martínez Garro, Marco Torres-Castro, Fernando P. Monroy, Luis Ernesto López-Rojas, Katerine Marín Velasquez, Berta Nelly Restrepo Jaramillo
Original abstract (English)
Background: dengue fever is a viral infection caused by the Dengue Virus (DENV), a positive-sense RNA virus belonging to Flaviviridae family. The virus is transmitted to humans through the bite of the Aedes aegypti mosquito. Worldwide, between 100 and 400 million cases are reported annually. The infection in humans can cause three clinical forms which are related to severity: dengue without warnings signs, dengue with warnings signs, and severe dengue. Mutations in some genes that code for immune system proteins have been linked to the appearance of severe clinical forms of the disease, including TNF-α. The TNF-α plays a key role in the immunopathogenesis of the disease: mutations in this gene can increase the production of TNF-α, favoring cytokine storms and vascular damage, which is associated with severe clinical forms of the disease. Therefore, it is important to study the immunopathogenesis of the disease in the human to try to predict and control the onset of severe clinical forms and fatal cases. Methods: This study explores the link between TNF-α promoter gene polymorphism and dengue severity. We re-cruited 92 laboratory-confirmed dengue cases, including 30 dengue without warnings signs, 43 dengue with warnings signs, and 19 severe dengue. Genotyping was done using amplification by PCR (polymerase chain reaction), Sanger sequencing, and bio-informatics analysis of the TNF-α gene promoter mutations. A logistic regression assessed the association between -308 G/A TNF-α mutation and dengue severity. Results: the mutated genotype -308 G/A TNF-α was detected in 10.5% of severe cases. Interestingly, Severe cases showed higher odds of carrying the mutation than those without warnings signs. Severe cases were twice as likely to possess this mutation compared to those with warnings signs, however, no statistically significant differences were found. Genotype frequencies were consistent with Hardy-Weinberg equilibrium (p = 0.112). The estimated post hoc statistical power was 11.3%. Conclusions: Although no statistically significant association was detected, the TNF-α -308G/A polymorphism showed a trend toward increased susceptibility to severe dengue, with a higher frequency among severe cases than among patients with or without warnings signs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is a viral infection caused by the Dengue Virus (DENV), a positive-sense RNA virus belonging to Flaviviridae family. The infection in humans can cause three clinical forms which are related to severity: dengue without warnings signs, dengue with warnings signs, and severe dengue.
Design and population
This study explores the link between TNF-α promoter gene polymorphism and dengue severity. We re-cruited 92 laboratory-confirmed dengue cases, including 30 dengue without warnings signs, 43 dengue with warnings signs, and 19 severe dengue.
Core methods
Genotyping was done using amplification by PCR (polymerase chain reaction), Sanger sequencing, and bio-informatics analysis of the TNF-α gene promoter mutations. A logistic regression assessed the association between -308 G/A TNF-α mutation and dengue severity.
Main results
The mutated genotype -308 G/A TNF-α was detected in 10.5% of severe cases. Severe cases were twice as likely to possess this mutation compared to those with warnings signs, however, no statistically significant differences were found. Genotype frequencies were consistent with Hardy-Weinberg equilibrium (p = 0.112).
Interpretation and novelty
Although no statistically significant association was detected, the TNF-α -308G/A polymorphism showed a trend toward increased susceptibility to severe dengue, with a higher frequency among severe cases than among patients with or without warnings signs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that TNF-α -308G/A polymorphism may be a potential biomarker for predicting severe dengue, which could inform future studies on the immunopathogenesis of the disease and its clinical management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The estimated post hoc statistical power was 11.3%, indicating limited evidence strength. The study may lack sufficient sample size to detect significant associations, and the results should be interpreted cautiously.

登革病毒 rNS3pro 免疫与 BALB/c 小鼠中调节性 T 细胞表型的扩展相关。

Dengue virus rNS3pro immunization is associated with the expansion of regulatory-like T-cell phenotypes in BALB/c mice.
作者： Víctor Adolfo Romero-Cruz, Angel Ramos-Ligonio, Diana Lizzet Murrieta-León, Denisse Placier-Sosa, Aracely López-Monteon
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 非结构蛋白 3 (NS3) 对于病毒复制和 宿主 免疫逃避至关重要。虽然其蛋白酶结构域 (NS3pro) 与 T 细胞无反应性有关,但其在诱导调节性 T 细胞 (Treg) 中的作用还需要进一步探索。分析用来自四种 DENV 血清型的重组 rNS3pro 蛋白免疫的 BALB/c 小鼠中不同调节性 T 细胞群的诱导。用rNS3pro免疫小鼠,并用同源蛋白在体外刺激脾细胞。通过 RT-PCR、ELISA 和流式细胞术分析细胞因子表达和 T 细胞表型(包括激活、耗竭和细胞毒性标记)。rNS3pro 免疫诱导了主要与调节相关的反应,其特征是显着的 IL-10 表达。观察到 CD4+CD25+ T 细胞显着扩增(对照组为 23.5%,对照组为 1.05%),其中包括 CD25+FoxP3+ 调节性 T 细胞,以及表达 CD25+IL-10+ 和 CD25+TGF-β+ 的 FoxP3 独立亚群。这些细胞共表达细胞毒性相关(FoxP3+穿孔素+/GzB+)和共抑制(CTLA-4+、PD-1+)标记物。效应CD8+T细胞增殖被显着抑制,且不能被外源IL-2恢复。总之,DENV rNS3pro 免疫驱动了多方面的类调节性 T 细胞表型的强劲扩展。这种免疫调节反应与 CD8+ 效应子增殖受损相关,从而确定 NS3 蛋白酶结构域是 DENV 免疫逃避策略中的潜在参与者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨 登革病毒 (DENV) 非结构蛋白 3 蛋白酶 (NS3pro) 在诱导类调节性 T 细胞反应中的作用,因为其在免疫逃避和 T 细胞无反应性中的功能尚不完全清楚。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用四种 DENV 血清型的重组 rNS3pro 蛋白对 BALB/c 小鼠进行免疫,并分析在体外用同源蛋白刺激的脾细胞中产生的 T 细胞表型。
核心方法
使用 RT-PCR、ELISA 和流式细胞术评估细胞因子表达和 T 细胞表型,重点关注激活、耗竭、细胞毒性和调节功能的标记。
主要结果
rNS3pro 免疫导致 CD4+CD25+ T 细胞显着扩增,观察到 23.5%,而对照组为 1.05%。这包括 CD25+FoxP3+ 调节样 T 细胞和表达 CD25+IL-10+ 和 CD25+TGF-β+ 的 FoxP3 独立亚群。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,DENV rNS3pro 免疫诱导了类似调节的 T 细胞反应,这可能通过抑制效应 CD8+ T 细胞增殖来促进免疫逃避。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 DENV NS3pro 免疫调节机制的深入了解,这可以为 疫苗 的开发和增强 免疫应答 抗登革热的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 BALB/c 小鼠的体外分析,并且证据基于单个实验,这可能会影响普遍性。证据强度中等。

Dengue virus rNS3pro immunization is associated with the expansion of regulatory-like T-cell phenotypes in BALB/c mice.

Authors: Víctor Adolfo Romero-Cruz, Angel Ramos-Ligonio, Diana Lizzet Murrieta-León, Denisse Placier-Sosa, Aracely López-Monteon
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) nonstructural protein 3 (NS3) is essential for viral replication and host immune evasion. While its protease domain (NS3pro) has been linked to T-cell anergy, its role in inducing regulatory T cells (Tregs) requires further exploration. To analyze the induction of diverse regulatory T-cell populations in BALB/c mice immunized with recombinant rNS3pro proteins from the four DENV serotypes. Mice were immunized with rNS3pro, and splenocytes were stimulated in vitro with homologous proteins. Cytokine expression and T-cell phenotypes-including markers for activation, exhaustion, and cytotoxicity-were analyzed via RT-PCR, ELISA, and flow cytometry. rNS3pro immunization induced a predominantly regulatory-associated response characterized by significant IL-10 expression. A marked expansion of CD4+CD25+ T cells (23.5% vs. 1.05% in controls) was observed, encompassing CD25+FoxP3+ regulatory-like T cells, alongside FoxP3-independent subsets expressing CD25+IL-10+ and CD25+TGF-β+. These cells co-expressed cytotoxicity-associated (FoxP3+Perforin+/GzB+) and co-inhibitory (CTLA-4+, PD-1+) markers. Effector CD8+ T-cell proliferation was significantly inhibited and could not be restored by exogenous IL-2. In conclusion, DENV rNS3pro immunization drives a robust expansion of multifaceted regulatory-like T-cell phenotypes. This immunomodulatory response correlates with compromised CD8+ effector proliferation, identifying the NS3 protease domain as a potential player in DENV immune evasion strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the role of Dengue virus (DENV) nonstructural protein 3 protease (NS3pro) in inducing regulatory-like T-cell responses, as its function in immune evasion and T-cell anergy is not fully understood.
Design and population
The study involved immunizing BALB/c mice with recombinant rNS3pro proteins from the four DENV serotypes and analyzing the resulting T-cell phenotypes in splenocytes stimulated in vitro with homologous proteins.
Core methods
Cytokine expression and T-cell phenotypes were assessed using RT-PCR, ELISA, and flow cytometry, focusing on markers of activation, exhaustion, cytotoxicity, and regulatory function.
Main results
rNS3pro immunization led to a significant expansion of CD4+CD25+ T cells, with 23.5% observed compared to 1.05% in controls. This included CD25+FoxP3+ regulatory-like T cells and FoxP3-independent subsets expressing CD25+IL-10+ and CD25+TGF-β+.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that DENV rNS3pro immunization induces a regulatory-like T-cell response, which may contribute to immune evasion by suppressing effector CD8+ T-cell proliferation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the immunomodulatory mechanisms of DENV NS3pro, which could inform vaccine development and strategies to enhance immune responses against dengue.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro analysis in BALB/c mice, and the evidence is based on a single experiment, which may affect generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate.

患病率 登革热患者中和 I 型干扰素自身抗体。

Prevalence of neutralizing autoantibodies against type I interferon in dengue patients.
作者： Mengtian Yang, Yixin Liao, Xiaorong Yang, Jiaqi Fan, Min Liao, Yongchang Wu, Huiqin Yang, Jian Wang, Wei Zhang, Feng Li
摘要中文翻译针对 I 型干扰素 (IFN-I) 的中和自身抗体 (NAb) 的存在与各种病毒感染引起的严重并发症有关。然而,登革病毒 (DENV) 感染中 IFN-I NAb 的 患病率 仍不清楚。在这里,我们招募了 2024 年 216 名住院登革热患者,其中包括 7 名登革热休克综合征 (DSS) 患者和 42 名登革出血热 (DHF) 患者。利用灵敏的荧光素酶报告基因检测,在 7 名患者(占 DSS/DHF 病例的 14.3%)中检测到了 IFN-I NAb,并且它们的中和能力在 DENV 感染的细胞中得到了进一步验证。值得注意的是,所有 NAb 阳性患者均表现出血浆渗漏倾向和心脏损伤,并且年龄显着高于总体 队列研究(75.34 对比 IFN-I NAb 与心肌损伤和血浆渗漏的临床参数相关,心脏损伤(OR = 22.8,P = 0.003)和低蛋白血症(OR = 53.5,P = 0.001)的比值比(OR)增加。这些发现表明IFN-I NAb 可能会导致登革热的严重临床表现,需要在更大的 队列研究 中进行验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染中针对 I 型干扰素 (IFN-I) 的中和自身抗体 (NAb) 的 患病率,因为它们的存在与各种病毒感染的严重并发症有关。
设计与对象
该研究招募了2024年的216名住院登革热患者,其中包括7名登革热休克综合征(DSS)患者和42名登革出血热(DHF)患者。
核心方法
采用灵敏的荧光素酶报告基因检测法检测I型干扰素中和抗体(IFN-I NAbs),并进一步在登革病毒感染的细胞中验证其中和能力。
主要结果
7 名患者(占 DSS/DHF 病例的 14.3%)检测到 IFN-I NAb,它们与心肌损伤和血浆渗漏的临床参数相关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,IFN-I NAb 可能会导致登革热的严重临床表现,凸显了其在疾病严重程度中的潜在作用,需要在更大的 队列研究 中进行验证。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 重症登革热 机制的深入了解,并可能为未来针对 IFN-I 中和自身抗体的诊断和治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其相对较小的样本量和需要在较大的 队列研究 中进行验证而受到限制,但考虑到 IFN-I NAb 与临床结果之间的直接关联,证据是强有力的。

Prevalence of neutralizing autoantibodies against type I interferon in dengue patients.

Authors: Mengtian Yang, Yixin Liao, Xiaorong Yang, Jiaqi Fan, Min Liao, Yongchang Wu, Huiqin Yang, Jian Wang, Wei Zhang, Feng Li
Original abstract (English)
The presence of neutralizing autoantibodies (NAbs) against type I IFN (IFN-I) has been associated with severe complications arising from various viral infections. However, the prevalence of IFN-I NAbs in dengue virus (DENV) infection remains unclear. Here, we enrolled 216 hospitalized dengue patients in 2024, including 7 individuals with dengue shock syndrome (DSS) and 42 individuals with dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF). Utilizing a sensitive luciferase reporter assay, IFN-I NAbs were detected in 7 patients (14.3% of DSS/DHF cases), and their neutralizing ability was further validated in DENV-infected cells. Notably, all NAb-positive patients exhibited plasma leakage tendency and cardiac injury and were significantly older than the overall cohort (75.34 vs. IFN-I NAbs were correlated with clinical parameters of myocardial injury and plasma leakage, with increased odds ratio (OR) for cardiac injury (OR = 22.8, P = 0.003) and hypoproteinemia (OR = 53.5, P = 0.001). These findings suggest that IFN-I NAbs may contribute to severe clinical manifestations of dengue, warranting validation in larger cohorts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the prevalence of neutralizing autoantibodies (NAbs) against type I interferon (IFN-I) in dengue virus (DENV) infection, as their presence has been linked to severe complications from various viral infections.
Design and population
The study enrolled 216 hospitalized dengue patients in 2024, including 7 individuals with dengue shock syndrome (DSS) and 42 individuals with dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF).
Core methods
A sensitive luciferase reporter assay was used to detect IFN-I NAbs, and their neutralizing ability was further validated in DENV-infected cells.
Main results
IFN-I NAbs were detected in 7 patients (14.3% of DSS/DHF cases), and they were correlated with clinical parameters of myocardial injury and plasma leakage.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that IFN-I NAbs may contribute to severe clinical manifestations of dengue, highlighting a potential role in disease severity that requires validation in larger cohorts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the mechanisms of severe dengue and may inform future diagnostic and therapeutic strategies targeting IFN-I neutralizing autoantibodies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its relatively small sample size and the need for validation in larger cohorts, but the evidence is strong given the direct association between IFN-I NAbs and clinical outcomes.

先前存在的抗血栓治疗与急性登革热感染患者临床结果的关联。

Association of Pre-Existing Antithrombotic Therapy With Clinical Outcomes in Patients with Acute Dengue Infection.
作者： Po-Tseng Lee, Ling-Wei Hsu, Mu-Shiang Huang, Po-Lin Chen, Tharmaraj Vairaperumal, Ling-Shan Syue, Po-Kai Yang, Wen-Chien Ko, Ping-Yen Liu
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热感染经常引起血小板减少和出血并发症。接受抗血小板或抗凝治疗的心血管疾病患者是急性登革热感染期间临床上具有挑战性的人群。有关这些患者的临床结果和血小板恢复的数据仍然有限。方法:这项观察性研究包括单中心回顾性心血管登革热 队列研究 和探索性血小板监测 队列研究。在回顾性 队列研究 中,根据登革热 诊断 时已有的抗血栓治疗 (ATT) 对患者进行分类:无抗血栓治疗 (No ATT)、单一抗血小板治疗 (SAPT)、双重抗血小板治疗 (DAPT) 或口服抗凝治疗 (OAC)。在登革热 诊断 后 7 和 30 天内评估大出血情况。在探索性血小板监测 队列研究 中,对登革热疾病期间的连续血小板计数和血小板功能进行了评估。结果:回顾性 队列研究 包括 1,693 名患者(无 ATT,n = 1,493;SAPT,n = 163;DAPT,n = 22;OAC,n = 15)。ATT 用户年龄较大,心血管合并症负担更大,且 重症登革热 表现更频繁。No ATT、SAPT、DAPT 和 OAC 组观察到的 7 天大出血率分别为 0.34%、0.61%、4.55% 和 6.67%(P = 0.011),相应的 30 天大出血率分别为 1.15%、1.84%、9.09% 和 13.33%(P = 0.002)。在探索性血小板监测 队列研究(6 名非抗血小板使用者和 2 名阿司匹林使用者)中,阿司匹林使用者的血小板计数最低点到恢复时间比非抗血小板使用者更长[中位数 (IQR),7.5 (6.0-9.0) vs 4.0 (3.75-4.25) 天;P = 0.04]并且从最低血小板功能值到恢复的时间间隔更长[中位(IQR),9.5(8.0-11.0)与4.0(3.75-4.25)天;P = 0.04]。结论:已有 ATT 用户有更大的合并症负担和更多的 重症登革热 表现。SAPT 用户观察到的短期大出血率与未使用 ATT 的患者相似。探索性监测表明阿司匹林使用者的血小板计数和功能恢复延迟。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热感染经常引起血小板减少和出血并发症。有关这些患者的临床结果和血小板恢复的数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
接受抗血小板或抗凝治疗的心血管疾病患者是急性登革热感染期间临床上具有挑战性的人群。这项观察性研究包括单中心回顾性心血管登革热 队列研究 和探索性血小板监测 队列研究。
核心方法
在回顾性 队列研究 中,根据登革热 诊断 时已有的抗血栓治疗 (ATT) 对患者进行分类:无抗血栓治疗 (No ATT)、单一抗血小板治疗 (SAPT)、双重抗血小板治疗 (DAPT) 或口服抗凝治疗 (OAC)。在登革热 诊断 后 7 和 30 天内评估大出血情况。在探索性血小板监测 队列研究 中,对登革热疾病期间的连续血小板计数和血小板功能进行了评估。
主要结果
回顾性 队列研究 包括 1,693 名患者(无 ATT,n = 1,493;SAPT,n = 163;DAPT,n = 22;OAC,n = 15)。ATT 用户年龄较大,心血管合并症负担更大,且 重症登革热 表现更频繁。No ATT、SAPT、DAPT 和 OAC 组观察到的 7 天大出血率分别为 0.34%、0.61%、4.55% 和 6.67%(P = 0.011),相应的 30 天大出血率分别为 1.15%、1.84%、9.09% 和 13.33%(P = 0.002)。
解释与创新
先前存在的 ATT 用户有更大的合并症负担和更多的 重症登革热 表现。探索性监测表明阿司匹林使用者的血小板计数和功能恢复延迟。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为急性登革热感染期间已接受抗血栓治疗的患者的临床管理提供信息。它们还可以指导未来关于 ATT 对登革热患者血小板恢复影响的研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性和回顾性的,可能会引入偏差并限制因果推断。由于研究设计和潜在的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

Association of Pre-Existing Antithrombotic Therapy With Clinical Outcomes in Patients with Acute Dengue Infection.

Authors: Po-Tseng Lee, Ling-Wei Hsu, Mu-Shiang Huang, Po-Lin Chen, Tharmaraj Vairaperumal, Ling-Shan Syue, Po-Kai Yang, Wen-Chien Ko, Ping-Yen Liu
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue infection frequently causes thrombocytopenia and bleeding complications. Patients with cardiovascular disease who receive antiplatelet or anticoagulant therapy represent a clinically challenging population during acute dengue infection. Data regarding clinical outcomes and platelet recovery in these patients remain limited. METHODS: This observational study included a single-center retrospective cardiovascular dengue cohort and an exploratory platelet-monitoring cohort. In the retrospective cohort, patients were classified according to pre-existing antithrombotic therapy (ATT) at dengue diagnosis: no antithrombotic therapy (No ATT), single antiplatelet therapy (SAPT), dual antiplatelet therapy (DAPT), or oral anticoagulant therapy (OAC). Major bleeding was assessed within 7 and 30 days after dengue diagnosis. In the exploratory platelet-monitoring cohort, serial platelet count and platelet function were assessed during dengue illness. RESULTS: The retrospective cohort included 1,693 patients (No ATT, n = 1,493; SAPT, n = 163; DAPT, n = 22; OAC, n = 15). ATT users were older and had a greater burden of cardiovascular comorbidities and more frequent severe dengue manifestations. The observed 7-day major bleeding rates in the No ATT, SAPT, DAPT, and OAC groups were 0.34%, 0.61%, 4.55%, and 6.67%, respectively (P = 0.011), with corresponding 30-day rates of 1.15%, 1.84%, 9.09%, and 13.33% (P = 0.002). In the exploratory platelet-monitoring cohort (6 non-antiplatelet users and 2 aspirin users), aspirin users had a longer platelet count nadir-to-recovery time than non-antiplatelet users [median (IQR), 7.5 (6.0-9.0) vs 4.0 (3.75-4.25) days; P = 0.04] and a longer interval from the lowest platelet function value to recovery [median (IQR), 9.5 (8.0-11.0) vs 4.0 (3.75-4.25) days; P = 0.04]. CONCLUSIONS: Pre-existing ATT users had a greater comorbidity burden and more severe dengue manifestations. SAPT users showed similar observed short-term major bleeding rates to patients without ATT. Exploratory monitoring suggested delayed platelet count and function recovery among aspirin users.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue infection frequently causes thrombocytopenia and bleeding complications. Data regarding clinical outcomes and platelet recovery in these patients remain limited.
Design and population
Patients with cardiovascular disease who receive antiplatelet or anticoagulant therapy represent a clinically challenging population during acute dengue infection. This observational study included a single-center retrospective cardiovascular dengue cohort and an exploratory platelet-monitoring cohort.
Core methods
In the retrospective cohort, patients were classified according to pre-existing antithrombotic therapy (ATT) at dengue diagnosis: no antithrombotic therapy (No ATT), single antiplatelet therapy (SAPT), dual antiplatelet therapy (DAPT), or oral anticoagulant therapy (OAC). Major bleeding was assessed within 7 and 30 days after dengue diagnosis. In the exploratory platelet-monitoring cohort, serial platelet count and platelet function were assessed during dengue illness.
Main results
The retrospective cohort included 1,693 patients (No ATT, n = 1,493; SAPT, n = 163; DAPT, n = 22; OAC, n = 15). ATT users were older and had a greater burden of cardiovascular comorbidities and more frequent severe dengue manifestations. The observed 7-day major bleeding rates in the No ATT, SAPT, DAPT, and OAC groups were 0.34%, 0.61%, 4.55%, and 6.67%, respectively (P = 0.011), with corresponding 30-day rates of 1.15%, 1.84%, 9.09%, and 13.33% (P = 0.002).
Interpretation and novelty
Pre-existing ATT users had a greater comorbidity burden and more severe dengue manifestations. Exploratory monitoring suggested delayed platelet count and function recovery among aspirin users.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform clinical management of patients with pre-existing antithrombotic therapy during acute dengue infection. They may also guide future studies on the impact of ATT on platelet recovery in dengue patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is observational and retrospective, which may introduce bias and limit causal inference. The evidence is moderate due to the study design and potential confounding factors.

2024–2025年圣保罗登革热疫情期间,TAK-003登革疫苗在青少年人群中对有症状登革热及住院病例的1年随访评估:一项病例对照研究

1-Year follow-up evaluation of TAK-003 dengue vaccine effectiveness against symptomatic disease and hospitalisation in adolescents during the 2024–2025 outbreak in São Paulo, Brazil: a test-negative case-control study
作者： Lisany Krug Mareto, Thiago Sanches Brumatti, Roberto Dias de Oliveira, Patrícia Vieira da Silva, Edinéia Ribeiro Dos Santos, Tatiana Lang D’Agostini, Regiane A. Cardoso de Paula, Natalie E. Dean, Albert I. Ko, Derek A.T. Cummings
摘要中文翻译背景 登革热正成为全球日益严重的公共卫生威胁。我们在2024年巴西圣保罗州登革热疫情期间对青少年人群开展的TAK-003登革疫苗初步评估显示,首剂疫苗可提供短期保护,但在首剂接种后90天可能出现保护效果减弱的迹象,且对第二剂疫苗在预防住院病例中的效果评估能力有限。本研究在2024–2025年期间评估TAK-003登革疫苗在青少年人群中对有症状、病毒学确认的登革热及登革热住院病例的1年保护效果。2024年主要流行DENV-1和DENV-2,2025年DENV-3流行逐渐增加。方法 本研究在巴西圣保罗州开展了一项病例对照研究,纳入年龄10–15岁的青少年,研究时间为2024年2月20日至2025年12月31日。我们于2026年2月4日从国家登革热监测数据库(SINAN-Dengue;强制报告)及圣保罗州卫生厅健康数据注册系统(Rede Nacional de Dados em Saúde)提取了研究期间个体的年龄、合并症、登革热检测、登革热疫苗接种史、住院及死亡信息。病例定义为急性发热疾病发作且在症状出现5天内通过NS1抗原或RT-PCR检测确认的登革热。对照为检测结果阴性的急性发热疾病发作。登革热疫苗接种史数据与州健康数据注册系统进行关联。TAK-003疫苗接种1剂或2剂后的保护效果通过混合效应逻辑回归进行评估。结果 共分析166,390份检测结果,其中65,365份来自病例,101,025份来自对照。TAK-003疫苗对有症状登革热的保护效果为:1剂后为59.2%(95% CI 56.1–62.0),2剂后为73.1%(69.1–76.6);对登革热住院病例的保护效果为:1剂后为74.6%(62.7–82.7),2剂后为87.9%(73.0–94.6)。1剂疫苗对有症状登革热的保护效果在1年随访期间未出现减弱迹象:在270–364天时为73.8%(64.7–80.6),在≥365天时为76.8%(59.3–86.8)。2剂疫苗的保护效果在1年内保持稳定(270–365天时为74.6%,95% CI 59.0–84.2)。敏感性分析结果稳健,且在DENV-3流行增加期间结果一致。结论 本研究结果表明,在大规模登革热疫情期间,TAK-003疫苗在青少年人群中可提供持续1年的有症状登革热及登革热住院保护。值得注意的是,单剂疫苗在1年内保护效果持续且未出现减弱迹象,为减少接种剂次这一重要假设提供了依据,有待进一步长期观察性评估。2剂疫苗可提供最佳保护效果,尤其是在预防住院病例方面。研究结果强调了登革热疫苗接种作为公共卫生措施的重要性。资助 国家科学技术发展委员会。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是一种日益严重的全球健康威胁。我们评估了 疫苗 在 2024 年至 2025 年期间对青少年有症状、病毒学确诊的登革热和登革热住院治疗的 1 年有效性,其中 DENV-1 和 DENV-2 在 2024 年占主导地位,随后在 2025 年增加了 DENV-3 传播。
设计与对象
我们于2024年2月20日至2025年12月31日期间在圣保罗州的10-15岁青少年中进行了一项测试呈阴性的病例对照研究。我们对巴西圣保罗州2024年疫苗期间青少年中的TAK-003登革热疫苗进行了初步评估,显示出短期保护作用,但表明在第一次剂量的 90 天,并且评估第二次剂量对住院治疗的有效性的能力有限。
核心方法
病例被定义为经病毒学证实的登革热急性发热性疾病发作,通过 NS1 抗原或 RT-PCR 检测在症状出现后 5 天内检测到。对照是测试阴性的急性发热性疾病发作。使用混合效应逻辑回归评估一剂或两剂 TAK-003 后 疫苗 的有效性。
主要结果
我们分析了 166,390 项测试:其中 65,365 项来自病例,101,025 项来自对照。疫苗 治疗一剂后对有症状登革热的有效性为 59.2%(95% CI 56.1-62.0),两剂后为 73.1%(69.1-76.6)。针对登革热住院治疗,接种一剂后的有效率为 74.6%(62.7-82.7),两剂后的有效性为 87.9%(73.0-94.6)。
解释与创新
单剂疫苗对有症状登革热的保护效果在1年随访期间未出现减弱迹象:在270–364天时为73.8%(64.7–80.6),在≥365天时为76.8%(59.3–86.8)。2剂疫苗的保护效果在1年内保持稳定(270–365天时为74.6%,95% CI 59.0–84.2)。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强化了登革热 疫苗接种 作为公共卫生措施的重要性。敏感性分析的结果是稳健的,并且在 DENV-3 循环增加期间具有可比性。
局限与证据强度
我们的研究结果表明,TAK-003 在大 暴发 期间对青少年有症状的登革热和登革热住院治疗具有持续 1 年的保护作用。所提供的证据没有报告任何具体限制。

1-Year follow-up evaluation of TAK-003 dengue vaccine effectiveness against symptomatic disease and hospitalisation in adolescents during the 2024–2025 outbreak in São Paulo, Brazil: a test-negative case-control study

Authors: Lisany Krug Mareto, Thiago Sanches Brumatti, Roberto Dias de Oliveira, Patrícia Vieira da Silva, Edinéia Ribeiro Dos Santos, Tatiana Lang D’Agostini, Regiane A. Cardoso de Paula, Natalie E. Dean, Albert I. Ko, Derek A.T. Cummings
Original abstract (English)
Background Dengue is a growing global health threat. Our initial evaluation of the TAK-003 dengue vaccine in adolescents during the 2024 outbreak in São Paulo State, Brazil, showed short-term protection but suggested possible effectiveness waning after 90-days of the first dose, and had limited power to estimate second-dose effectiveness against hospitalisation. We evaluated 1-year vaccine effectiveness against symptomatic, virologically confirmed dengue and dengue hospitalisation in adolescents during 2024–2025, when DENV-1 and DENV-2 predominated in 2024 followed by increasing DENV-3 transmission in 2025. Methods We did a test-negative case–control study among adolescents aged 10–15 years in São Paulo State between Feb 20, 2024, and Dec 31, 2025. We extracted individual-level information on demographic characteristics, comorbidities, dengue testing, dengue vaccination, hospitalisation, and death during the study period from the national surveillance databases for dengue (SINAN-Dengue; compulsory notification) and the São Paulo State Secretary of Health vaccination registry (Rede Nacional de Dados em Saúde) on Feb 4, 2026. Cases were defined as acute febrile illness episodes with virologically confirmed dengue, as detected by NS1 antigen or RT-PCR testing within 5 days of symptom onset. Controls were test-negative acute febrile illness episodes. Surveillance data were linked to the state vaccination registry. Vaccine effectiveness after one or two TAK-003 doses was estimated with mixed-effects logistic regression. Findings We analysed 166,390 tests: 65,365 from cases and 101,025 from controls. Vaccine effectiveness against symptomatic dengue was 59.2% (95% CI 56.1–62.0) after one dose and 73.1% (69.1–76.6) after two doses. Against dengue hospitalisation, effectiveness was 74.6% (62.7–82.7) after one dose and 87.9% (73.0–94.6) after two doses. One-dose protection against symptomatic disease showed no evidence of waning over 1-year follow-up: effectiveness was 73.8% (64.7–80.6) at 270–364 days and 76.8% (59.3–86.8) at 365 days or more. Two-dose protection remained stable to 1 year (74.6%, 95% CI 59.0–84.2, at 270–365 days). Findings were robust in sensitivity analyses and comparable during increased DENV-3 circulation. Interpretation Our findings show that TAK-003 demonstrated sustained 1-year protection against symptomatic dengue and dengue hospitalisation in adolescents during a large outbreak. Notably, a single dose showed sustained effectiveness without evidence of waning over 1 year, generating an important hypothesis regarding dose reduction that warrants continued long-term observational evaluation. A second dose provided optimal protection, particularly against hospitalisation. These findings reinforce the importance of dengue vaccination as a public health measure. Funding National Council for Scientific and Technological Development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a growing global health threat. We evaluated 1-year vaccine effectiveness against symptomatic, virologically confirmed dengue and dengue hospitalisation in adolescents during 2024–2025, when DENV-1 and DENV-2 predominated in 2024 followed by increasing DENV-3 transmission in 2025.
Design and population
We did a test-negative case–control study among adolescents aged 10–15 years in São Paulo State between Feb 20, 2024, and Dec 31, 2025. Our initial evaluation of the TAK-003 dengue vaccine in adolescents during the 2024 outbreak in São Paulo State, Brazil, showed short-term protection but suggested possible effectiveness waning after 90-days of the first dose, and had limited power to estimate second-dose effectiveness against hospitalisation.
Core methods
Cases were defined as acute febrile illness episodes with virologically confirmed dengue, as detected by NS1 antigen or RT-PCR testing within 5 days of symptom onset. Controls were test-negative acute febrile illness episodes. Vaccine effectiveness after one or two TAK-003 doses was estimated with mixed-effects logistic regression.
Main results
We analysed 166,390 tests: 65,365 from cases and 101,025 from controls. Vaccine effectiveness against symptomatic dengue was 59.2% (95% CI 56.1–62.0) after one dose and 73.1% (69.1–76.6) after two doses. Against dengue hospitalisation, effectiveness was 74.6% (62.7–82.7) after one dose and 87.9% (73.0–94.6) after two doses.
Interpretation and novelty
One-dose protection against symptomatic disease showed no evidence of waning over 1-year follow-up: effectiveness was 73.8% (64.7–80.6) at 270–364 days and 76.8% (59.3–86.8) at 365 days or more. Two-dose protection remained stable to 1 year (74.6%, 95% CI 59.0–84.2, at 270–365 days).
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings reinforce the importance of dengue vaccination as a public health measure. Findings were robust in sensitivity analyses and comparable during increased DENV-3 circulation.
Limitations and evidence strength
Our findings show that TAK-003 demonstrated sustained 1-year protection against symptomatic dengue and dengue hospitalisation in adolescents during a large outbreak. The supplied evidence does not report any specific limitations.

流行病学 Demam Berdarah 登革热问题 Kesehatan Masyarakat:Tren Satu Dekade dan Variabilitas Iklim di Nusa Tenggara Barat

Dinamika Epidemiologi Demam Berdarah Dengue dalam Perspektif Kesehatan Masyarakat: Tren Satu Dekade dan Variabilitas Iklim di Nusa Tenggara Barat
作者： Eva Hikmatul Damayanti, Nurhidayati Nurhidayati, Deasy Irawati, Marisa Syavitri
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述印度尼西亚西努沙登加拉省 (NTB) 十年间登革热的流行病学模式,并探讨其与气候变化的关系。尽管气候因素的影响因当地环境而异,但登革出血热仍然是热带地区紧迫的公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
分析了2015年至2024年的二级年度数据,包括登革热发病率、死亡率和病死率(CFR),以及平均和最高温度、相对湿度、降雨量、雨天数和日照时数等气象指标。年度变化与重大破坏性事件同时发生,包括 2018 年地震和 COVID-19 大流行。
核心方法
进行描述性统计,然后进行简单线性回归以探索潜在关系。应用时间序列模型来评估预测能力。
主要结果
登革热 发病率 范围为每 100,000 人 10.7 至 88.9 人(平均 51.7/100,000),病死率始终低于 2%。线性回归分析显示了一致的模式:相对湿度与 发病率 呈正相关(B ≈ +9.8,R2 高达 0.45,当排除大流行年份时,p = 0.071),而平均温度和日照时长则呈现负相关。降雨量和下雨天数显示出微弱且不一致的关联。
解释与创新
相对湿度似乎是最一致的气候决定因素。时间序列建模反映了短期趋势,但缺乏精度,凸显了加强本地 监测 系统的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
在 NTB,登革热 发病率 每年都会波动,并受到气候变化以及地震和 COVID-19 大流行等异常事件的影响。研究结果强调需要改进本地 监测 系统,以更好地监测和预测登革热 监测。
局限与证据强度
使用 Holt 指数平滑的时间序列分析捕获了短期变异性(平稳 R2 = 0.82),但产生了相对较高的预测误差(MAPE 44%)。提供的证据未报告具体的统计功效或样本量计算。

Dinamika Epidemiologi Demam Berdarah Dengue dalam Perspektif Kesehatan Masyarakat: Tren Satu Dekade dan Variabilitas Iklim di Nusa Tenggara Barat

Authors: Eva Hikmatul Damayanti, Nurhidayati Nurhidayati, Deasy Irawati, Marisa Syavitri
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue hemorrhagic fever remains a pressing public health concern in tropical regions, although the influence of climatic factors varies across local settings. This study aims to describe the epidemiological patterns of dengue over a ten-year period in West Nusa Tenggara (NTB), Indonesia, and to examine its association with climate variability. Secondary annual data from 2015 to 2024 were analyzed, including dengue incidence, mortality, and case fatality rate (CFR), along with meteorological indicators such as average and maximum temperature, relative humidity, rainfall, number of rainy days, and duration of sunshine. Descriptive statistics were performed, followed by simple linear regression to explore potential relationships. Time series modeling was applied to assess predictive capacity. Dengue incidence ranged from 10.7 to 88.9 per 100,000 population (mean 51.7/100,000), with CFR consistently below 2%. Annual variations coincided with major disruptive events, including the 2018 earthquake and the COVID-19 pandemic. Linear regression analysis demonstrated consistent patterns: relative humidity showed a positive association with incidence (B ≈ +9.8, R2 up to 0.45, p = 0.071 when pandemic years were excluded), whereas average temperature and sunshine duration exhibited inverse relationships. Rainfall and number of rainy days showed weak and inconsistent associations. Time series analysis using Holt’s exponential smoothing captured short-term variability (stationary R2 = 0.82) but produced relatively high prediction error (MAPE 44%). In NTB, dengue incidence fluctuates annually and is influenced by climatic variability as well as extraordinary events such as earthquakes and the COVID-19 pandemic. Relative humidity appears to be the most consistent climatic determinant. Time series modeling reflects short-term trends but lacks precision, underscoring the importance of strengthening local surveillance systems. Keywords: Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever, Climate Variability, Epidemiology, Humidity, West Nusa Tenggara. ABSTRAK Demam berdarah dengue masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat yang mendesak di wilayah tropis, namun pengaruh iklim bervariasi antar konteks lokal. Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk menggambarkan epidemiologi demam berdarah dengue selama satu dekade di Nusa Tenggara Barat (NTB), Indonesia, dan mengkaji hubungannya dengan variabilitas iklim. Data tahunan sekunder dari tahun 2015–2024 dianalisis, meliputi insiden demam berdarah, mortalitas, dan tingkat fatalitas kasus (CFR) beserta indikator meteorologi (suhu rata-rata dan maksimum, kelembapan relatif, curah hujan, hari hujan, dan durasi sinar matahari). Statistik deskriptif dilakukan, dilanjutkan dengan regresi linier sederhana untuk mengeksplorasi hubungan. Pemodelan deret waktu diterapkan untuk menilai potensi prediktif. Insiden dengue berkisar antara 10,7 dan 88,9 per 100.000 penduduk (rata-rata 51,7/100.000), dengan CFR secara konsisten di bawah 2%. Fluktuasi tahunan bertepatan dengan peristiwa anomali seperti gempa bumi tahun 2018 dan pandemi COVID-19. Regresi linier menunjukkan tren yang konsisten: kelembapan relatif berkorelasi positif dengan insiden (B ≈ +9,8, R2 hingga 0,45, p = 0,071 ketika tahun pandemi tidak termasuk), sementara suhu rata-rata dan durasi sinar matahari berkorelasi terbalik. Curah hujan dan hari hujan memberikan kontribusi asosiasi yang lemah dan tidak konsisten. Analisis deret waktu (pemulusan eksponensial Holt) menangkap variabilitas jangka pendek (R2 stasioner = 0,82) tetapi menunjukkan kesalahan prediksi yang tinggi (MAPE 44%). Di NTB, insiden demam berdarah berfluktuasi setiap tahun, dipengaruhi oleh variasi iklim serta kejadian luar biasa seperti gempa bumi dan wabah COVID-19. Kelembapan menunjukkan hubungan paling konsisten dengan insiden. Pemodelan deret waktu mencerminkan pola jangka pendek tetapi kurang presisi, sehingga menyoroti pentingnya sistem pengawasan lokal. Kata Kunci: Demam Berdarah, Perubahan Iklim, Epidemiologi, Kelembaban, Nusa Barat Tenggara.

双读出 RT-LAMP,用于快速现场 登革病毒 检测和血清分型

Dual readout RT-LAMP for rapid on-site dengue virus detection and serotyping
作者： Rebecca Brambilla, Neesha Rockwood, Janine Michel, Kalpa Kariyawasam, Hansi Ganhewa, Johannes Scholz, Inoka C. Perera, Andreas Nitsche, Andreas Puyskens
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Dengue is the fastest-growing mosquito-borne disease, with over 14 million reported cases globally in 2024, rendering dengue a major public health issue of global concern.
设计与对象
我们开发了四种血清型特异性 登革病毒 (DENV) 逆转录 (RT)-LAMP 检测,其中引物设计基于来自不同地理区域的 4,277 个完整 基因组 序列。
核心方法
虽然实时 PCR 目前仍然是 登革病毒 检测的黄金标准,但它需要在已建立的实验室基础设施中配备热循环仪和经过培训的人员,这限制了其在资源匮乏的环境中的使用。
主要结果
使用来自斯里兰卡的 95 个 PCR 确认的阳性样本(包括血清型 1-3)进行的临床评估显示,DENV1 的检出率为 91.2%,DENV2 的检出率为 78.6%,DENV3 的检出率为 100%,并且未观察到血清型之间的交叉反应。
解释与创新
60 分钟后,每次反应的分析灵敏度达到 96–734 基因组 当量。
科研与公卫意义
双读出设计可实现实时荧光检测和无设备视觉比色检测,使该检测适用于全球登革热流行地区的现场护理应用。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Dual readout RT-LAMP for rapid on-site dengue virus detection and serotyping

Authors: Rebecca Brambilla, Neesha Rockwood, Janine Michel, Kalpa Kariyawasam, Hansi Ganhewa, Johannes Scholz, Inoka C. Perera, Andreas Nitsche, Andreas Puyskens
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue is the fastest-growing mosquito-borne disease, with over 14 million reported cases globally in 2024, rendering dengue a major public health issue of global concern. Fast and reliable serotype-specific diagnosis of dengue is critical for patient risk management and epidemiological surveillance. While real-time PCR currently remains the gold standard for dengue virus detection, it requires thermal cyclers and trained personnel in an established laboratory infrastructure, limiting its use in low-resource settings. Loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) offers a promising alternative, yet currently available reverse transcription assays for dengue either rely on limited sequences used for primer design, thus failing to adequately capture global genetic diversity, or use multiple primer sets, increasing complexity and costs. We developed four serotype-specific dengue virus (DENV) reverse transcription (RT)-LAMP assays in which primer design was informed by 4,277 whole-genome sequences from different geographical regions. Degenerate base positions were incorporated into primers to achieve broad detection capability while minimizing the number of distinct oligonucleotides needed. The assays achieved analytical sensitivities of 96–734 genome equivalents per reaction after 60 min. Clinical evaluation using 95 PCR-confirmed positive samples from Sri Lanka, including serotypes 1–3, demonstrated detection rates of 91.2% for DENV1, 78.6% for DENV2, and 100% for DENV3, with no cross-reactivity observed between serotypes. Evaluation using DENV4-spiked serum demonstrated reliable detection at clinically relevant viral loads, with complete detection at quantification cycle (Cq) values of ~30. A dual readout design enables both real-time fluorescence detection and equipment-free visual colorimetric detection, rendering the assays suitable for point-of-care applications in dengue-endemic regions worldwide. IMPORTANCE Dengue virus infections pose a growing threat to global health, with cases doubling annually since 2021, yet reliable diagnostic tools remain inaccessible in many affected regions. Current gold-standard molecular tests require expensive equipment and specialized laboratories, creating a critical gap in dengue-endemic areas where rapid, on-site diagnosis could save lives through early patient management and outbreak control. This study addresses this gap by developing dengue virus serotype-specific detection assays that work at a constant temperature without the need for sophisticated and expensive equipment. By analyzing thousands of dengue virus genome sequences and strategically incorporating degenerate bases, we designed primers that can detect diverse viral strains while maintaining simplicity and low cost. The assays provide results in 40–60 min, depending on the workflow, and can be read visually without instruments, making them practical for use in clinics, field settings, and resource-limited laboratories where dengue burden is highest.

印度的 登革热 处于十字路口:后疫情时代的时空趋势、气候变化和 疫苗 挑战

Dengue Fever in India at a Crossroads: Spatiotemporal Trends, Climate Change, and Vaccine Challenges in the Post-COVID Era
作者： Kaliyamoorthy Dass, Rengarajan Murugesan
摘要中文翻译登革热 是印度的一个重大公共卫生问题,因为其 发病率 和 死亡率 比率随环境、社会和基础设施因素而变化。目前有效的疫苗还不多,所以主要的治疗是支持治疗、媒介控制、监测。本研究旨在研究 2023 年至 2025 年印度各邦的登革热病例和死亡模式,重点关注区域差异、COVID-19 大流行和气候变化的影响以及 疫苗 的开发进展。由于 COVID-19 大流行,印度的登革热病例仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题。2023年,印度报告了289,235例登革热病例,485人死亡。2024 年,报告病例数下降至 233,519 例,死亡 297 例。截至 2025 年 11 月 30 日,已报告 113,540 例登革热病例和 95 例死亡。在此期间,西孟加拉邦、马哈拉施特拉邦、旁遮普邦、北方邦和卡纳塔克邦一直是登革热负担最高的地区。城市化和气候变化是两个主要原因。目前仍然只有少数 疫苗 可供选择,并且尚不清楚减毒活 疫苗(Dengvaxia® 和 Qdenga)的安全性和有效性。登革热在印度仍然是一个日益严重且不断演变的威胁。全面的解决方案必须涵盖环境管理、疫苗 研究、公共卫生监测和气候变化适应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在研究 2023 年至 2025 年印度各邦的登革热病例和死亡模式,重点关注区域差异、COVID-19 大流行和气候变化的影响以及 疫苗 的开发进展。登革热 是印度的一个重大公共卫生问题,因为其 发病率 和 死亡率 比率随环境、社会和基础设施因素而变化。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2023 年至 2025 年印度各邦的登革热病例和死亡情况。研究重点是区域差异以及 COVID-19 大流行和气候变化对登革热趋势的影响。2023 年,印度报告了 289,235 例登革热病例和 485 例死亡,2024 年下降至 233,519 例病例和 297 例死亡。
核心方法
该研究使用了 2023 年至 2025 年期间印度各邦报告的病例和死亡数据。它的重点是分析区域差异以及大流行和气候变化对登革热趋势的影响。数据来源于公共卫生记录,并分析其模式和相关性。
主要结果
2023年,印度报告了289,235例登革热病例,485人死亡。2024 年,报告病例数下降至 233,519 例,死亡 297 例。截至 2025 年 11 月 30 日,已报告 113,540 例登革热病例和 95 例死亡。
解释与创新
该研究强调了尽管登革热大流行,但印度对登革热的持续公共卫生担忧。它强调需要采取多方面的方法来应对不断变化的登革热威胁。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有必要继续开展 监测、环境管理和 疫苗 研究,以减轻印度登革热的影响。他们还强调气候变化在塑造登革热趋势中的作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于报告的数据,这些数据可能会受到 监测 实践的漏报或变化的影响。证据是温和的,因为它基于未经实验验证的观察数据。

Dengue Fever in India at a Crossroads: Spatiotemporal Trends, Climate Change, and Vaccine Challenges in the Post-COVID Era

Authors: Kaliyamoorthy Dass, Rengarajan Murugesan
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever is a major public health issue in India because its incidence and mortality rates vary with environmental, social, and infrastructural factors. There are not many effective vaccines yet, so the main treatments are supportive care, vector control, and surveillance. This study aimed to examine patterns in dengue cases and deaths across Indian states from 2023-2025, focusing on regional differences, the effects of the COVID-19 pandemic and climate change, and the progress of vaccine development. Due to the COVID-19 pandemic, dengue cases in India remained a significant public health concern. In 2023, India reported 289,235 dengue cases and 485 deaths. In 2024, the number of reported cases declined to 233,519, with 297 deaths. As of 30 November 2025, 113,540 dengue cases and 95 deaths had been reported. Throughout this period, West Bengal, Maharashtra, Punjab, Uttar Pradesh, and Karnataka consistently recorded the highest dengue burden. Urbanization and climate change were two of the main reasons. There are still only a few vaccines to choose from, and it’s not clear how safe and effective the live-attenuated vaccines (Dengvaxia® and Qdenga). Dengue remains a growing and evolving threat in India. A comprehensive solution must encompass environmental management, vaccine research, public health monitoring, and climate change adaptation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to examine patterns in dengue cases and deaths across Indian states from 2023-2025, focusing on regional differences, the effects of the COVID-19 pandemic and climate change, and the progress of vaccine development. Dengue fever is a major public health issue in India because its incidence and mortality rates vary with environmental, social, and infrastructural factors.
Design and population
The study analyzed dengue cases and deaths across Indian states from 2023 to 2025. It focused on regional differences and the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic and climate change on dengue trends. India reported 289,235 dengue cases and 485 deaths in 2023, with a decline to 233,519 cases and 297 deaths in 2024.
Core methods
The study used reported case and death data from Indian states over the period 2023-2025. It focused on analyzing regional differences and the effects of the pandemic and climate change on dengue trends. The data were sourced from public health records and analyzed for patterns and correlations.
Main results
In 2023, India reported 289,235 dengue cases and 485 deaths. In 2024, the number of reported cases declined to 233,519, with 297 deaths. As of 30 November 2025, 113,540 dengue cases and 95 deaths had been reported.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the persistent public health concern of dengue in India despite the pandemic. It underscores the need for a multifaceted approach to address the evolving threat of dengue.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the necessity of continued surveillance, environmental management, and vaccine research to mitigate the impact of dengue in India. They also emphasize the role of climate change in shaping dengue trends.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on reported data, which may be subject to underreporting or variability in surveillance practices. The evidence is moderate as it is based on observational data without experimental validation.

绘制印度尼西亚打横登革热 传播 时空热点图。

Mapping Spatio-Temporal Hotspots of Dengue Transmission in Tasikmalaya, Indonesia.
作者： Imas Masturoh, Ida Sugiarti, Fery Fadly, Mujiyanto Mujiyanto, Endang Puji Astuti
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是热带和亚热带地区(包括印度尼西亚)由 媒介 传播的主要疾病,在印度尼西亚,媒介 的复发仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题。本研究旨在解决了解登革热 监测 模式以改进 监测 和控制的需要。
设计与对象
该研究在印度尼西亚西爪哇省打横市进行,使用了 2022 年全年报告的登革热病例和 2023 年 1 月至 9 月报告的 279 例登革热病例。研究对象包括不同城中村的登革热病例个体。
核心方法
使用地理信息系统(GIS)和空间统计分析进行村级空间分析。The space-time analysis was based on symptom onset dates ranging from 27 December 2022 to 30 September 2023. Global Moran's I,Local Indicators of Spatial Association (LISA),bivariate LISA,and the spatial scan statistic implemented in SaTScan were used to assess spatial distribution and spatio-temporal clustering.
主要结果
Of the 279 cases reported in 2023, 275 had valid residential coordinates and were included in the space-time cluster analysis. Spatial clustering of dengue cases was identified in several urban villages, particularly around Cipedes and Tugujaya. Spatio-temporal analysis detected three statistically significant clusters during 2023, indicating that dengue transmission was concentrated in specific geographic areas during distinct periods.
解释与创新
研究结果强调了登革热 传播 的独特时空热点,并强调了人口密度和季节性因素在影响登革热风险中的作用。这项研究为打横登革热 暴发 的地理和时间模式提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
空间测绘和聚类检测可以支持有针对性的 媒介 控制和公共卫生干预措施。研究结果可为登革热多发地区制定更有效的 监测 和预防策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到对报告病例的依赖和潜在数据缺失的限制。该证据是温和的,因为它是基于登革热病例的空间分析,而没有对病毒进行直接的生物学确认。

Mapping Spatio-Temporal Hotspots of Dengue Transmission in Tasikmalaya, Indonesia.

Authors: Imas Masturoh, Ida Sugiarti, Fery Fadly, Mujiyanto Mujiyanto, Endang Puji Astuti
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND OBJECTIVES: Dengue is a major vector-borne disease in tropical and subtropical regions, including Indonesia, where recurrent outbreaks remain a significant public health concern. Understanding the spatial and temporal distribution of dengue transmission is essential for effective surveillance and targeted control strategies. This study aimed to map the spatio-temporal hotspots of dengue transmission in Tasikmalaya City, West Java, Indonesia, using Geographic Information Systems (GIS) and spatial statistical analysis. METHODS: Village-level spatial analyses were conducted using reported dengue cases from the complete calendar year of 2022 and 279 cases reported between January and September 2023. Of the 279 cases reported in 2023, 275 had valid residential coordinates and were included in the space-time cluster analysis. The space-time analysis was based on symptom onset dates ranging from 27 December 2022 to 30 September 2023. Global Moran's I, Local Indicators of Spatial Association (LISA), bivariate LISA, and the spatial scan statistic implemented in SaTScan were used to assess the spatial distribution and spatio-temporal clustering of dengue cases. RESULTS: Spatial clustering of dengue cases was identified in several urban villages, particularly around Cipedes and Tugujaya. Bivariate LISA revealed significant spatial co-occurrence between high dengue incidence and high population density. Spatio-temporal analysis detected three statistically significant clusters during 2023, indicating that dengue transmission was concentrated in specific geographic areas during distinct periods rather than being uniformly distributed over time. INTERPRETATION CONCLUSION: The findings highlight distinct spatio-temporal hotspots of dengue transmission and emphasize the role of population density and seasonal factors in shaping dengue risk. Spatial mapping and cluster detection can support targeted vector control and public health interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a major vector-borne disease in tropical and subtropical regions, including Indonesia, where recurrent outbreaks remain a significant public health concern. This study aimed to address the need for understanding dengue transmission patterns to improve surveillance and control.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Tasikmalaya City, West Java, Indonesia, using reported dengue cases from the complete calendar year of 2022 and 279 cases reported between January and September 2023. The population included individuals with dengue cases across different urban villages.
Core methods
Village-level spatial analyses were performed using Geographic Information Systems (GIS) and spatial statistical analysis. The space-time analysis was based on symptom onset dates ranging from 27 December 2022 to 30 September 2023. Global Moran's I, Local Indicators of Spatial Association (LISA), bivariate LISA, and the spatial scan statistic implemented in SaTScan were used to assess spatial distribution and spatio-temporal clustering.
Main results
Of the 279 cases reported in 2023, 275 had valid residential coordinates and were included in the space-time cluster analysis. Spatial clustering of dengue cases was identified in several urban villages, particularly around Cipedes and Tugujaya. Spatio-temporal analysis detected three statistically significant clusters during 2023, indicating that dengue transmission was concentrated in specific geographic areas during distinct periods.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight distinct spatio-temporal hotspots of dengue transmission and emphasize the role of population density and seasonal factors in shaping dengue risk. This study provides novel insights into the geographic and temporal patterns of dengue outbreaks in Tasikmalaya.
Scientific and public-health significance
Spatial mapping and cluster detection can support targeted vector control and public health interventions. The results can inform the development of more effective surveillance and prevention strategies in dengue-prone areas.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on reported cases and the potential for missing data. The evidence is moderate as it is based on spatial analysis of dengue cases without direct biological confirmation of the virus.

墨西哥实验室确诊的登革热病例中,流行年增加了住院治疗,但 死亡率 没有增加:2020-2025 年全国 监测 研究

Epidemic Years Increase Hospitalization but Not Mortality Among Laboratory-Confirmed Dengue Cases in Mexico: A Nationwide Surveillance Study, 2020–2025
作者： Leticia Jaimes Betancourt, Alfonso Vallejos-Parás, Porfirio Felipe Hernández Bautista, David Alejandro Cabrera-Gaytán, Bernardo Cacho‐Díaz, Adriana Josefina Toríz Saldaña, Lumumba Arriaga Nieto, Oscar Cruz Orozco, Gabriel Valle Alvarado, Mónica Grisel Rivera Mahey
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是墨西哥的一个主要公共卫生问题,近年来该国曾发生大规模流行病。尽管 重症登革热 的人口统计学和临床​​预测因素已得到广泛研究,但流行病 传播 时期对住院治疗和 死亡率 的影响仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
使用墨西哥国家流行病学 监测 系统报告的 2020 年至 2025 年间实验室确诊的登革热病例进行了一项全国性回顾性分析研究。根据官方流行渠道分析,年份被分为流行期(2023-2024 年)或非流行期(2020-2022 年和 2025 年)。总共纳入了 235,226 例实验室确诊的登革热病例,其中 90,661 例(38.5%)住院治疗,1604 例(0.68%)死亡。
核心方法
开发多变量逻辑回归模型来识别与住院和 死亡率 独立相关的因素。住院治疗和 死亡率 作为主要结局进行评估。
主要结果
Hospitalization was more frequent during epidemic years than during non-epidemic years (40.2% vs. 34.1%; crude OR 1.30, 95% CI 1.27–1.32; p < 0.001), whereas mortality did not differ significantly (0.68% vs. 0.69%; crude OR 0.99, 95% CI 0.89–1.11; p = 0.919). In multivariable analysis, epidemic-year status remained independently associated with hospitalization (aOR 1.26, 95% CI 1.24–1.29; p < 0.001) but not with mortality (aOR 1.00, 95% CI 0.90–1.12; p = 0.946).
解释与创新
流行年份与墨西哥实验室确诊登革热病例住院率的增加有关,但与 死亡率 的增加无关。这些发现强调了在流行期间加强医院准备工作的重要性,同时优先考虑对高危患者的临床监测。
科研与公卫意义
死亡率 与年龄和潜在合并症(尤其是慢性肾脏病)的相关性更强。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。该研究是一项全国性的监测研究,样本量大,统计分析清晰,证据强度高。

Epidemic Years Increase Hospitalization but Not Mortality Among Laboratory-Confirmed Dengue Cases in Mexico: A Nationwide Surveillance Study, 2020–2025

Authors: Leticia Jaimes Betancourt, Alfonso Vallejos-Parás, Porfirio Felipe Hernández Bautista, David Alejandro Cabrera-Gaytán, Bernardo Cacho‐Díaz, Adriana Josefina Toríz Saldaña, Lumumba Arriaga Nieto, Oscar Cruz Orozco, Gabriel Valle Alvarado, Mónica Grisel Rivera Mahey
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is a major public health problem in Mexico, where large epidemics have occurred during recent years. Although demographic and clinical predictors of severe dengue have been widely studied, the impact of epidemic transmission periods on hospitalization and mortality remains poorly understood. This study evaluated the association between epidemic years and hospitalization and mortality among laboratory-confirmed dengue cases in Mexico. A nationwide retrospective analytical study was conducted using laboratory-confirmed dengue cases reported through the Mexican National Epidemiological Surveillance System between 2020 and 2025. Years were classified as epidemic (2023–2024) or non-epidemic (2020–2022 and 2025) according to official endemic channel analyses. Hospitalization and mortality were evaluated as primary outcomes. Multivariable logistic regression models were developed to identify factors independently associated with hospitalization and mortality. A total of 235,226 laboratory-confirmed dengue cases were included, of whom 90,661 (38.5%) were hospitalized and 1604 (0.68%) died. Overall, 170,819 cases (72.6%) occurred during epidemic years. Hospitalization was more frequent during epidemic years than during non-epidemic years (40.2% vs. 34.1%; crude OR 1.30, 95% CI 1.27–1.32; p < 0.001), whereas mortality did not differ significantly (0.68% vs. 0.69%; crude OR 0.99, 95% CI 0.89–1.11; p = 0.919). In multivariable analysis, epidemic-year status remained independently associated with hospitalization (aOR 1.26, 95% CI 1.24–1.29; p < 0.001) but not with mortality (aOR 1.00, 95% CI 0.90–1.12; p = 0.946). Chronic kidney disease showed the strongest independent association with both hospitalization (aOR 5.36, 95% CI 4.33–6.65) and mortality (aOR 6.98, 95% CI 5.23–9.32). Advanced age and other underlying comorbidities were also independently associated with adverse outcomes. Epidemic years were associated with increased hospitalization among laboratory-confirmed dengue cases in Mexico but were not associated with increased mortality. Mortality was more strongly associated with age and underlying comorbidities, particularly chronic kidney disease. These findings highlight the importance of strengthening hospital preparedness during epidemic periods while prioritizing clinical monitoring of high-risk patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a major public health problem in Mexico, where large epidemics have occurred during recent years. Although demographic and clinical predictors of severe dengue have been widely studied, the impact of epidemic transmission periods on hospitalization and mortality remains poorly understood.
Design and population
A nationwide retrospective analytical study was conducted using laboratory-confirmed dengue cases reported through the Mexican National Epidemiological Surveillance System between 2020 and 2025. A total of 235,226 laboratory-confirmed dengue cases were included, of whom 90,661 (38.5%) were hospitalized and 1604 (0.68%) died.
Core methods
Multivariable logistic regression models were developed to identify factors independently associated with hospitalization and mortality. Hospitalization and mortality were evaluated as primary outcomes.

Hospitalization was more frequent during epidemic years than during non-epidemic years (40.2% vs. 34.1%; crude OR 1.30, 95% CI 1.27–1.32; p < 0.001), whereas mortality did not differ significantly (0.68% vs. 0.69%; crude OR 0.99, 95% CI 0.89–1.11; p = 0.919). In multivariable analysis, epidemic-year status remained independently associated with hospitalization (aOR 1.26, 95% CI 1.24–1.29; p < 0.001) but not with mortality (aOR 1.00, 95% CI 0.90–1.12; p = 0.946).
Interpretation and novelty

These findings highlight the importance of strengthening hospital preparedness during epidemic periods while prioritizing clinical monitoring of high-risk patients.

Mortality was more strongly associated with age and underlying comorbidities, particularly chronic kidney disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study. The evidence strength is high as the study is a nationwide surveillance study with a large sample size and clear statistical analyses.

中国热带地区登革热的气象驱动因素和短期预测:来自海南省的证据

Meteorological drivers and short-term prediction of dengue fever in a tropical region of China: evidence from Hainan Province
作者： Yiyao Lian, Yan Jin, Yu-Fei Wang, Li Qiu, Jing-Jing Chen, Yu-Qing Guo, Rui Jiang, Dapeng Yin, Wenbiao Hu, Li-Ping Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查气象因素如何通过非线性和滞后效应影响登革热 传播,满足了解这些动态以改进早期预警系统的需要。
设计与对象
该研究分析了2018-2019年和2023-2024年中国海南省的每周登革热病例和气象数据,重点关注天气变量与登革热风险之间的关联。
核心方法
采用分布式滞后非线性模型(DLNM)对数据进行分析,并根据累积的气象风险信号和输入病例构建早期风险预测指数(ERPI)。
主要结果
在四个模拟年份报告的 927 例登革热病例中,727 例(78.43%)是本地感染。周平均每日最高气温 (Tmax)、降水量 (Pmean)、风速 (WSmean) 和昼夜温差 (DTRmean) 与登革热风险呈非线性、滞后相关。
解释与创新
研究发现,高温主要与中长期登革热风险相关,而中低至中等昼夜温度范围则增加了短期风险,凸显了气候因素与登革热之间的复杂关系。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将气象数据纳入预警系统可以加强热带地区的登革热控制,为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供基础。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于观察数据,在实施之前需要外部验证,表明对预测模型的实用性有一定的信心。

Meteorological drivers and short-term prediction of dengue fever in a tropical region of China: evidence from Hainan Province

Authors: Yiyao Lian, Yan Jin, Yu-Fei Wang, Li Qiu, Jing-Jing Chen, Yu-Qing Guo, Rui Jiang, Dapeng Yin, Wenbiao Hu, Li-Ping Wang
Original abstract (English)
Objective Meteorological factors influence dengue transmission through nonlinear and lagged effects. This study assessed these associations in Hainan Province and developed an early risk prediction index. Methods Weekly dengue case and meteorological data from Hainan Province (2018–2019 and 2023–2024) were analyzed using distributed lag non-linear models (DLNMs). An early risk prediction index(ERPI) was constructed from DLNM-derived cumulative meteorological risk signals and imported cases. Predictive performance was evaluated using leave-one-year-out cross-validation and assessed by the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC), sensitivity, specificity, positive predictive value (PPV), and negative predictive value (NPV). Results Among 927 dengue cases reported during the four modelled years, 727 (78.43%) were locally acquired. Weekly mean daily maximum temperature (Tmax), precipitation (Pmean), wind speed (WSmean), and diurnal temperature range (DTRmean) showed nonlinear, lag-dependent associations with dengue risk, with distinct temporal patterns. Higher Tmax showed longer-lag effects, peaking at 33.73 °C at lag 8 weeks ( RR = 2.45, 95% CI: 1.70–3.63). Its cumulative effect was strongest over lags 5–8 weeks, peaking at approximately 32 °C. Pmean showed a nonlinear association, peaking at 4 mm/day at a lag of 4 weeks ( RR = 1.92, 95% CI: 1.59–2.31), with significant cumulative effect across both the 1–4- and 5–8-week windows. Low WSmean and moderate DTRmean showed their strongest cumulative associations over lags 1–4 weeks, peaking at 2.0 m/s and 6.1 °C, respectively. These harmful associations attenuated over lags 5–8 weeks, whereas higher WSmean and DTRmean were generally protective. The ERPI predicted local dengue occurrence within the subsequent 1–4-week windows, with AUCs of 0.762–0.843. Across these windows, sensitivities were 0.768–0.797, specificities were 0.644–0.708, PPVs were 0.468–0.624, and NPVs were 0.822–0.911. Conclusion In Hainan, high temperature was primarily associated with medium-to-long-term dengue risk, whereas low-to-moderate diurnal temperature range increased short-term risk. Precipitation was associated with risk across both short and medium-to-long lags, while cumulative wind-speed associations were strongest over shorter lags and attenuated thereafter. The DLNM-based early risk prediction index may support meteorology-informed early warning and dengue control in tropical settings, although requiring external validation is needed before operational implementation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate how meteorological factors influence dengue transmission through nonlinear and lagged effects, addressing the need for understanding these dynamics to improve early warning systems.
Design and population
The study analyzed weekly dengue case and meteorological data from Hainan Province in China between 2018–2019 and 2023–2024, focusing on the associations between weather variables and dengue risk.
Core methods
Distributed lag non-linear models (DLNMs) were used to analyze the data, and an early risk prediction index (ERPI) was constructed from cumulative meteorological risk signals and imported cases.
Main results
Among 927 dengue cases reported during the four modelled years, 727 (78.43%) were locally acquired. Weekly mean daily maximum temperature (Tmax), precipitation (Pmean), wind speed (WSmean), and diurnal temperature range (DTRmean) showed nonlinear, lag-dependent associations with dengue risk.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that high temperature was primarily associated with medium-to-long-term dengue risk, while low-to-moderate diurnal temperature range increased short-term risk, highlighting the complex relationship between climate factors and dengue transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that integrating meteorological data into early warning systems could enhance dengue control in tropical regions, providing a basis for targeted public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on observational data and require external validation before operational implementation, indicating moderate confidence in the predictive model's utility.

应用 AI 深度学习模型使用完整的 基因组 数据对 登革病毒 血清型进行实时预测和分类

Applying AI Embodied Deep Learning Models for Real‐Time Prediction and Classification of Dengue Virus Serotypes Using Complete Genome Data
作者： N. Sivaranjani, N. Nithiyanandam, V. Manickavasagam, G.K. Sandhia, S. Nikkath Bushra
摘要中文翻译本研究专为 登革病毒 (DE VI) 的血清型分类和序列生成而设计,使用整个 基因组 数据,提出了一种人工智能体现的深度学习系统 AIemDL DVE。我们使用处理后的 2000 个 基因组 序列构建了统一的数据集,并构建了一个基于 LSTM 的生成模型,能够识别 DE VI 序列中的不同特征。GRU、一维卷积分析(1D-Con)和简单 RNN 模型的比较分析发现,LSTM 模型以很高的精度生成了超过 10 kb 的序列,从而揭示了其性能的改进。通过前馈神经网络 (FNN),我们分类的 F1 分数为 99%,验证准确度为 98.12%。此外,主题分析使用 MEME Suite 强调了保守的遗传成分,从而增强了模型的可解释性。尽管它与类似大小的 基因组 最相关,但我们的 AIemDL DVE 方法提供了可靠的预测,可能有助于 DE VI 相关的 疫苗 研究和诊断。这项工作展示了人工智能如何有效地体现深度学习可以改善病毒序列分类并支持病毒 进化 和传播的知识。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发一种人工智能深度学习系统,使用完整的 基因组 数据对 登革病毒 (DE VI) 血清型进行实时预测和分类,解决病毒序列分析和理解病毒 进化 的改进方法的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用处理过的 2000 个 基因组 的 基因组 序列构建统一的数据集,并构建基于 LSTM 的生成模型来识别这些序列中的不同特征。
核心方法
研究人员使用前馈神经网络 (FNN) 进行分类,并比较了不同的模型,包括 GRU、一维卷积分析 (1D-Con) 和简单 RNN,以评估基于序列生成和分类准确性的性能。
主要结果
LSTM 模型以极高的精度生成了超过 10 kb 的序列,FNN 在对 登革病毒 序列进行分类时实现了 99% 的 F1 分数和 98.12% 的验证准确率。
解释与创新
该研究强调了 AIemDL DVE 方法通过基序分析增强的可解释性,该方法使用 MEME Suite 识别保守的遗传成分,为病毒序列分类提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,AIemDL DVE 方法可以支持 疫苗 对 进化 的研究和诊断,提供可靠的预测,可能有助于了解病毒 进化 和传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于 2000 个序列的单一数据集,这可能会限制普遍性,而且证据仅限于抽象级别的报告,导致研究结果的可信度中等。

Applying AI Embodied Deep Learning Models for Real‐Time Prediction and Classification of Dengue Virus Serotypes Using Complete Genome Data

Authors: N. Sivaranjani, N. Nithiyanandam, V. Manickavasagam, G.K. Sandhia, S. Nikkath Bushra
Original abstract (English)
Designed for the serotype categorization and sequence generation of Dengue Virus (DE VI ), using whole genome data, this study presents an AI embodied deep learning system, AIemDL DVE . We constructed unified datasets using processed 2000 genome sequences and built an LSTM-based generative model capable of identifying distinct characteristics in DE VI sequences. Comparative analysis of GRU, 1 Dimensional convolutional analysis (1D-Con), and Simple RNN models found that the LSTM model produced sequences surpassing 10 kb with great precision, therefore revealing its improved performance. With a feed-forward neural network (FNN), we categorized with an F1 score of 99% and validation accuracy of 98.12%. Moreover, motif analysis underscored conserved genetic components using the MEME Suite highlighted, which enhances model interpretability. Although it is most pertinent to similar-sized genomes, our AIemDL DVE method provides reliable predictions that might help DE VI -related vaccine research and diagnostics. This work demonstrates how effectively AI embodied deep learning may improve viral sequence classification and support knowledge of viral evolution and spread.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to develop an AI embodied deep learning system for real-time prediction and classification of Dengue Virus (DE VI) serotypes using complete genome data, addressing the need for improved methods in viral sequence analysis and understanding of viral evolution.
Design and population
The study involved constructing unified datasets using processed 2000 genome sequences of Dengue Virus and building an LSTM-based generative model to identify distinct characteristics in these sequences.
Core methods
The researchers used a feed-forward neural network (FNN) for categorization and compared different models, including GRU, 1 Dimensional convolutional analysis (1D-Con), and Simple RNN, to evaluate performance based on sequence generation and classification accuracy.
Main results
The LSTM model generated sequences surpassing 10 kb with great precision, and the FNN achieved an F1 score of 99% and validation accuracy of 98.12% in categorizing Dengue Virus sequences.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the enhanced interpretability of the AIemDL DVE method through motif analysis, which identified conserved genetic components using the MEME Suite, offering a novel approach to viral sequence classification.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the AIemDL DVE method could support vaccine research and diagnostics for Dengue Virus, providing reliable predictions that may aid in understanding viral evolution and spread.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on a single dataset of 2000 sequences, which may limit generalizability, and the evidence is limited to abstract-level reporting, resulting in moderate confidence in the findings.

气候变化对登革热的影响。

Impacts of climate change on dengue.
作者： Erin Mordecai, Mordecai E., Erin A. Mordecai
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了气候变化为何导致登革热和其他蚊媒疾病激增,并确定了理解气候和社会经济因素如何相互作用以改变登革热 传播 动态的关键知识差距。
设计与对象
该论文是一篇叙述性综述,综合了气候变化对登革热影响的证据,利用全球研究和荟萃分析来评估 传播 模式和风险因素。
核心方法
该综述采用荟萃分析方法来评估温度对登革热 发病率 的影响,整合气候和社会经济中介因素来模拟非线性关系。
主要结果
该评论报告称,气候变暖正在扩大美洲和亚洲的登革热负担,温度对登革热的影响是非线性的,并受到气候和社会经济因素的调节。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为气候变化正在通过复杂的相互作用重塑登革热 流行病学 的证据,强调需要采取综合气候适应策略来缓解 传播 的影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了加强 媒介 控制、监测 和气候信息疾病负担预测的紧迫性,以增强气候变化中登革热的抵御能力。
局限与证据强度
该审查依赖于综合证据而不是原始数据,限制了因果推断和普遍性;由于纳入研究的异质性和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Impacts of climate change on dengue.

Authors: Erin Mordecai, Mordecai E., Erin A. Mordecai
Original abstract (English)
Combined effects of hydrometeorological hazards and urbanisation on dengue risk in Brazil: a spatiotemporal modelling study. Climate-sensitive disease outbreaks in the aftermath of extreme climatic events: a scoping review. The impact of climate change on transmission season length: West Nile virus as a case study. A scoping review of mosquito vector range shifts: widespread expansions and evidence gaps in climate attribution. Climate change, extreme weather and socioeconomic change drive surges in dengue and other mosquito-borne infectious diseases. Strengthening dengue resilience in a changing climate requires improved vector control, surveillance, climate-informed disease burden projections, sustainable urban and environmental management, and stronger community engagement. Partnerships that span health, environment, climate, economic and community sectors are critical for creating climate-resilient systems that improve health, environment and well-being. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study examines why climate change is driving surges in dengue and other mosquito-borne diseases, identifying a critical knowledge gap in understanding how climatic and socioeconomic factors interact to alter dengue transmission dynamics.
Design and population
The paper is a narrative review synthesizing evidence on climate change impacts on dengue, drawing on global studies and meta-analyses to assess transmission patterns and risk factors.
Core methods
The review employs a meta-analysis approach to evaluate temperature impacts on dengue incidence, integrating climatic and socioeconomic mediators to model nonlinear relationships.
Main results
The review reports that climate warming is expanding the dengue burden in the Americas and Asia, with temperature effects on dengue incidence being nonlinear and mediated by climatic and socioeconomic factors.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that climate change is reshaping dengue epidemiology through complex interactions, highlighting the need for integrated climate-resilient strategies to mitigate transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the urgency of strengthening vector control, surveillance, and climate-informed disease burden projections to build dengue resilience in a changing climate.
Limitations and evidence strength
The review relies on synthesized evidence rather than primary data, limiting causal inference and generalisability; the strength of evidence is moderate due to heterogeneity in included studies and potential confounding.

Tinjauan Pelaksanaan 监测登革出血热 Rawat Inap di Rumah Sakit Marinir Cilandak

Tinjauan Pelaksanaan Surveilans Dengue Haemorrhagic Fever Rawat Inap di Rumah Sakit Marinir Cilandak
作者： Gissela Vaisky
摘要中文翻译2022年,印度尼西亚共记录了131,265例登革出血热(DHF)病例,其中40%发生在0-14岁的儿童中。2022 年 12 月在 Cilandak 海洋医院进行的初步观察显示,虽然 监测 每天都进行,但经常出现延误。数据收集报告滞后的原因是人力资源有限,因为只有一名经常被指派执行外部职责的专职工作人员。本研究采用定性描述方法来说明 DHF 监测 系统在 Cilandak 海洋医院的实施情况。观察和与病历官员访谈的结果表明,DHF 监测 没有标准操作程序 (SOP)。关于住院 DHF 患者报告及时性的数据分析显示,3 月份的报告及时率仅为 7.6%(共 13 名患者),而 10 月份为 50%(共 4 名患者)。此外,2023 年 3 月活跃医院 监测 的日出勤率仅为 3.3%,10 月为 6.5%,表明 监测 绩效指标的遵守率较低。数据收集和提交的及时性对于预防登革热 传播 广泛传播和 暴发(异常事件)的发生至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估医院中登革热出血热 (DHF) 监测 的实施情况,以解决登革热疾病的公共卫生问题,登革热疾病是发病率和 监测 的重要原因,特别是在儿童中。
设计与对象
该研究于 2022 年 12 月和 2023 年 3 月/10 月在 Cilandak 海洋医院进行,重点关注住院 DHF 患者以及那里实施的日常 监测 实践。
核心方法
该研究采用定性描述方法,包括观察和采访病历官员,以评估 DHF 监测 的实施情况,并确定数据收集和报告方面的挑战。
主要结果
初步观察显示,监测 经常延误,且没有针对 DHF 监测 的标准操作程序 (SOP)。数据分析显示,3 月份(13 名患者)的报告及时率为 7.6%,10 月份(4 名患者)的报告及时率为 50%。
解释与创新
调查结果强调了缺乏标准化程序以及对 监测 绩效指标的遵守率低,这表明需要改进系统以确保及时的数据收集和报告。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了及时收集和提交数据对于早期预警和 监测 预防的重要性,这可以为登革热流行地区更好的 监测 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于观察和访谈,这可能会引入偏差。证据是中等的,因为它提供了对 监测 实践的见解,但缺乏更广泛影响的定量数据。

Tinjauan Pelaksanaan Surveilans Dengue Haemorrhagic Fever Rawat Inap di Rumah Sakit Marinir Cilandak

Authors: Gissela Vaisky
Original abstract (English)
In 2022, Indonesia recorded a total of 131,265 Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) cases, with 40% occurring in children aged 0-14 years. Preliminary observations conducted in December 2022 at Cilandak Marine Hospital revealed that while surveillance was performed daily, it was frequently delayed. Reporting lags in data collection were attributed to limited human resources, as there was only one dedicated staff member who was frequently assigned to external duties. This study employs a qualitative descriptive method to illustrate the implementation of the DHF surveillance system at Cilandak Marine Hospital. Results from observations and interviews with medical record officers indicate that there is no Standard Operating Procedure (SOP) for DHF surveillance. Data analysis regarding the timeliness of reporting for hospitalized DHF patients showed a rate of only 7.6% (out of 13 patients) in March, compared to 50% (out of 4 patients) in October. Furthermore, daily attendance for active hospital surveillance was recorded at only 3.3% in March 2023 and 6.5% in October, indicating low compliance with surveillance performance indicators. Timeliness in data collection and submission is critical as an early warning indicator to prevent the widespread transmission of dengue and the occurrence of outbreaks (Extraordinary Events).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the implementation of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) surveillance in hospitals to address the public health problem of dengue disease, which is a significant cause of morbidity and mortality, particularly in children.
Design and population
The study was conducted at Cilandak Marine Hospital in December 2022 and March/October 2023, focusing on hospitalized DHF patients and the daily surveillance practices implemented there.
Core methods
The study used a qualitative descriptive method involving observations and interviews with medical record officers to assess the implementation of DHF surveillance and identify challenges in data collection and reporting.
Main results
Preliminary observations revealed that surveillance was frequently delayed, with no Standard Operating Procedure (SOP) for DHF surveillance. Data analysis showed a reporting timeliness rate of 7.6% in March (out of 13 patients) and 50% in October (out of 4 patients).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the lack of standardized procedures and low compliance with surveillance performance indicators, indicating a need for improved systems to ensure timely data collection and reporting.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results emphasize the importance of timely data collection and submission for early warning and outbreak prevention, which can inform better surveillance strategies in dengue-endemic areas.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to observations and interviews, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate as it provides insights into surveillance practices but lacks quantitative data on broader impacts.
