登革热 恢复期间血小板指数的纵向变化:一项前瞻性观察研究

Longitudinal changes in platelet indices during recovery from dengue fever: A prospective observational study
作者： Aishwarya G, Roopa KN, Yashashwini A, Nischita B
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热 由黄病毒引起,是热带地区的一个主要健康问题。严重感染表现为血小板减少、血液浓缩和出血表现。血小板指数,包括平均血小板体积(MPV)、血小板分布宽度(PDW)、血小板压积(PCT)、血小板大细胞计数(P-LCC)和血小板大细胞比率(P-LCR),已被提议作为登革热疾病活动的潜在标志物;然而,它们的纵向行为仍然不确定。目的和目的:本研究旨在确定和评估不同血小板指数(MPV、PDW-标准差、PDW-变异系数、P-LCC、P-LCR)在 登革热 中的作用,并确定 登革热 的临床病程和结果与血小板指数之间的相关性。材料和方法:这项前瞻性研究纳入了 53 名血清学确诊的登革热患者,历时 6 个月。收集临床数据。每隔 12 小时测量一次血小板计数、MPV、PDW、PCT、P-LCC、P-LCR 和血细胞比容 (HCT)。重复测量分析和线性混合效应模型评估了纵向趋势。逻辑回归和受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线分析评估了中度/不良临床状况的关联性和预测性能。结果:该研究包括 42 名男性(79.2%),11 名女性(20.8%),平均年龄 32.1 岁。根据世界卫生组织 2009 年的分类,大多数病例都是没有警告信号的登革热病例。住院期间血小板计数增加(P<0.001)。MPV 随着时间的推移呈上升趋势(β=0.542 fL/天,P<0.001)。较高的 MPV 与中等/较差临床状况的几率较低相关(优势比 0.68,P=0.017)。然而,ROC 分析显示独立判别能力有限(曲线下面积 = 0.33)。PDW、P-LCC 和 P-LCR 显示恢复期增加,而 HCT 下降。结论:血小板指数在登革热恢复过程中表现出显着的动态变化,可作为血液再生的辅助标志物。然而,它们对临床严重程度的独立预测准确性需要在更大的 队列研究 中进行验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 由黄病毒引起,是热带地区的一个主要健康问题。严重感染表现为血小板减少、血液浓缩和出血表现。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性观察性研究纳入了 53 名血清学确诊的登革热患者,历时 6 个月。该研究包括 42 名男性(79.2%)、11 名女性(20.8%),平均年龄为 32.1 岁。根据世界卫生组织 2009 年的分类,大多数病例都是没有警告信号的登革热病例。
核心方法
收集临床数据。每隔 12 小时测量一次血小板计数、MPV、PDW、PCT、P-LCC、P-LCR 和血细胞比容 (HCT)。重复测量分析和线性混合效应模型评估了纵向趋势。逻辑回归和受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线分析评估了中度/不良临床状况的关联性和预测性能。
主要结果
住院期间血小板计数增加(P<0.001)。MPV 随着时间的推移呈上升趋势(β=0.542 fL/天,P<0.001)。较高的 MPV 与中等/较差临床状况的几率较低相关(优势比 0.68,P=0.017)。然而,ROC 分析显示独立判别能力有限(曲线下面积 = 0.33)。PDW、P-LCC 和 P-LCR 显示恢复期增加,而 HCT 下降。
解释与创新
血小板指数显示出登革热恢复期间显着的动态变化,并且可以作为血液再生的辅助标志物。它们对临床严重程度的独立预测准确性需要在更大的 队列研究 中进行验证。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于使用血小板指数作为辅助标记物来监测登革热恢复期间的血液再生。他们强调需要进一步验证这些指标以预测临床严重程度。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量有限,且 ROC 分析的曲线下面积较低,表明证据是中等的。

Longitudinal changes in platelet indices during recovery from dengue fever: A prospective observational study

Authors: Aishwarya G, Roopa KN, Yashashwini A, Nischita B
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue fever, caused by the flavivirus, is a major health problem in tropical regions. Severe infection presents with thrombocytopenia, hemoconcentration, and bleeding manifestations. Platelet indices, including mean platelet volume (MPV), platelet distribution width (PDW), plateletcrit (PCT), platelet large cell count (P-LCC), and platelet large cell ratio (P-LCR), have been proposed as potential markers of disease activity in dengue fever; however, their longitudinal behavior remains uncertain. Aims and Objectives: This study was undertaken to determine and assess the role of different platelet indices (MPV, PDW-standard deviation, PDW-coefficient of variation, P-LCC, P-LCR) in dengue fever and to determine the correlation between the clinical course and outcome of dengue fever with the platelet indices. Materials and Methods: This prospective study included 53 serologically confirmed dengue patients over 6 months. Clinical data were collected. Platelet count, MPV, PDW, PCT, P-LCC, P-LCR, and hematocrit (HCT) were measured at 12-h intervals. Repeated measures analysis and linear mixed-effects modeling evaluated longitudinal trends. Logistic regression and receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve analysis assessed the association and predictive performance for moderate/poor clinical conditions. Results: The study included 42 males (79.2%), 11 females (20.8%), with mean age of 32.1 years. Most cases were of dengue without warning signs as per the World Health Organization 2009 classification. Platelet counts increased during hospitalization (P<0.001). MPV demonstrated an upward trend over time (β=0.542 fL/day, P<0.001). Higher MPV was associated with lower odds of moderate/poor clinical condition (odds ratio 0.68, P=0.017). However, ROC analysis showed limited standalone discriminative ability (area under the curve=0.33). PDW, P-LCC, and P-LCR showed recovery-phase increases, while HCT declined. Conclusion: Platelet indices demonstrate significant dynamic changes during recovery from dengue and may serve as adjunct markers of hematological regeneration. However, their independent predictive accuracy for clinical severity requires validation in larger cohorts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever, caused by the flavivirus, is a major health problem in tropical regions. Severe infection presents with thrombocytopenia, hemoconcentration, and bleeding manifestations.
Design and population
This prospective observational study included 53 serologically confirmed dengue patients over 6 months. The study included 42 males (79.2%), 11 females (20.8%), with mean age of 32.1 years. Most cases were of dengue without warning signs as per the World Health Organization 2009 classification.
Core methods
Clinical data were collected. Platelet count, MPV, PDW, PCT, P-LCC, P-LCR, and hematocrit (HCT) were measured at 12-h intervals. Repeated measures analysis and linear mixed-effects modeling evaluated longitudinal trends. Logistic regression and receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve analysis assessed the association and predictive performance for moderate/poor clinical conditions.
Main results
Platelet counts increased during hospitalization (P<0.001). MPV demonstrated an upward trend over time (β=0.542 fL/day, P<0.001). Higher MPV was associated with lower odds of moderate/poor clinical condition (odds ratio 0.68, P=0.017). However, ROC analysis showed limited standalone discriminative ability (area under the curve=0.33). PDW, P-LCC, and P-LCR showed recovery-phase increases, while HCT declined.
Interpretation and novelty
Platelet indices demonstrate significant dynamic changes during recovery from dengue and may serve as adjunct markers of hematological regeneration. Their independent predictive accuracy for clinical severity requires validation in larger cohorts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the use of platelet indices as adjunct markers for monitoring hematological regeneration during dengue recovery. They highlight the need for further validation of these indices for predicting clinical severity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limited sample size and the low area under the curve from ROC analysis suggest that the evidence is moderate.

导致登革热蚊子基因分化的是地理因素,而不是气候变化

Geography, not climate change, makes dengue mosquitoes diverge genetically
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译差异影响了它们传播疾病或抵抗杀虫剂的能力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查导致登革热蚊子遗传分化的因素,重点关注地理因素而不是气候变化作为主要影响因素。
设计与对象
该研究分析了不同地理区域登革热蚊子的遗传数据,以确定地理在其分化中的作用。
核心方法
研究人员比较了不同地点的登革热蚊子的基因序列,以评估差异模式,并评估地理因素对这些差异的影响。
主要结果
研究发现,登革热蚊子之间的遗传差异与地理位置而非气候变化显着相关。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证据,表明地理而非气候变化是登革热蚊子遗传分化的关键驱动因素,为这些 进化 的 进化 提供了新的视角。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,了解地理因素对于有效的登革热 监测 和控制策略至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的证据,其结论基于遗传比较,没有详细的生态或流行病学数据。

Geography, not climate change, makes dengue mosquitoes diverge genetically

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
Differences influence their ability to spread disease or resist insecticides.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the factors driving genetic divergence among dengue mosquitoes, with a focus on geography rather than climate change as the primary influence.
Design and population
The study analyzed genetic data from dengue mosquitoes across different geographic regions to determine the role of geography in their divergence.
Core methods
Researchers compared genetic sequences of dengue mosquitoes from various locations to assess divergence patterns and evaluated the impact of geographic factors on these differences.
Main results
The study found that genetic divergence among dengue mosquitoes was significantly associated with geographic location rather than climate change.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as evidence that geography, not climate change, is the key driver of genetic divergence in dengue mosquitoes, offering a novel perspective on the evolution of these vectors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that understanding geographic factors is crucial for effective dengue surveillance and control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence, and its conclusions are based on genetic comparisons without detailed ecological or epidemiological data.

蛋白尿和尿蛋白肌酐比值作为预测儿科疾病严重程度和不良结果工具的研究 登革热

A study of proteinuria and urine protein-creatinine ratio as a tool in predicting disease severity and adverse outcome in paediatric dengue fever
作者： Arfa Mohammadi, Mallikarjun Ravi Kobal, Shruthi M. S, Rohini Patil, Sharankumar Deshmukh, Mohd Faisal Zoheb, M S Shruthi
摘要中文翻译目的:登革热 具有广泛的临床表现,从轻度发热性疾病到登革出血热和登革休克综合征等严重形式。早期识别更有可能发展为严重疾病的患者对于及时干预和适当的管理至关重要。在 重症登革热 中观察到显着的蛋白尿,这可能作为疾病早期阶段的有用标志。因此,本研究旨在评估蛋白尿和尿蛋白肌酐比值(UPCR)作为预测儿童登革热患者登革热严重程度和不良后果的指标。材料和方法:获得机构伦理委员会的批准。获得了参与受试者父母的知情同意。我们对三级医疗中心住院部的 90 名登革热 血清学 阳性儿科患者进行了一项前瞻性观察研究。在临床怀疑登革热的基础上,进行了登革热血清学(非结构蛋白1抗原和免疫球蛋白M抗体)和全血细胞计数。连续 5 天每天测量蛋白尿和 UPCR,以及患者生命体征。将 UPCR 峰值值与无警告信号、有警告信号和 重症登革热 的登革热患者进行比较。结果:该研究共纳入了 90 名年龄在 1 个月至 18 岁之间、经血清学确诊患有登革热的儿童。平均年龄为 7.9 ± 4.5 岁,年龄和疾病严重程度之间没有发现显着关系 (p = 0.698)。男性占病例的 55.6%,性别与严重程度无显着相关性 (p = 0.479)。根据世界卫生组织的分类,47.78%的病例被归类为A类,24.44%被归类为B类,27.78%被归类为C类。38.89%的儿童不存在蛋白尿,并且在A类中更常见,而较高级别(2+和3+)主要在C类中出现,与疾病严重程度呈显着正相关(r = 0.35,p = 0.001)。UPCR 平均峰值在患病第 4.04 ± 1.34 天记录。UPCR 水平升高 (>3) 与警告信号、低血压、出血表现、正性肌力支持需求、儿科重症监护病房入院和 死亡率 显着相关 (p < 0.001)。总体而言,UPCR 水平升高与 重症登革热 和较差的临床结果密切相关。结论:蛋白尿和 UPCR 升高与 重症登革热 和儿童不良临床结果显着相关。重症患者和预后较差的患者的 UPCR 峰值和蛋白尿值较高。尿蛋白评估,尤其是 UPCR,是确定登革热儿童疾病严重程度的重要标志。蛋白尿和 UPCR 都是简单且无创的参数,可能有助于早期风险分层;然而,还需要进一步的大规模研究来验证其预测效用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 具有广泛的临床表现,从轻度发热性疾病到登革出血热和登革休克综合征等严重形式。早期识别更有可能发展为严重疾病的患者对于及时干预和适当的管理至关重要。
设计与对象
我们对三级医疗中心住院部的 90 名登革热 血清学 阳性儿科患者进行了一项前瞻性观察研究。该研究共纳入了 90 名年龄在 1 个月至 18 岁之间、经血清学证实患有登革热的儿童。
核心方法
在临床怀疑登革热的基础上,进行了登革热血清学(非结构蛋白1抗原和免疫球蛋白M抗体)和全血细胞计数。连续 5 天每天测量蛋白尿和 UPCR,以及患者生命体征。
主要结果
38.89% 的儿童不存在蛋白尿,并且在 A 类中更为常见,而较高级别(2+ 和 3+)主要在 C 类中出现,与疾病严重程度呈显着正相关(r = 0.35,p = 0.001)。UPCR 平均峰值在患病第 4.04 ± 1.34 天记录。UPCR 水平升高 (>3) 与警告信号、低血压、出血表现、正性肌力支持需求、儿科重症监护病房入院和 死亡率 显着相关 (p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
重症患者和预后较差的患者的 UPCR 峰值和蛋白尿值较高。尿蛋白评估,尤其是 UPCR,是确定登革热儿童疾病严重程度的重要标志。
科研与公卫意义
蛋白尿和 UPCR 升高与 重症登革热 和儿童不良临床结果显着相关。蛋白尿和 UPCR 都是简单且无创的参数,可能有助于早期风险分层。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。由于该研究基于明确的人群和明确的统计关联,因此研究结果的可信度很高。

A study of proteinuria and urine protein-creatinine ratio as a tool in predicting disease severity and adverse outcome in paediatric dengue fever

Authors: Arfa Mohammadi, Mallikarjun Ravi Kobal, Shruthi M. S, Rohini Patil, Sharankumar Deshmukh, Mohd Faisal Zoheb, M S Shruthi
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: Dengue fever presents with a wide spectrum of clinical manifestations, which range from mild febrile illness to severe forms such as dengue haemorrhagic fever and dengue shock syndrome. Early identification of patients who are more likely to develop severe disease is essential for timely intervention and appropriate management. Significant proteinuria has been observed in severe dengue, and this may serve as a useful marker during the early phase of illness. Therefore, the present study aims to evaluate proteinuria and urine protein-creatinine ratio (UPCR) as indicators for predicting the severity of dengue and adverse outcomes in paediatric dengue patients. Material and Methods: Approval from the institutional ethics committee was obtained. Informed consent from the parents of participating subjects was obtained. We conducted a prospective observational study among 90 paediatric patients with positive dengue serology in the inpatient department of a tertiary care centre. On the basis of clinical suspicion of dengue, dengue serology (non-structural protein 1 antigen and immunoglobulin M antibody) and complete blood count were performed. Proteinuria and UPCR were measured daily for 5 days, along with patient vitals. Peak UPCR values were compared with dengue patients without warning signs, with warning signs and severe dengue. Results: A total of 90 children aged between 1 month and 18 years with serologically confirmed dengue were included in the study. The mean age was 7.9 ± 4.5 years, and no significant relationship was found between age and disease severity ( p = 0.698). Males accounted for 55.6% of the cases, and gender was not significantly associated with severity ( p = 0.479). Based on World Health Organization classification, 47.78% of cases were categorised as Category A, 24.44% as Category B and 27.78% as Category C. Proteinuria was absent in 38.89% of children and was more commonly observed in Category A, whereas higher grades (2+ and 3+) were predominantly seen in Category C, showing a significant positive correlation with disease severity (r = 0.35, p = 0.001). The mean peak UPCR was recorded on day 4.04 ± 1.34 of illness. Elevated UPCR levels (>3) were significantly associated with warning signs, hypotension, bleeding manifestations, need for inotropic support, paediatric intensive care unit admission and mortality ( p < 0.001). Overall, increasing UPCR levels were strongly linked to severe dengue and poorer clinical outcomes. Conclusion: Proteinuria and elevated UPCR are significantly associated with severe dengue and adverse clinical outcomes in children. Higher values of peak UPCR and proteinuria were seen in patients in the severe category and those with poorer outcomes. Assessment of urinary protein, especially the UPCR, serves as a valuable marker for determining disease severity in children with dengue. Both proteinuria and UPCR are simple and noninvasive parameters and may aid in early risk stratification; however, further large-scale studies are required to validate their predictive utility.

Question and background
Dengue fever presents with a wide spectrum of clinical manifestations, which range from mild febrile illness to severe forms such as dengue haemorrhagic fever and dengue shock syndrome. The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study. The confidence in the findings is high as the study is based on a well-defined population and clear statistical associations.

全面的保守性分析可实现强大的多重 RT-qPCR 检测,可同时进行 登革病毒 的泛检测和血清型区分

Comprehensive conservation profiling enables a robust multiplex RT-qPCR assay for simultaneous pan-detection and serotype discrimination of dengue virus
作者： Bohan Xu, Dongyan Xiong, Jinzhang Wang, Chunjing Zhao, Junhua Li, Hongping Wei, Junping Yu
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒 (DENV) 仍然是一个严重的全球健康威胁,需要快速的泛检测和精确的血清分型。在本研究中,我们开发了一种多重实时 RT-qPCR 检测方法,用于同时检测和鉴定 DENV1-4。在对 4,754 个序列进行系统性基因组保守性分析的指导下,我们针对高度保守的 5' 非翻译区 (5' UTR) 设计了一套泛 DENV 和四种血清型特异性引物探针组。该检测显示出强大的分析性能,检测限 (LOD) 范围为 15.8 至 1.58 × 10 3 拷贝/mL,并且使用特定引物-探针组观察到血清型 I 的最低 LOD(15.8 拷贝/mL)。与正黄病毒或甲病毒属病原体的其他常见病原体没有交叉反应,并且使用非登革热样本的阴性 队列研究 进一步验证临床特异性为 100%。使用 53 个临床 DENV 阳性样本进行的验证显示,与血清型鉴定的参考诊断试剂盒 100% 一致。虽然泛 DENV 组作为具有广泛反应性的高特异性筛查工具,但其临床敏感性为 88.7%,特别是缺失低滴度样本 (Ct > 30)。这种差异,加上血清型特异性组的卓越灵敏度,表明急性期血清中存在不完整的基因组片段,突出了我们的 5'UTR 靶向方法在评估病毒基因组完整性方面的优势。这种双层多重检测为临床 诊断 和流行病学 监测 提供了强大、快速且经济高效的工具。重要性 登革热 是全球系统性病毒性疾病的主要原因。四种不同血清型的共同循环使临床管理变得复杂并增加了严重疾病的风险。在这里,我们提出了一种新型多重 RT-qPCR 检测,可在两个试管中同时进行泛 DENV 检测和血清型鉴定,包括六个反应,包括内部对照 (IC),从而简化登革热 诊断。一项关键创新是在大数据基因组分析的指导下,系统地选择 5'UTR 作为诊断靶点。与针对 3'UTR 的检测不同,3'UTR 可能通过捕获冗余的亚基因组正黄病毒 RNA (sfRNA) 来过度代表病毒载量,我们的 5'UTR 靶向设计提供了更严格的基因组完整性测量。此外,我们的检测的模块化性能将通过泛 DENV 集进行的广泛筛选与高灵敏度亚型相结合,提供了双层诊断框架。这项工作为及时临床干预提供了高性能工具,并增强了我们对临床标本中 DENV 片段的分子理解。

问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 仍然是一个严重的全球健康威胁,需要快速的泛检测和精确的血清分型。

在本研究中,我们开发了一种多重实时 RT-qPCR 检测方法,用于同时检测和鉴定 DENV1-4。在对 4,754 个序列进行系统性基因组保守性分析的指导下,我们针对高度保守的 5' 非翻译区 (5' UTR) 设计了一套泛 DENV 和四种血清型特异性引物探针组。

该检测显示出强大的分析性能,检测限 (LOD) 范围为 15.8 至 1.58 × 10^3 拷贝/mL,并且使用特定引物探针组观察到血清型 I 的最低 LOD(15.8 拷贝/mL)。与正黄病毒或甲病毒属病原体的其他常见病原体没有交叉反应,并且使用非登革热样本的阴性 队列研究 进一步验证临床特异性为 100%。
主要结果
使用 53 个临床 DENV 阳性样本进行的验证显示,与血清型鉴定的参考诊断试剂盒 100% 一致。

这项工作为及时临床干预提供了高性能工具,并增强了我们对临床标本中 DENV 片段的分子理解。
局限与证据强度
虽然泛 DENV 组作为具有广泛反应性的高特异性筛查工具,但其临床敏感性为 88.7%,特别是缺失低滴度样本 (Ct > 30)。这种差异,加上血清型特异性组的卓越敏感性,表明急性期血清中存在不完整的基因组片段。

Comprehensive conservation profiling enables a robust multiplex RT-qPCR assay for simultaneous pan-detection and serotype discrimination of dengue virus

Authors: Bohan Xu, Dongyan Xiong, Jinzhang Wang, Chunjing Zhao, Junhua Li, Hongping Wei, Junping Yu
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue virus (DENV) remains a critical global health threat, necessitating rapid pan-detection and precise serotyping. In this study, we developed a multiplex real-time RT-qPCR assay for simultaneous detection and identification of DENV1–4. Guided by a systematic genomic conservation analysis of 4,754 sequences, we targeted the highly conserved 5′ untranslated region (5′ UTR) to design one pan-DENV and four serotype-specific primer-probe sets. The assay demonstrated robust analytical performance, with the limit of detection (LOD) ranging from 15.8 to 1.58 × 10 3 copies/mL and the lowest LOD (15.8 copies/mL) observed for serotype I with specific primer-probe sets. There was no cross-reactivity with other common pathogens of the Orthoflavivirus or Alphavirus genus pathogens , and clinical specificity was further validated as 100% using a negative cohort of non-dengue febrile samples. Validation using 53 clinical DENV-positive samples showed 100% concordance with a reference diagnostic kit for serotype identification. While the pan-DENV set served as a high-specificity screening tool with broad reactivity, it exhibited a clinical sensitivity of 88.7%, particularly missing low-titer samples (Ct > 30). This discrepancy, coupled with the superior sensitivity of the serotype-specific sets, suggests the presence of incomplete genomic fragments in acute-phase sera, highlighting the advantage of our 5′ UTR-targeted approach in assessing viral genomic integrity. This dual-layered multiplex assay provides a robust, rapid, and cost-effective tool for clinical diagnosis and epidemiological surveillance. IMPORTANCE Dengue fever is a leading cause of systemic viral disease worldwide. Co-circulation of four distinct serotypes complicates clinical management and increases severe disease risk. Here, we presented a novel multiplex RT-qPCR assay enabling simultaneous pan-DENV detection and serotype identification in two tubes, comprising six reactions including an internal control (IC), thereby streamlining dengue diagnosis. A key innovation is the systematic selection of the 5′ UTR as the diagnostic target, guided by big-data genomic analysis. Unlike assays targeting the 3′ UTR, which may overrepresent viral loads by capturing redundant subgenomic orthoflavivirus RNA (sfRNA), our 5′ UTR-targeted design provides a more stringent measure of genomic integrity. Furthermore, the modular performance of our assay combining broad screening via a pan-DENV set with high-sensitivity subtyping which offers a dual-layer diagnostic framework. This work provides a high-performance tool for timely clinical intervention and enhances our molecular understanding of DENV fragments in clinical specimens.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

针对 SARS-CoV-2、流感、DENV-2 和 CHIKV 的新型 1,2,3-三唑-喹啉衍生物的合成和广谱 抗病毒药物 评价

Synthesis and Broad-Spectrum Antiviral Evaluation of Novel 1,2,3-Triazole–Quinoline Derivatives Targeting SARS-CoV-2, Influenza, DENV-2, and CHIKV
作者： Alcione Silva de Carvalho, Natalia Fintelman-Rodrigues, Victória Toledo Diniz de Camargo, Lina Silva-Trujillo, Caroline Souza de Freitas, Isabela Alencar Graciano, Acácio Silva de Souza, Camilla Blanco de Assis, Otávio Augusto Chaves, Fernando de Carvalho da Silva
摘要中文翻译新兴和重新出现的 RNA 病毒(包括 SARS-CoV-2、流感 A(H1N1)pdm09、DENV-2 和 CHIKV)构成的持续全球威胁凸显了对新型广谱 抗病毒药物 药物的迫切需求。基于喹啉支架已确定的 抗病毒药物 活性,本研究采用分子杂交策略设计并合成了一系列新型 1,2,3-三唑-喹啉衍生物(化合物 4a-4h)。这种方法策略性地将生物活性喹啉核与药学上有利的 1,2,3-三唑环融合。新化合物通过三步高效合成,产率中等至较高,随后确定了其结构。在体外评估了它们对四种病毒的抑制能力和相关细胞系的细胞毒性。初步筛选表明,化合物 4a-4h 具有广谱 抗病毒药物 活性,在 10 μM 浓度下对 SARS-CoV-2、DENV-2 和 CHIKV 显示出高抑制百分比。值得注意的是,分别被甲氧基和乙基取代的化合物 4c 和 4e 对 SARS-CoV-2 显示出卓越的效力和安全性,产生高选择性指数(SI:分别为 1896.5 和 1265.8)。此外,计算机吸收、分布、代谢和排泄 (ADME) 和分子对接计算表明了良好的药物相似性,包括改善的亲脂性和非 P-糖蛋白底物的可行性。这些发现验证了分子杂交策略,将这类新型化合物确立为有前景的多靶点 抗病毒药物 原型,用于针对流行病毒病原体的进一步临床前探索。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了包括 DENV-2 在内的新兴和重新出现的 RNA 病毒所构成的持续全球威胁,由于现有疗法的局限性,需要开发新型广谱 抗病毒药物 药物。
设计与对象
该研究利用分子杂交策略合成了一系列新型1,2,3-三唑-喹啉衍生物(化合物4a-4h),并评估了它们针对DENV-2和其他RNA病毒的抗病毒药物活性和体外细胞毒性。
核心方法
化合物分三步合成,具有中等到高产率,并且在体外评估了它们对 DENV-2 和其他病毒的抑制能力,同时评估了相关细胞系的细胞毒性。
主要结果
化合物 4a-4h 表现出广谱 抗病毒药物 活性,在 10 μM 时对 DENV-2 具有高抑制百分比,化合物 4c 和 4e 对 DENV-2 显示出卓越的效力和安全性,选择性指数分别为 1896.5 和 1265.8。
解释与创新
研究结果验证了分子杂交策略的有效性,确立了1,2,3-三唑-喹啉衍生物这一新型类别作为针对登革病毒2型及相关病原体进一步临床前探索的多靶点抗病毒药物原型药物的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
该研究确定了用于 DENV-2 抗病毒药物 开发的潜在先导化合物,为未来的临床前和转化研究奠定了基础,以解决登革热管理中未满足的治疗需求。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于体外结果和计算机预测,缺乏动物模型验证或临床数据,这限制了结论的强度。

Synthesis and Broad-Spectrum Antiviral Evaluation of Novel 1,2,3-Triazole–Quinoline Derivatives Targeting SARS-CoV-2, Influenza, DENV-2, and CHIKV

Authors: Alcione Silva de Carvalho, Natalia Fintelman-Rodrigues, Victória Toledo Diniz de Camargo, Lina Silva-Trujillo, Caroline Souza de Freitas, Isabela Alencar Graciano, Acácio Silva de Souza, Camilla Blanco de Assis, Otávio Augusto Chaves, Fernando de Carvalho da Silva
Original abstract (English)
The persistent global threat posed by emerging and re-emerging RNA viruses, including SARS-CoV-2, influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, DENV-2, and CHIKV, highlights the critical need for novel and broad-spectrum antiviral agents. Building upon the established antiviral activity of the quinoline scaffold, this study employed a molecular hybridization strategy to design and synthesize a novel series of 1,2,3-triazole–quinoline derivatives (compounds 4a–4h). This approach strategically fused the bioactive quinoline nucleus with the pharmaceutically favorable 1,2,3-triazole ring. The new compounds were synthesized efficiently in three steps with moderate-to-high yields, and their structures were subsequently determined. Their inhibitory capacities against the four viruses and cytotoxicity across relevant cell lines were evaluated in vitro. The initial screening demonstrated that compounds 4a–4h possessed broad-spectrum antiviral activity, showing high inhibition percentages at 10 μM against SARS-CoV-2, DENV-2, and CHIKV. Notably, compounds 4c and 4e, substituted with methoxy and ethyl groups, respectively, displayed exceptional potency and safety against SARS-CoV-2, yielding high selectivity indices (SI: 1896.5 and 1265.8, respectively). Furthermore, in silico absorption, distribution, metabolism, and excretion (ADME) and molecular docking calculations suggested favorable drug-likeness profiles, including improved lipophilicity and non-P-glycoprotein substrate feasibility. The findings validate the molecular hybridization strategy, establishing this new class of compounds as promising, multi-target antiviral prototypes for further preclinical exploration against prevalent viral pathogens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the persistent global threat posed by emerging and re-emerging RNA viruses, including DENV-2, which necessitates the development of novel broad-spectrum antiviral agents due to the limitations of existing therapies.
Design and population
The research synthesized a novel series of 1,2,3-triazole–quinoline derivatives (compounds 4a–4h) using a molecular hybridization strategy and evaluated their antiviral activity and cytotoxicity in vitro against DENV-2 and other RNA viruses.
Core methods
Compounds were synthesized in three steps with moderate-to-high yields, and their inhibitory capacities against DENV-2 and other viruses were assessed in vitro alongside cytotoxicity evaluations across relevant cell lines.
Main results
Compounds 4a–4h demonstrated broad-spectrum antiviral activity, with high inhibition percentages at 10 μM against DENV-2, and compounds 4c and 4e showed exceptional potency and safety against DENV-2, yielding selectivity indices of 1896.5 and 1265.8, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings validate the molecular hybridization strategy, establishing the new class of 1,2,3-triazole–quinoline derivatives as promising multi-target antiviral prototypes for further preclinical exploration against DENV-2 and related pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study identifies potential lead compounds for DENV-2 antiviral development, offering a foundation for future preclinical and translational research to address unmet therapeutic needs in dengue management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to in vitro results and in silico predictions, lacking animal model validation or clinical data, which constrains the strength of conclusions to moderate.

蜂毒肽和 Clinacanthus nutans 提取物的组合增强了 抗病毒药物 对抗 登革病毒 血清型 2 感染的功效

Combination of Melittin and Clinacanthus nutans Extract Enhances Antiviral Efficacy Against Dengue Virus Serotype 2 Infection
作者： Natthanich Boonsatit, Somluethai Fungfueang, Saruda Thongyim, Yingmanee Tragoolpua, Terd Disayathanoowat, George S. Baillie, Aussara Panya
摘要中文翻译由于缺乏有效的 抗病毒药物 疗法,抗病毒药物 (DENV) 仍然是全球主要的健康威胁,特别是在热带和亚热带地区。在这项研究中,我们评估了一种新的组合策略,使用蜂毒肽(一种蜂毒衍生的 抗病毒药物 肽)与 Clinacanthus nutans 提取物一起增强 抗病毒药物 功效,同时降低蜂毒肽相关的针对 抗病毒药物 血清型 2 (DENV-2) 的细胞毒性。使用细胞活力测定评估 Vero 细胞的细胞毒性,而通过焦点形成单位 (FFU) 还原测定、基于细胞的 ELISA、FFU 滴定测定和免疫荧光测定 (IFA) 评估 抗病毒药物 活性。蜂毒肽 (1.25–2.5 μg/mL) 和 C. nutans 提取物 (15.625–500 μg/mL) 将细胞活力维持在 80% 以上,而 5 μg/mL 蜂毒肽则显着降低细胞活力。值得注意的是,治疗 与 C. nutans 的共表达使细胞活力恢复到 80% 以上,表现出显着的细胞保护作用。更重要的是,治疗 组合比单独使用任一药物表现出更大的 抗病毒药物 活性,从而在较低浓度下完全抑制病毒感染,同时抑制细胞内病毒蛋白表达和子代病毒的产生。这些发现表明,在测试条件下,C. nutans 可能增强蜂毒肽的 抗病毒药物 活性,同时降低其细胞毒性。总体而言,这项研究提供了初步证据,支持这种基于天然化合物的组合策略的潜力,并值得进一步临床前研究作为抗 DENV 感染的候选 抗病毒药物 方法。

核心方法

Combination of Melittin and Clinacanthus nutans Extract Enhances Antiviral Efficacy Against Dengue Virus Serotype 2 Infection

Authors: Natthanich Boonsatit, Somluethai Fungfueang, Saruda Thongyim, Yingmanee Tragoolpua, Terd Disayathanoowat, George S. Baillie, Aussara Panya
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) remains a major global health threat, particularly in tropical and subtropical regions, due to the lack of effective antiviral therapies. In this study, we evaluated a novel combination strategy using melittin, a bee venom-derived antiviral peptide, together with Clinacanthus nutans extract to enhance antiviral efficacy while reducing melittin-associated cytotoxicity against dengue virus serotype 2 (DENV-2). Cytotoxicity was assessed in Vero cells using a cell viability assay, whereas antiviral activity was evaluated by focus-forming unit (FFU) reduction assay, cell-based ELISA, FFU titration assay, and immunofluorescence assay (IFA). Melittin (1.25–2.5 μg/mL) and C. nutans extract (15.625–500 μg/mL) maintained cell viability above 80%, whereas 5 μg/mL melittin markedly reduced cell viability. Notably, co-treatment with C. nutans restored cell viability to above 80%, demonstrating a significant cytoprotective effect. More importantly, the combination treatment exhibited greater antiviral activity than either agent alone, resulting in complete inhibition of viral infection at lower concentrations while simultaneously suppressing intracellular viral protein expression and production of progeny viruses. These findings suggest that C. nutans may enhance the antiviral activity of melittin while reducing its cytotoxicity under the conditions tested. Overall, this study provides preliminary evidence supporting the potential of this natural compound-based combination strategy and warrants further preclinical investigation as a candidate antiviral approach against DENV infection.

Dengue virus (DENV) remains a major global health threat, particularly in tropical and subtropical regions, due to the lack of effective antiviral therapies.

The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

登革热肝功能障碍的研究

A Study of Liver Dysfunction in Dengue
作者： G. Shrunal, Radha Wajapey M, Jerin Abraham Joseph
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热是一种主要的蚊媒病毒性疾病,具有广泛的临床表现,从单纯性发热到多器官受累的 重症登革热。肝功能障碍是一种常见的实验室异常,但其在预测疾病严重程度方面的作用仍不确定。目的:评估 登革热 患者的肝功能障碍,评估肝功能测试异常,并确定其与疾病严重程度、并发症和住院时间的关系。材料和方法:2017 年 9 月至 2019 年 7 月在马尼帕尔 Kasturba 医院进行了一项前瞻性观察研究。总共包括 200 名血清学确诊的登革热成年患者(NS1 抗原或 IgM 阳性)。记录临床细节和实验室参数,包括肝功能测试、全血细胞计数、凝血曲线和肾功能测试。根据 AST 和 ALT 升高情况将患者分为三组,并与 WHO 登革热严重程度分类进行比较。使用SPSS进行统计分析,p值<0.05被认为有统计学意义。结果:200 名患者中,75% 为男性,发病率 在 21-30 岁年龄组中观察到最高。84.5% 的患者存在 ALT 水平升高,而 AST 升高比 ALT 更明显。然而,AST 和 ALT 水平均未显示与 WHO 定义的登革热严重程度存在统计学显着相关性(ALT:p=0.600;AST:p=0.224)。高胆红素血症和低白蛋白血症并不常见,并且与疾病严重程度没有显着相关性。十四名患者需要重症监护,三人死亡。肝酶升高与住院时间、ICU 要求或 死亡率 没有显着相关性。结论:登革热 患者常见肝功能障碍,特别是 AST 和 ALT 水平升高。尽管转氨酶升高很常见,但它们并不能可靠地预测疾病的严重程度、住院时间、入住 ICU 或 死亡率。因此,肝功能测试应与临床结果和其他实验室参数一起解释,而不是单独用于预后评估。关键词:登革热;肝功能障碍;天冬氨酸转氨酶(AST);丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是一种主要的蚊媒病毒性疾病,具有广泛的临床谱,经常观察到肝功能障碍,但其预后价值仍不清楚。该研究旨在评估登革热的肝功能测试异常,并确定它们是否可以预测疾病的严重程度、并发症或住院时间。
设计与对象
2017 年 9 月至 2019 年 7 月期间,马尼帕尔 Kasturba 医院进行了一项前瞻性观察性研究。该研究纳入了 200 名血清学确诊的登革热(NS1 抗原或 IgM 阳性)成年患者。记录临床细节和实验室参数。
核心方法
记录肝功能测试、全血细胞计数、凝血曲线和肾功能测试。使用SPSS进行统计分析,p值<0.05被认为有统计学意义。
主要结果
84.5%的患者存在ALT升高,且AST升高比ALT更明显。AST 和 ALT 水平均未显示与 WHO 定义的登革热严重程度存在统计学显着相关性(ALT:p=0.600;AST:p=0.224)。高胆红素血症和低白蛋白血症并不常见,并且与疾病严重程度没有显着相关性。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,虽然登革热患者肝功能障碍很常见,但转氨酶升高并不能可靠地预测疾病的严重程度、住院时间、入住 ICU 或 死亡率。这对单独使用肝功能测试来评估登革热的预后提出了挑战。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,肝功能测试应与临床和其他实验室参数一起解释,而不是单独用于登革热治疗中的预后目的。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其单中心设计、缺乏对照组以及依赖世界卫生组织严重程度分类而没有额外的经过验证的预后标志物。由于这些限制以及缺乏显着关联,证据是中等的。

A Study of Liver Dysfunction in Dengue

Authors: G. Shrunal, Radha Wajapey M, Jerin Abraham Joseph
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue is a major mosquito-borne viral illness associated with a broad spectrum of clinical manifestations ranging from uncomplicated fever to severe dengue with multiorgan involvement. Liver dysfunction is a common laboratory abnormality, but its role in predicting disease severity remains uncertain. Aim: To evaluate liver dysfunction in patients with dengue fever, assess liver function test abnormalities, and determine their association with disease severity, complications, and duration of hospital stay. Materials and Methods: A prospective observational study was conducted between September 2017 and July 2019 at Kasturba Hospital, Manipal. A total of 200 adult patients with serologically confirmed dengue (NS1 antigen or IgM positive) were included. Clinical details and laboratory parameters, including liver function tests, complete blood count, coagulation profile, and renal function tests, were recorded. Patients were categorized into three groups based on AST and ALT elevations and compared with the WHO classification of dengue severity. Statistical analysis was performed using SPSS, with a p-value <0.05 considered statistically significant. Results: Among the 200 patients, 75% were males, with the highest incidence observed in the 21–30-year age group. Elevated ALT levels were present in 84.5% of patients, while AST elevation was more pronounced than ALT. However, neither AST nor ALT levels showed a statistically significant association with WHO-defined dengue severity (ALT: p=0.600; AST: p=0.224). Hyperbilirubinemia and hypoalbuminemia were uncommon and were not significantly associated with disease severity. Fourteen patients required intensive care, and three deaths were recorded. Liver enzyme elevations did not correlate significantly with hospital stay, ICU requirement, or mortality. Conclusion: Liver dysfunction, particularly elevated AST and ALT levels, is a frequent finding in dengue fever. Although transaminase elevations are common, they do not reliably predict disease severity, hospital stay, ICU admission, or mortality. Liver function tests should therefore be interpreted alongside clinical findings and other laboratory parameters rather than used alone for prognostic assessment. Keywords: Dengue fever; Liver dysfunction; Aspartate aminotransferase (AST); Alanine aminotransferase (ALT).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a major mosquito-borne viral illness with a wide clinical spectrum, and liver dysfunction is frequently observed, yet its prognostic value remains unclear. The study aimed to assess liver function test abnormalities in dengue and determine whether they predict disease severity, complications, or hospital stay.
Design and population
A prospective observational study was conducted at Kasturba Hospital, Manipal, between September 2017 and July 2019. It included 200 adult patients with serologically confirmed dengue (NS1 antigen or IgM positive). Clinical details and laboratory parameters were recorded.
Core methods
Liver function tests, complete blood count, coagulation profile, and renal function tests were recorded. Statistical analysis was performed using SPSS, with a p-value <0.05 considered statistically significant.
Main results
Elevated ALT was present in 84.5% of patients, and AST elevation was more pronounced than ALT. Neither AST nor ALT levels showed a statistically significant association with WHO-defined dengue severity (ALT: p=0.600; AST: p=0.224). Hyperbilirubinemia and hypoalbuminemia were uncommon and not significantly associated with disease severity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that while liver dysfunction is common in dengue, transaminase elevations do not reliably predict disease severity, hospital stay, ICU admission, or mortality. This challenges the use of liver function tests alone for prognostic assessment in dengue.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that liver function tests should be interpreted alongside clinical and other laboratory parameters rather than used in isolation for prognostic purposes in dengue management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-centre design, lack of a control group, and reliance on WHO severity classification without additional validated prognostic markers. The evidence is moderate due to these constraints and the absence of significant associations.

巴西需要重症监护的登革热患者的临床结果:一项为期 10 年的全国观察性研究。

Clinical outcomes of dengue patients requiring intensive care in Brazil: A 10-year nationwide observational study.
作者： Luciano Cesar Pontes Azevedo, Marina Martins Siqueira, Lucas Hernandes Correa, Melissa Tassano Pitrowsky, Lunna Perdigao Borges, Milla Fuks Salluh, Henrique Andrade R Fonseca, Marcio Soares, Jorge Ibrain Figueira Salluh
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热可能导致需要入住重症监护病房(ICU)的危重疾病。尽管巴西是全球受影响最严重的国家之一,但国家数据有限。了解临床严重程度和结果对于制定政策和做好准备至关重要。方法:我们使用来自两个全国性补充数据集(巴西 ICU 登记处 (UTIs Brasileiras) 和 DATASUS 医院信息系统)的汇总数据,对 2015 年至 2024 年巴西成人登革热 ICU 入院情况进行了回顾性研究。登记数据集包括来自公共和私人机构的临床、严重程度和器官支持变量,而 DATASUS 则捕获公共系统 (SUS) 报销的住院费用。结果是 ICU 和住院 死亡率、器官支持和住院时间。结果:我们在 ICU 登记处确定了 32,016 起登革热入院病例,在 DATASUS 中确定了 11,241 起登革热入院病例。ICU 前 24 小时的登记数据显示,5.8% 使用机械通气,7.2% 使用血管加压药。在 ICU 登记中,住院 死亡率 为 8.2%。在 DATASUS 中,住院 死亡率 为 18.6%。ICU 登记处公立医院收治的患者中,未经调整的 ICU 死亡率 为 20%,住院 死亡率 为 25%。在老年患者和使用血管加压药的患者等亚组中,未经调整的 死亡率 率较高。结论:在巴西,需要入住 ICU 的登革热与显着的死亡率相关。在老年患者和公立医院住院患者等亚组中,死亡率 率较高。需要进一步的研究来确定与结果相关的潜在可改变的、组织的和患者层面的因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热可能会导致需要入住重症监护病房 (ICU) 的危重疾病。尽管巴西是全球受影响最严重的国家之一,但国家数据有限。
设计与对象
我们使用来自两个全国性补充数据集(巴西 ICU 登记处 (UTIs Brasileiras) 和 DATASUS 医院信息系统)的汇总数据,对 2015 年至 2024 年巴西成人登革热 ICU 入院情况进行了回顾性研究。ICU 登记处公立医院收治的患者中,未经调整的 ICU 死亡率 为 20%,住院 死亡率 为 25%。
核心方法
结果是 ICU 和住院 死亡率、器官支持和住院时间。登记数据集包括来自公共和私人机构的临床、严重程度和器官支持变量,而 DATASUS 则捕获公共系统 (SUS) 报销的住院费用。
主要结果
我们在 ICU 登记处确定了 32,016 起登革热入院病例,在 DATASUS 中确定了 11,241 起登革热入院病例。ICU 前 24 小时的登记数据显示,5.8% 使用机械通气,7.2% 使用血管加压药。在 ICU 登记中,住院 死亡率 为 8.2%。在 DATASUS 中,住院 死亡率 为 18.6%。
解释与创新
在巴西,需要入住 ICU 的登革热与显着的死亡率相关。在老年患者和公立医院住院患者等亚组中,死亡率 率较高。
科研与公卫意义
了解临床严重程度和结果对于制定政策和做好准备至关重要。需要进一步的研究来确定与结果相关的潜在可改变的、组织的和患者层面的因素。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。基于全面的全国数据集和直接观察结果,人们的置信度很高。

Clinical outcomes of dengue patients requiring intensive care in Brazil: A 10-year nationwide observational study.

Authors: Luciano Cesar Pontes Azevedo, Marina Martins Siqueira, Lucas Hernandes Correa, Melissa Tassano Pitrowsky, Lunna Perdigao Borges, Milla Fuks Salluh, Henrique Andrade R Fonseca, Marcio Soares, Jorge Ibrain Figueira Salluh
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue may lead to critical illness requiring intensive care unit (ICU) admission. Even though Brazil is one of the most affected countries worldwide, national data are limited. Understanding clinical severity and outcomes is essential to inform policy and preparedness. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective study of adult dengue ICU admissions in Brazil from 2015 to 2024 using aggregated data from two nationwide complementary datasets: the Brazilian ICU registry (UTIs Brasileiras) and DATASUS Hospital Information System. The registry dataset includes clinical, severity, and organ-support variables from public and private institutions, while DATASUS captures public-system (SUS) reimbursed hospitalizations. Outcomes were ICU and in-hospital mortality, organ support, and length of stay. RESULTS: We identified 32,016 episodes of dengue admissions in the ICU registry and 11,241 in DATASUS. Data from the registry for the first 24 h of ICU shows mechanical ventilation use in 5.8% and vasopressors use in 7.2%. In-hospital mortality was 8.2% in the ICU registry. In DATASUS, in-hospital mortality was 18.6%. Patients admitted to public hospitals in the ICU registry had unadjusted ICU mortality of 20% and in-hospital mortality of 25%. Unadjusted mortality rates were higher in subgroups like older patients and those in use of vasopressors. CONCLUSION: Dengue requiring ICU admission in Brazil is associated with significant morbimortality. Mortality rates were higher in subgroups such as old patients and those admitted to public hospitals. Further studies are necessary to identify the potentially modifiable, organizational and patient-level factors associated with outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue may lead to critical illness requiring intensive care unit (ICU) admission. Even though Brazil is one of the most affected countries worldwide, national data are limited.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective study of adult dengue ICU admissions in Brazil from 2015 to 2024 using aggregated data from two nationwide complementary datasets: the Brazilian ICU registry (UTIs Brasileiras) and DATASUS Hospital Information System. Patients admitted to public hospitals in the ICU registry had unadjusted ICU mortality of 20% and in-hospital mortality of 25%.
Core methods
Outcomes were ICU and in-hospital mortality, organ support, and length of stay. The registry dataset includes clinical, severity, and organ-support variables from public and private institutions, while DATASUS captures public-system (SUS) reimbursed hospitalizations.
Main results
We identified 32,016 episodes of dengue admissions in the ICU registry and 11,241 in DATASUS. Data from the registry for the first 24 h of ICU shows mechanical ventilation use in 5.8% and vasopressors use in 7.2%. In-hospital mortality was 8.2% in the ICU registry. In DATASUS, in-hospital mortality was 18.6%.
Interpretation and novelty
Dengue requiring ICU admission in Brazil is associated with significant morbimortality. Mortality rates were higher in subgroups such as old patients and those admitted to public hospitals.
Scientific and public-health significance
Understanding clinical severity and outcomes is essential to inform policy and preparedness. Further studies are necessary to identify the potentially modifiable, organizational and patient-level factors associated with outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design. Confidence is high based on the comprehensive nationwide datasets and direct observational outcomes.

登革热 并发脑出血、急性呼吸衰竭和院内肺炎:A 病例报告

Dengue Fever Complicated by Intracerebral Haemorrhage, Acute Respiratory Failure and Nosocomial Pneumonia: A Case Report
作者： Ronak Shah, Parth R Shah, Mittal Sindhav
摘要中文翻译登革热 是一种由 登革病毒 引起的蚊媒病毒性疾病。大多数病例具有自限性病程,极少数情况下重症登革热会导致致命的神经系统并发症,如脑炎和出血。这是一名 38 岁男性的 病例报告,他出现三天高烧、一天感觉改变、头痛和全身无力。血清学检测证实登革热 IgM 呈阳性。格拉斯哥昏迷评分 (GCS) 从 15 分快速下降至 11 分,导致临床病程变得复杂,在肺部分泌物带血的情况下,需要在第二天进行气管插管。胸部高分辨率计算机断层扫描(CT)提示合并肺水肿。脑部CT显示3.9×3.8×3.5厘米的脑实质出血(IPH),周围水肿,造成明显的占位效应。实验室参数记录了严重的血小板减少症(最低 0.6 lac/cumm)、凝血病、肝细胞损伤(SGOT/SGPT 峰值为 227/276 IU/L)以及 C 反应蛋白升高(85.6 mg/L)。第五天,气管内抽吸培养物中出现了广泛耐药的鲍曼不动杆菌,需要将抗菌治疗升级为美罗培南和多粘菌素。支持性治疗包括血小板和新鲜冰冻血浆(FFP)输注。患者在第九天成功拔管,并于第十二天出院回家。值得注意的是,在免疫功能正常的年轻成人中同时发生登革热相关脑出血 (ICH)、急性低氧血症性呼吸衰竭和多重耐药 (MDR) 院内肺炎是已发表文献中很少记录的三联征。早期识别神经功能恶化、及时进行神经影像学检查、积极的血液制品支持以及针对 ICH 的保守治疗的靶向抗菌治疗仍然是良好结果的关键决定因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 是一种蚊媒病毒性疾病,病程主要为自限性,但严重病例可导致罕见但致命的神经系统并发症,如脑炎和出血。该研究强调需要记录登革热感染的非典型和严重表现,以提高临床识别和管理。
设计与对象
这是一名 38 岁男性的 病例报告,他出现三天高烧、感觉改变、头痛和全身无力。患者接受了支持治疗,包括血小板和新鲜冰冻血浆输注。
核心方法
血清学检测显示登革热 IgM 阳性,证实了登革热 诊断。支持治疗包括输血,并在整个住院期间进行临床监测。
主要结果
该患者的格拉斯哥昏迷评分从 15 分下降至 11 分,由于肺部分泌物带血,需要在第二天进行气管插管。脑部CT扫描显示3.9×3.8×3.5厘米的脑实质出血,周围水肿,造成明显的占位效应。实验室检查结果包括严重血小板减少症(最低 0.6 lac/cumm)、凝血病、肝细胞损伤(SGOT/SGPT 峰值为 227/276 IU/L)以及 C 反应蛋白升高(85.6 mg/L)。
解释与创新
在免疫功能正常的年轻成人中同时发生登革热相关脑出血、急性低氧血症性呼吸衰竭和多重耐药医院肺炎是已发表文献中很少记录的三联征。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调了早期识别神经功能恶化、及时进行神经影像学检查、积极的血液制品支持和靶向抗菌治疗在管理 重症登革热 并发症中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
作为单个 病例报告,证据的普遍性有限,无法建立因果关系。由于缺乏比较数据或更广泛的临床背景,证据强度较低。

Dengue Fever Complicated by Intracerebral Haemorrhage, Acute Respiratory Failure and Nosocomial Pneumonia: A Case Report

Authors: Ronak Shah, Parth R Shah, Mittal Sindhav
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever is a mosquito-borne viral illness caused by the dengue virus. Majority of cases have self-limiting course, rarely severe dengue causes fatal neurological complications like encephalitis and haemorrhage. This is case report of a 38-year-old male who presented with three days of high-grade fever, one day of altered sensorium, headache, and generalised weakness. Serological testing confirmed dengue IgM positivity. The clinical course was complicated by a rapid decline in Glasgow Coma Scale (GCS) from 15 to 11, necessitating endotracheal intubation on day two in the setting of blood-stained pulmonary secretions. Highresolution Computed Tomography (CT) of the thorax suggested consolidation with pulmonary oedema. CT of the brain revealed a 3.9×3.8×3.5 cm Intracerebral Parenchymal Haemorrhage (IPH) with surrounding oedema causing significant mass effect. Laboratory parameters documented severe thrombocytopaenia (nadir 0.6 lac/cumm), coagulopathy, hepatocellular injury with peak SGOT/ SGPT of 227/276 IU/L, and elevated C-reactive protein at 85.6 mg/L. Endotracheal aspirate cultures grew extensively drug-resistant Acinetobacter baumannii on day five, requiring escalation of antimicrobial therapy to meropenem and polymyxin. Supportive management included platelet and Fresh Frozen Plasma (FFP) transfusion. The patient was successfully extubated on day nine and discharged home on day twelve. Notably, the simultaneous occurrence of dengue-associated Intracerebral Haemorrhage (ICH), acute hypoxaemic respiratory failure, and Multi-Drug Resistant (MDR) nosocomial pneumonia in a young immunocompetent adult represents a triad that is rarely documented in the published literature. Early recognition of neurological deterioration, timely neuroimaging, aggressive blood product support, and targeted antimicrobial therapy with conservative management of ICH remains critical determinants of a favourable outcome.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is a mosquito-borne viral illness with a predominantly self-limiting course, but severe cases can lead to rare but fatal neurological complications such as encephalitis and haemorrhage. The study highlights the need to document atypical and severe presentations of dengue infection to improve clinical recognition and management.
Design and population
This is a case report of a 38-year-old male who presented with three days of high-grade fever, altered sensorium, headache, and generalised weakness. The patient received supportive management including platelet and Fresh Frozen Plasma transfusions.
Core methods
The diagnosis of dengue was confirmed by serological testing showing dengue IgM positivity. Supportive care included blood product transfusions, and clinical monitoring was performed throughout the hospital course.
Main results
The patient developed a Glasgow Coma Scale decline from 15 to 11, requiring endotracheal intubation on day two due to blood-stained pulmonary secretions. A CT scan of the brain revealed a 3.9×3.8×3.5 cm intracerebral parenchymal haemorrhage with surrounding oedema causing significant mass effect. Laboratory findings included severe thrombocytopaenia (nadir 0.6 lac/cumm), coagulopathy, hepatocellular injury with peak SGOT/SGPT of 227/276 IU/L, and elevated C-reactive protein at 85.6 mg/L.
Interpretation and novelty
The simultaneous occurrence of dengue-associated intracerebral haemorrhage, acute hypoxaemic respiratory failure, and multi-drug resistant nosocomial pneumonia in a young immunocompetent adult represents a rarely documented triad in the published literature.
Scientific and public-health significance
The case underscores the importance of early recognition of neurological deterioration, timely neuroimaging, aggressive blood product support, and targeted antimicrobial therapy in managing severe dengue complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a single case report, the evidence is limited in generalisability and cannot establish causality. The strength of evidence is low due to the absence of comparative data or broader clinical context.

红色微藻紫紫球藻中所有四种血清型的重组 登革病毒 C-prM-E 结构抗原的异源表达

Heterologous expression of recombinant dengue virus C-prM-E structural antigens from all four serotypes in the red microalga Porphyridium purpureum
作者： Doddy Irawan Setyo Utomo, Asep Bayu, Muhammad Luqman Ar Rasyid, Astutiati Nurhasanah, Siti Irma Rahmawati, Debora Christin Purbani, Diah Radini Noerdjito, Dwi Wahyu Indriani, Masteria Yunovilsa Putra
摘要中文翻译摘要 疫苗 (DENV) 仍然是一个重大的全球健康威胁,目前可用的 疫苗 表现出对安全性、有效性和成本效益的担忧。微藻代表了一种生产天然蛋白质的创新方法。它们被公认为安全(GRAS),易于扩展,并且能够在真核生物中诱导翻译后修饰。本研究说明了代表所有四种 DENV 血清型 (DV1-DV4) 的重组 登革病毒 衣壳-前膜-包膜 (C-prM-E) 结构抗原构建体在红色微藻紫紫球藻中通过农杆菌介导的转化进行有效异源表达。将含有带有 C 末端表位标签的衣壳前膜包膜 (CprME) 多蛋白片段的基因构建体克隆到 pCAMBIA1301 媒介 中,随后引入 P 中。PCR 扩增证实了所有四种血清型构建体成功的染色体整合,产生了预期的 2.5-kb 扩增子。SDS-PAGE 分析显示蛋白质条带约为 85 kDa,与加工后的 CprME 衍生蛋白质的预期分子量一致。我们在蛋白质印迹测定中使用了抗 DENV-2 和抗 FLAG 抗体,以证明重组抗原在所有血清型中都是相同的,并且与免疫系统相互作用。这些发现表明,P. purpureum 是表达 DENV 抗原的有效系统,为开发具有成本效益的植物源性登革热 疫苗 候选药物铺平了道路。该微藻平台可能有助于大规模生产四价登革热疫苗,特别是在登革热流行且资源稀缺的地区。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决 登革病毒 (DENV) 造成的全球健康威胁以及围绕当前 疫苗 的安全性、有效性和成本效益的担忧。该研究试图探索生产登革热病毒抗原的替代方法。
设计与对象
本研究涉及重组 DENV C-prM-E 结构抗原在红色微藻 Porphyridium purpureum 中的异源表达。该研究是利用根癌农杆菌介导的转化将基因构建体引入微藻中进行的。
核心方法
将含有带有C末端表位标签的CprME多蛋白片段的基因构建体克隆至pCAMBIA1301 媒介中并引入紫紫球藻中。PCR 扩增用于确认染色体整合,并进行 SDS-PAGE 分析以评估蛋白质表达。
主要结果
PCR 扩增证实了所有四种血清型构建体的染色体整合成功,产生了预期的 2.5-kb 扩增子。SDS-PAGE 分析显示蛋白质条带约为 85 kDa,与加工后的 CprME 衍生蛋白质的预期分子量一致。
解释与创新
结果表明,DENV 抗原在紫球藻中成功表达,这是一种生产 疫苗 候选药物的新方法。研究结果表明,该系统可用于开发具有成本效益的植物源登革热 疫苗。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果为大规模生产四价登革热疫苗提供了潜在的平台,特别是在登革热流行的资源有限地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象水平的证据,结果基于分子分析,未经临床或流行病学验证。由于缺乏详细的实验结果和实际应用数据,证据强度中等。

Heterologous expression of recombinant dengue virus C-prM-E structural antigens from all four serotypes in the red microalga Porphyridium purpureum

Authors: Doddy Irawan Setyo Utomo, Asep Bayu, Muhammad Luqman Ar Rasyid, Astutiati Nurhasanah, Siti Irma Rahmawati, Debora Christin Purbani, Diah Radini Noerdjito, Dwi Wahyu Indriani, Masteria Yunovilsa Putra
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The dengue virus (DENV) remains a significant global health threat, and the currently available vaccines exhibit concerns regarding safety, efficacy, and cost-effectiveness. Microalgae represent an innovative method for producing proteins that do not occur naturally. They are Generally Recognized As Safe (GRAS), easily scalable, and capable of inducing post-translational modifications in eukaryotes. This study illustrates the effective heterologous expression of recombinant dengue virus capsid–premembrane–envelope (C-prM-E) structural antigen constructs representing all four DENV serotypes (DV1–DV4) in the red microalga Porphyridium purpureum through Agrobacterium tumefaciens -mediated transformation. Gene constructs containing capsid-premembrane-envelope (CprME) polyprotein fragments with C-terminal epitope tags were cloned into pCAMBIA1301 vectors and subsequently introduced into P. PCR amplification confirmed the successful chromosomal integration of all four serotype constructs, yielding the anticipated 2.5-kb amplicons. The SDS-PAGE analysis revealed protein bands at around 85 kDa, consistent with the expected molecular weight of the processed CprME-derived proteins. We employed both anti-DENV-2 and anti-FLAG antibodies in a western blot assay to demonstrate that the recombinant antigens were identical and interacted with the immune system across all serotypes. These findings indicate that P. purpureum is an effective system for expressing DENV antigens, paving the path for the development of cost-effective, plant-derived dengue vaccine candidates. This microalgal platform may facilitate the large-scale production of tetravalent dengue vaccines, particularly in regions where dengue is prevalent and resources are scarce.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the global health threat posed by dengue virus (DENV) and the concerns surrounding current vaccines regarding safety, efficacy, and cost-effectiveness. The research sought to explore alternative methods for producing DENV antigens.
Design and population
This study involved the heterologous expression of recombinant DENV C-prM-E structural antigens in the red microalga Porphyridium purpureum. The research was conducted using Agrobacterium tumefaciens-mediated transformation to introduce gene constructs into the microalga.
Core methods
The gene constructs containing CprME polyprotein fragments with C-terminal epitope tags were cloned into pCAMBIA1301 vectors and introduced into Porphyridium purpureum. PCR amplification was used to confirm chromosomal integration, and SDS-PAGE analysis was performed to assess protein expression.
Main results
PCR amplification confirmed the successful chromosomal integration of all four serotype constructs, yielding the anticipated 2.5-kb amplicons. SDS-PAGE analysis revealed protein bands at around 85 kDa, consistent with the expected molecular weight of the processed CprME-derived proteins.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate the successful expression of DENV antigens in Porphyridium purpureum, which is a novel approach for producing vaccine candidates. The findings suggest that this system can be used for the development of cost-effective, plant-derived dengue vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a potential platform for the large-scale production of tetravalent dengue vaccines, particularly in resource-limited regions where dengue is prevalent.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence, and the results are based on molecular analysis without clinical or epidemiological validation. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed experimental outcomes and real-world application data.

与 重症登革热 发烧相关的预测因子、危险因素和临床特征

Predictors, Risk Factors and Clinical Features Associated with Severe Dengue Fever
作者： Amir Rashid, Amer Fakhr, Abdul Rehman Azeem, Abdul Rehman Arshad, Waqas Ahmed, Yastoor Ahmed Baig
摘要中文翻译目的:根据初始症状发作、实验室检查和体检结果,研究与 重症登革热 发热相关的预测因素、危险因素和临床特征。研究设计:横断面分析研究。研究地点和持续时间:2021 年 12 月至 2022 年 3 月,巴基斯坦巴基斯坦酋长国军事医院拉瓦尔品第登革热病房。方法:研究包括 198 例经血清学证实的住院 登革热 病例。记录患者人口统计、症状、实验室参数和疾病并发症,包括出血、胸腔积液、腹水。对正态分布的连续变量进行参数检验。对分类变量进行平均值比较。皮尔逊系数用于定量变量。p 值<0.05 被视为显着。结果:198例患者中,男性136例(68%),女性62例(31%)。常见症状为发烧 76 例(38%)、寒战 57 例(29%)、寒战 72 例(36%)、头痛 66 例(33%)和全身疼痛 56 例(28%)。疾病并发症中,出血30例(15%),胸腔积液49例(24%),腹水44例(22%)。结论:老年人、男性、血小板减少、白细胞减少、肝功能异常患者的严重程度和并发症发生率较高。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定与 重症登革热 发烧相关的预测因素、危险因素和临床特征,满足了解导致疾病严重程度的因素以改善临床管理的需要。
设计与对象
2021年12月至2022年3月,在巴基斯坦拉瓦尔品第巴基斯坦酋长国军事医院登革热病房进行了一项横断面分析研究,涉及198名血清学确诊为登革热的住院患者。
核心方法
记录患者人口统计、症状、实验室参数和疾病并发症。参数检验用于正态分布的连续变量,并且对钙分类变量进行均值比较。Pearson 系数应用于定量变量,p 值<0.05 被认为是显着的。
主要结果
198 名患者中,男性 136 名(68%),女性 62 名(31%)。常见症状包括发烧(38%)、发冷(29%)、寒战(36%)、头痛(33%)和身体疼痛(28%)。分别有 15%、24% 和 22% 的患者出现出血、胸腔积液和腹水等并发症。
解释与创新
作者解释说,老年人、男性以及患有血小板减少症、白细胞减少症和肝功能检查异常的患者发生 重症登革热 和并发症的风险较高,强调了这些因素与疾病严重程度之间的新关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为高危人群制定有针对性的 监测 和早期干预策略提供信息,从而有可能改善登革热管理中的临床结果和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横截面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差。由于该研究的观察性质和缺乏纵向随访,证据是中等的。

Predictors, Risk Factors and Clinical Features Associated with Severe Dengue Fever

Authors: Amir Rashid, Amer Fakhr, Abdul Rehman Azeem, Abdul Rehman Arshad, Waqas Ahmed, Yastoor Ahmed Baig
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To study predictors, risk factors and clinical features associated with severe Dengue fever on basis of initial symptom onset, laboratory investigations and physical findings. Study Design: Cross-Sectional Analytical Study. Place and Duration of Study: Dengue Ward, Pak Emirates Military Hospital Rawalpindi, Pakistan from Dec 2021to Mar 2022. Methodology: Study included 198 hospitalized, serologically proven cases of dengue fever. Patient demographics, symptoms, laboratory parameters and disease complications including bleeding, pleural effusion, ascites were recorded. Parametric tests were done for normally distributed continuous variables. Comparison of means was done for categorical variables. Pearson coefficient was used for quantitative variables. A p value of <0.05 was taken as significant. Results: Out of total 198 patients, 136(68%) were male and 62(31%) were female. Commonly reported symptoms were fever 76(38%), chills 57(29%), rigors 72(36%), headache 66(33%) and body aches 56(28%). Among disease complications bleeding occurred in 30(15%), pleural effusions in 49(24%), ascites in 44(22%) of patients. Conclusion: The elderly individuals, male gender, patients with thrombocytopenia, leucopenia, and abnormal liver function test showed higher severity and complication rates.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify predictors, risk factors, and clinical features associated with severe Dengue fever, addressing the need to understand factors contributing to disease severity for improved clinical management.
Design and population
A cross-sectional analytical study was conducted in the Dengue Ward of Pak Emirates Military Hospital Rawalpindi, Pakistan, from December 2021 to March 2022, involving 198 hospitalized patients with serologically confirmed dengue fever.
Core methods
Patient demographics, symptoms, laboratory parameters, and disease complications were recorded. Parametric tests were used for normally distributed continuous variables, and comparisons of means were made for categorical variables. Pearson coefficient was applied for quantitative variables, with a p value of <0.05 considered significant.
Main results
Among the 198 patients, 136 (68%) were male and 62 (31%) were female. Common symptoms included fever (38%), chills (29%), rigors (36%), headache (33%), and body aches (28%). Complications such as bleeding, pleural effusion, and ascites were reported in 15%, 24%, and 22% of patients, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that elderly individuals, males, and patients with thrombocytopenia, leucopenia, and abnormal liver function tests were at higher risk for severe dengue and complications, highlighting novel associations between these factors and disease severity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance and early intervention strategies for high-risk groups, potentially improving clinical outcomes and resource allocation in dengue management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and lack of longitudinal follow-up.

HUBUNGAN ANTARA LINGKUNGAN FISIK TERHADAP KEJADIAN DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE KABUPATEN SLEMAN

Authors: Tika Pustika Fersari, Nur Afni Maftukhah, Zairinayati
Original abstract (English)
Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat yang penting di Indonesia, termasuk di Kabupaten Sleman. Berbagai penelitian menunjukkan bahwa kondisi lingkungan fisik rumah berperan dalam mendukung keberadaan vektor Aedes aegypti, namun hasil penelitian mengenai pengaruh ventilasi berkasa, kelembaban, dan pencahayaan terhadap kejadian DBD masih menunjukkan temuan yang belum konsisten. Penelitian ini bertujuan menganalisis hubungan antara lingkungan fisik terhadap kejadian DBD pada masyarakat di wilayah kerja Puskesmas Gamping I Kabupaten Sleman. Penelitian menggunakan pendekatan kuantitatif analitik observasional dengan rancangan case control. Sampel penelitian berjumlah 32 responden yang terdiri atas 8 kelompok kasus dan 24 kelompok kontrol dengan teknik purposive sampling. Variabel yang diteliti meliputi ventilasi berkasa, kelembaban, dan pencahayaan rumah. Data dikumpulkan melalui observasi dan dianalisis menggunakan uji Fisher Exact pada tingkat kepercayaan 95%. Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa ventilasi berkasa memiliki hubungan yang signifikan dengan kejadian DBD (p = 0,039; OR = 0,072). Pencahayaan rumah juga menunjukkan hubungan yang signifikan dengan kejadian DBD (p = 0,039; OR = 0,072). Sementara itu, kelembaban rumah tidak menunjukkan hubungan yang signifikan dengan kejadian DBD (p = 0,642; OR = 0,347). Penelitian ini menyimpulkan bahwa ventilasi berkasa dan pencahayaan merupakan determinan lingkungan fisik yang berhubungan dengan kejadian DBD pada masyarakat di wilayah kerja Puskesmas Gamping I Kabupaten Sleman. Temuan ini menunjukkan pentingnya perbaikan kondisi fisik rumah sebagai bagian dari strategi pengendalian DBD berbasis lingkungan.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat yang penting di Indonesia, termasuk di Kabupaten Sleman.
Design and population
Penelitian ini bertujuan menganalisis hubungan antara lingkungan fisik terhadap kejadian DBD pada masyarakat di wilayah kerja Puskesmas Gamping I Kabupaten Sleman.
Core methods
Penelitian menggunakan pendekatan kuantitatif analitik observasional dengan rancangan case control.
Main results
Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa ventilasi berkasa memiliki hubungan yang signifikan dengan kejadian DBD (p = 0,039; OR = 0,072). Pencahayaan rumah juga menunjukkan hubungan yang signifikan dengan kejadian DBD (p = 0,039; OR = 0,072). Sementara itu, kelembaban rumah tidak menunjukkan hubungan yang signifikan dengan kejadian DBD (p = 0,642; OR = 0,347).
Interpretation and novelty
Penelitian ini menyimpulkan bahwa ventilasi berkasa dan pencahayaan merupakan determinan lingkungan fisik yang berhubungan dengan kejadian DBD pada masyarakat di wilayah kerja Puskesmas Gamping I Kabupaten Sleman.
Scientific and public-health significance
Temuan ini menunjukkan pentingnya perbaikan kondisi fisik rumah sebagai bagian dari strategi pengendalian DBD berbasis lingkungan.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment.

登革热分析

Analisis Faktor yang Berhubungan dengan Angka Kejadian Demam Berdarah Dengue di Kelurahan Menteng Kota Palangka Raya
作者： Syamira Syamira, Husaini Husaini, Nelly Al Audhah, Erida Wydiamala, Ermina Istiqomah
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革出血热仍然是印度尼西亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,帕朗卡拉亚市的 发病率 病例不断增加。本研究旨在分析帕朗卡拉雅市门腾街道登革出血热 发病率 的相关因素。进行了一项采用病例对照设计的分析观察研究,涉及 130 名受访者,病例组与对照组的比例为 1:1。数据通过使用结构化问卷的访谈和使用清单的直接观察来收集。使用卡方检验进行统计分析。结果显示知识与登革出血热 发病率 之间没有显着关联 (p=1.000)。然而,态度(p=0.002)、储水条件(p=0.008)和住房条件(p=0.011)与发病率显着相关。态度和恶劣的住房条件增加了登革出血热的风险,而适当的储水则起到了保护因素的作用。总之,态度、储水条件和住房条件与帕朗卡拉雅市门腾街道的登革出血热发病率显着相关。关键词:态度、登革出血热、住房条件、知识、储水。摘要 Demam Berdarah 登革热 (DBD) tetap menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat yang serius di Indonesia,dengan angka kejadian yang terus meningkat di Kota Palangka Raya。Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk menganalisis faktor-faktor yang terkait dengan kejadian Demam Berdarah dengue di Kecamatan Menteng,Kota Palangka Raya. Penelitian 观察性肛门炎以 1:1 的比例进行控制,并以 1:1 的比例进行控制。数据将被显示在测量数据和观察数据中。分析统计数据并分析 uji 卡方。Hasil menunjukkan tidak ada hubungan yang signifikan antara pengetahuan dan kejadian Demam Berdarah Dengue (p=1,000)。Namun、sikap (p=0,002)、kondisi penyimpanan air (p=0,008)、dan kondisi perumahan (p=0,011) secara signifikan terkait dengan insidensi。如果您患有登革热,请注意预防登革热。Kesimpulannya、sikap、kondisi penyimpanan 空气、dan kondisi perumahan secara 表示 terkait dengan kejadian Demam Berdarah 登革热 di Kecamatan Menteng、Kota Palangka Raya。Kata Kunci:Sikap、Demam Berdarah 登革热、Kondisi Rumah、Pengetahuan、Penyimpanan Air。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革出血热仍然是印度尼西亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,帕朗卡拉亚市的 发病率 病例不断增加。本研究旨在分析帕朗卡拉雅市门腾街道登革出血热 发病率 的相关因素。
设计与对象
进行了一项采用病例对照设计的分析观察研究,涉及 130 名受访者,病例组与对照组的比例为 1:1。数据通过使用结构化问卷的访谈和使用清单的直接观察来收集。
核心方法
使用卡方检验进行统计分析。分析统计数据并分析 uji 卡方。
主要结果
结果显示知识与登革出血热 发病率 之间没有显着关联 (p=1.000)。然而,态度(p=0.002)、储水条件(p=0.008)和住房条件(p=0.011)与发病率显着相关。
解释与创新
态度和恶劣的住房条件增加了登革出血热的风险,而适当的储水则起到了保护因素的作用。研究结果强调了行为和环境因素在登革出血热 传播 动态中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为旨在改善态度、储水做法和住房条件的有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息,以减少门腾街道登革出血热的 发病率。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对自我报告数据的依赖和纯抽象的性质而受到限制。由于观察设计和缺乏纵向或实验数据,证据强度中等。

Analisis Faktor yang Berhubungan dengan Angka Kejadian Demam Berdarah Dengue di Kelurahan Menteng Kota Palangka Raya

Authors: Syamira Syamira, Husaini Husaini, Nelly Al Audhah, Erida Wydiamala, Ermina Istiqomah
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever remains a major public health problem in Indonesia, with increasing incidence in Palangka Raya City. This study aimed to analyze factors associated with the incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever in Menteng Subdistrict, Palangka Raya City. An analytical observational study with a case-control design was conducted involving 130 respondents with a 1:1 ratio between case and control groups. Data were collected through interviews using structured questionnaires and direct observation using checklists. Statistical analysis was performed using Chi-Square tests. The results showed no significant association between knowledge and Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever incidence (p=1.000). However, attitude (p=0.002), water storage conditions (p=0.008), and housing conditions (p=0.011) were significantly associated with the incidence. Attitude and poor housing conditions increased the risk of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever, while proper water storage acted as a protective factor. In conclusion, attitude, water storage conditions, and housing conditions are significantly associated with Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever incidence in Menteng Subdistrict, Palangka Raya City. Keywords: Attitude, Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever, Housing Condition, Knowledge, Water Storage. ABSTRAK Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) tetap menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat yang serius di Indonesia, dengan angka kejadian yang terus meningkat di Kota Palangka Raya. Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk menganalisis faktor-faktor yang terkait dengan kejadian Demam Berdarah Dengue di Kecamatan Menteng, Kota Palangka Raya. Penelitian observasional analitis dengan desain kasus-kontrol dilakukan dengan melibatkan 130 responden dengan rasio 1:1 antara kelompok kasus dan kelompok kontrol. Data dikumpulkan melalui wawancara menggunakan kuesioner terstruktur dan observasi langsung menggunakan daftar periksa. Analisis statistik dilakukan menggunakan uji Chi-Square. Hasil menunjukkan tidak ada hubungan yang signifikan antara pengetahuan dan kejadian Demam Berdarah Dengue (p=1,000). Namun, sikap (p=0,002), kondisi penyimpanan air (p=0,008), dan kondisi perumahan (p=0,011) secara signifikan terkait dengan insidensi. Sikap dan kondisi perumahan yang buruk meningkatkan risiko Demam Berdarah Dengue, sedangkan penyimpanan air yang baik berperan sebagai faktor pelindung. Kesimpulannya, sikap, kondisi penyimpanan air, dan kondisi perumahan secara signifikan terkait dengan kejadian Demam Berdarah Dengue di Kecamatan Menteng, Kota Palangka Raya. Kata Kunci: Sikap, Demam Berdarah Dengue, Kondisi Rumah, Pengetahuan, Penyimpanan Air.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever remains a major public health problem in Indonesia, with increasing incidence in Palangka Raya City. This study aimed to analyze factors associated with the incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever in Menteng Subdistrict, Palangka Raya City.
Design and population
An analytical observational study with a case-control design was conducted involving 130 respondents with a 1:1 ratio between case and control groups. Data were collected through interviews using structured questionnaires and direct observation using checklists.
Core methods
Statistical analysis was performed using Chi-Square tests. Analisis statistik dilakukan menggunakan uji Chi-Square.
Main results
The results showed no significant association between knowledge and Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever incidence (p=1.000). However, attitude (p=0.002), water storage conditions (p=0.008), and housing conditions (p=0.011) were significantly associated with the incidence.
Interpretation and novelty
Attitude and poor housing conditions increased the risk of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever, while proper water storage acted as a protective factor. The findings highlight the importance of behavioral and environmental factors in the transmission dynamics of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted public health interventions aimed at improving attitudes, water storage practices, and housing conditions to reduce the incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever in Menteng Subdistrict.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data and its abstract-only nature. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational design and lack of longitudinal or experimental data.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

孟加拉国达卡一家三级儿科医院 登革热 的临床特征和结果

Clinical Features and Outcomes of Dengue Fever in a Tertiary Paediatric Hospital in Dhaka, Bangladesh
作者： Md Aktaruzzaman, Sharmin Afroze, Farhana Ahmed, Mostafizur Rahman, Gazi Abdul Bari, Kiriti Kumar Dhali
摘要中文翻译全球范围内,儿童、青少年和年轻人构成了登革热的最大负担。早期诊断对于及时干预和改善预后至关重要。本横断面研究于2025年4月至9月期间在孟加拉国达卡Dr. M R Khan Shishu医院与研究所儿科收治的51名登革热患儿中进行,旨在评估儿童登革热感染的临床特征及预后。最常见的就诊年龄为2至10岁。最常见的症状为发热不足5天,占51%,其次为全身疼痛(47%)和厌食(38%),再次为皮疹(28%)、腹泻(22%)及咳嗽感冒(22%)。呕吐(55%)和腹痛(37%)是常见的警示征象,其次为休克(18%)。无合并症。所有患者均康复出院。儿童登革热的常见临床表现与其他研究结果一致。早期疑诊与有效治疗可带来良好的预后。《国际人类与健康科学》第04卷第26年10月,第236-239页
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估儿童登革热感染的临床特征并评估登革热的结果。在全球范围内,儿童、青少年和年轻人是登革热的最大负担。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究是在 2025 年 4 月至 9 月期间在孟加拉国达卡 M R Khan Shishu 博士医院和研究所儿科收治的 51 名 登革热 儿童中进行的。所有患者均已康复并出院回家。
核心方法
该研究在孟加拉国达卡的一家三级儿科医院进行,旨在评估儿童登革热感染的临床特征。最常见的发病年龄为 2 至 10 岁。
主要结果
最常见的症状是发烧不到 5 天,占 51%,其次是身体疼痛 (47%) 和厌食 (38%),然后是皮疹 (28%)、腹泻 (22%) 以及咳嗽和感冒 (22%)。呕吐(55%)和腹痛(37%)是常见的警告特征,其次是休克(18%)。
解释与创新
其他研究表明,儿童登革热具有常见的表现。早期怀疑和有效管理可以带来有利的结果。
科研与公卫意义
早期 诊断 对于及时干预和获得更好的结果至关重要。这些发现可以为儿童 登革热 的有针对性的诊断和管理策略的制定提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。该证据基于孟加拉国达卡的一家三级儿科医院,可能无法推广到其他人群。信心程度适中。

Clinical Features and Outcomes of Dengue Fever in a Tertiary Paediatric Hospital in Dhaka, Bangladesh

Authors: Md Aktaruzzaman, Sharmin Afroze, Farhana Ahmed, Mostafizur Rahman, Gazi Abdul Bari, Kiriti Kumar Dhali
Original abstract (English)
Globally, children, adolescents, and young adults constitute the largest burden of Dengue. Early diagnosis is crucial for timely intervention and better outcome. This cross-sectional study was conducted among 51 children with dengue fever admitted in the Department of Paediatrics, Dr. M R Khan Shishu Hospital & Institute, Dhaka, Bangladesh, between April and September of 2025, to assess the clinical features of the dengue infection in children and to evaluate the outcomes. The most common age of presentation was between 2 and 10 years. The most common presenting symptom was fever less than 5 days, seen in 51% cases followed by body ache (47%) and anorexia (38%) followed by rash (28%), diarrhea (22%) and cough and cold (22%). Vomiting (55%) and abdominal pain (37%) were common warning features followed by shock (18%). No co-morbidities were found. All the patients were recovered and discharged to home. Dengue in children bears the common presentation as evident by other research studies. Early suspicion and effective management can lead to a favorable outcome. Serotype skewing and immune imprinting shape response to the tetravalent dengue virus Qdenga vaccine
作者： Luciana Conde, Valter Monteiro, Joao C.R. Freitas, Jameson Crandell, Lauren Lawres, Fernanda Tana, Mallery I. Breban, Fahima Akther, Jordan Polster, Abigail Porzucek

Serotype skewing and immune imprinting shape response to the tetravalent dengue virus Qdenga vaccine

Authors: Luciana Conde, Valter Monteiro, Joao C.R. Freitas, Jameson Crandell, Lauren Lawres, Fernanda Tana, Mallery I. Breban, Fahima Akther, Jordan Polster, Abigail Porzucek
Original abstract (English)
Dengue causes over 390 million annual infections globally. Qdenga (TAK-003), the only widely available dengue vaccine, has an incompletely defined immunological profile. We performed longitudinal immunological profiling of 110 adults from a dengue-endemic region, including older adults (≥65 years), to assess the effects of immune imprinting and vaccine design on humoral and cellular immunity. Qdenga elicited B and T cell activation across groups but generated serostatus-dependent, serotype-skewed antibody responses. Only 8% of DENV-naïve individuals developed tetravalent neutralizing responses, whereas one-third responded to a single serotype, predominantly DENV-2, the vaccine backbone. DENV-exposed individuals mounted broader responses shaped by preexisting immunity, yet DENV-4 neutralization remained consistently poor. Neutralizing antibody titers plateaued after the first dose, with no increase following the second. These findings clarify why balanced tetravalent immunity is rarely achieved with Qdenga and demonstrate that both immune imprinting and vaccine backbone limit the breadth and magnitude of the responses. This has important implications for deployment, long-term protection in naive populations, and DENV-4–targeted boosters.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue causes over 390 million annual infections globally.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
We performed longitudinal immunological profiling of 110 adults from a dengue-endemic region, including older adults (≥65 years), to assess the effects of immune imprinting and vaccine design on humoral and cellular immunity.
Main results
DENV-exposed individuals mounted broader responses shaped by preexisting immunity, yet DENV-4 neutralization remained consistently poor.
Interpretation and novelty
Neutralizing antibody titers plateaued after the first dose, with no increase following the second.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings clarify why balanced tetravalent immunity is rarely achieved with Qdenga and demonstrate that both immune imprinting and vaccine backbone limit the breadth and magnitude of the responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
This has important implications for deployment, long-term protection in naive populations, and DENV-4–targeted boosters.

感染沃尔巴克氏体的埃及伊蚊对流行地区登革热 发病率 的影响:系统评价和荟萃分析。

Impact of Wolbachia-infected Aedes aegypti on dengue incidence in endemic regions: a systematic review and meta-analysis.
作者： Husni Mubarak Zainal, Ricardo Soares Guimarães, Abdullah Al Owesie, Laura Santos Ribeiro, Andrea Roman-Pimentel, Tanika Sharma, Vitoria Nallin de Godoy, Rafael Bezerra Mendes
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该评价评估了释放感染沃尔巴克氏体的埃及伊蚊作为生物防治干预措施以减少流行地区登革热 发病率 的有效性,重点关注登革热流行环境中的人群水平和个体水平结果(A1、A2)。
证据基础与方法
A systematic review and meta-analysis was conducted, searching PubMed, Embase, and the Cochrane Library until 30 April 2025 for randomized controlled trials and quasi-experimental studies in dengue-endemic settings (A3, A4). Population-level effectiveness was assessed using pooled incidence rate ratios (IRRs), and individual-level protective efficacy using pooled odds ratios (ORs) with random-effects meta-analysis and restricted maximum likelihood with Hartung-Knapp adjustment (A5, A6). Subgroup analyses examined geographic region and deployment coverage (A7). Nine studies involving over 8 million individuals were included (A8).
共识与结论
沃尔巴克氏体部署与登革热发病率的显著降低相关,汇总发病率比值比(IRR)为0.15(95% 置信区间 0.05-0.39),相当于估计减少了85%(A9)。全区域部署的效果优于部分区域部署,前者IRR为0.046(95% 置信区间 0.038-0.055),后者IRR为0.22(95% 置信区间 0.07-0.74)(A10)。
争议与缺口
探索性个体水平分析得出的汇总 OR 为 0.29 (95% CI 0.01-8.41),具有显着异质性 (I2 = 86.5%),表明个体水平保护功效估计存在不确定性 (A11)。
科研与实践启示
该审查强调需要进一步研究,以完善个人水平的疗效估计并解决研究设计和部署策略中的异质性,以优化基于沃尔巴克氏体的登革热控制计划(A11)。

Impact of Wolbachia-infected Aedes aegypti on dengue incidence in endemic regions: a systematic review and meta-analysis.

Authors: Husni Mubarak Zainal, Ricardo Soares Guimarães, Abdullah Al Owesie, Laura Santos Ribeiro, Andrea Roman-Pimentel, Tanika Sharma, Vitoria Nallin de Godoy, Rafael Bezerra Mendes
Original abstract (English)
Dengue remains a major public health challenge in endemic regions, and conventional vector-control strategies have shown limited long-term effectiveness. The release of Wolbachia-infected Aedes aegypti mosquitoes has emerged as a novel biocontrol strategy to reduce dengue transmission. We conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis to evaluate the effectiveness of Wolbachia deployments in reducing dengue incidence. We searched PubMed, Embase, and the Cochrane Library until 30 April 2025 for randomized controlled trials and quasi-experimental studies conducted in dengue-endemic settings. Population-level effectiveness was assessed using pooled incidence rate ratios (IRRs), and individual-level protective efficacy using pooled odds ratios (ORs). Random-effects meta-analysis with restricted maximum likelihood with Hartung-Knapp adjustment was applied. Subgroup analyses examined geographic region and deployment coverage. Nine studies involving >8 million individuals were included. Wolbachia deployment was associated with a substantial reduction in dengue incidence (pooled IRR 0.15, 95% CI 0.05-0.39), corresponding to an estimated 85% reduction. Full-area deployments demonstrated greater reductions (IRR 0.046, 95% CI 0.038-0.055) than partial-area deployments (IRR 0.22, 95% CI 0.07-0.74). Exploratory individual-level analyses yielded a pooled OR of 0.29 (95% CI 0.01-8.41) with substantial heterogeneity (I2 = 86.5%).
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review evaluates the effectiveness of releasing Wolbachia-infected Aedes aegypti mosquitoes as a biocontrol intervention to reduce dengue incidence in endemic regions, focusing on population-level and individual-level outcomes in dengue-endemic settings (A1, A2).
Evidence base and method
A systematic review and meta-analysis was conducted, searching PubMed, Embase, and the Cochrane Library until 30 April 2025 for randomized controlled trials and quasi-experimental studies in dengue-endemic settings (A3, A4). Population-level effectiveness was assessed using pooled incidence rate ratios (IRRs), and individual-level protective efficacy using pooled odds ratios (ORs) with random-effects meta-analysis and restricted maximum likelihood with Hartung-Knapp adjustment (A5, A6). Subgroup analyses examined geographic region and deployment coverage (A7). Nine studies involving over 8 million individuals were included (A8).
Consensus and conclusions
Wolbachia deployment was associated with a substantial reduction in dengue incidence, with a pooled IRR of 0.15 (95% CI 0.05-0.39), corresponding to an estimated 85% reduction (A9). Full-area deployments demonstrated greater reductions (IRR 0.046, 95% CI 0.038-0.055) than partial-area deployments (IRR 0.22, 95% CI 0.07-0.74) (A10).
Controversies and gaps
Exploratory individual-level analyses yielded a pooled OR of 0.29 (95% CI 0.01-8.41) with substantial heterogeneity (I2 = 86.5%), indicating uncertainty in individual-level protective efficacy estimates (A11).
Research and practice implications
The review highlights the need for further research to refine individual-level efficacy estimates and address heterogeneity in study designs and deployment strategies to optimize Wolbachia-based dengue control programs (A11).

溶瘤病毒疗法中新出现和重新出现的病毒病原体:综合综述。

Emerging and Re-Emerging Viral Pathogens in Oncolytic Virotherapy: A Comprehensive Review.
作者： Xin An, Nan Liu, Junchao Liu
摘要中文翻译溶瘤病毒疗法最近成为一种有前途的免疫肿瘤学策略,利用病毒病原体的天然溶细胞潜力来选择性地靶向和破坏恶性细胞。尽管各种工程病毒,如腺病毒、牛痘病毒和单纯疱疹病毒,目前正处于临床评估阶段,但具有内在溶瘤潜力的新出现和重新出现的病毒,包括寨卡病毒(ZIKV)、严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(SARS-CoV-2)、登革病毒(DENV)和西尼罗河病毒(WNV)也显示出有希望的结果。这些新出现和重新出现的病毒病原体表现出特定的肿瘤趋向性、免疫逃避策略和免疫原性细胞死亡机制,可用于下一代治疗平台。尽管如此,与安全性、病毒致病性、宿主 免疫应答 以及不受控制的病毒传播风险相关的担忧提出了重大的转化挑战。本综述全面概述了溶瘤病毒治疗中的新型候选病毒,包括 ZIKV、SARS-CoV-2、DENV 和 WNV、它们的肿瘤靶向分子机制,以及旨在提高治疗效果和生物安全性的基因工程的最新进展。此外,这篇综述描述了将新兴病毒治疗药物整合到精准肿瘤学中的临床观点、监管考虑因素和未来方向。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述在溶瘤病毒疗法的背景下研究了 登革病毒(DENV-1 至 DENV-4)和登革热疾病,重点关注 登革病毒 由于其肿瘤趋向性、免疫逃避和免疫原性细胞死亡机制而作为治疗药物的潜力。
证据基础与方法
该综述是一篇叙述性综合文章,评估了新出现和重新出现的病毒病原体(包括 DENV)的内在溶瘤潜力和在肿瘤学中的治疗应用。
共识与结论
登革病毒 展示了特定的肿瘤趋向性和免疫原性特性,可用于下一代溶瘤病毒治疗平台。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了转化挑战,包括安全性问题、病毒致病性、宿主 免疫应答 以及不受控制的病毒传播风险,这些挑战对于基于 DENV 的疗法来说仍未解决。
科研与实践启示
未来研究应优先通过基因工程提升登革病毒基础的溶瘤剂的治疗效果与生物安全性,同时推进临床转化及监管路径开发。

Emerging and Re-Emerging Viral Pathogens in Oncolytic Virotherapy: A Comprehensive Review.

Authors: Xin An, Nan Liu, Junchao Liu
Original abstract (English)
Oncolytic virotherapy has recently emerged as a promising immuno-oncological strategy, utilising the natural cytolytic potentials of viral pathogens to selectively target and destroy malignant cells. Although various engineered viruses, such as adenoviruses, vaccinia virus, and herpes simplex virus, are currently in clinical evaluation, emerging and re-emerging viruses with intrinsic oncolytic potential, including Zika virus (ZIKV), Severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), Dengue virus (DENV), and West Nile virus (WNV), have also shown promising results. These emerging and re-emerging viral pathogens show specific tumour tropism, immune evasion strategies, and immunogenic cell death mechanisms that could be harnessed for next-generation therapeutic platforms. Nonetheless, concerns related to safety, viral pathogenicity, host immune responses, and the risk of uncontrolled viral dissemination present significant translational challenges. The present review provides a comprehensive overview of novel viral candidates, including ZIKV, SARS-CoV-2, DENV, and WNV, in oncolytic virotherapy, their molecular mechanisms of tumour targeting, and current advancements in genetic engineering aimed at improving therapeutic efficacy and biosafety. Moreover, this review describes clinical perspectives, regulatory considerations, and future directions in integrating emerging virotherapeutic agents into precision oncology.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines Dengue virus (DENV-1 to DENV-4) and dengue disease within the context of oncolytic virotherapy, focusing on the potential of dengue viruses as therapeutic agents due to their tumour tropism, immune evasion, and immunogenic cell death mechanisms.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis that evaluates emerging and re-emerging viral pathogens, including DENV, for their intrinsic oncolytic potential and therapeutic applications in oncology.
Consensus and conclusions
Dengue virus demonstrates specific tumour tropism and immunogenic properties that could be leveraged for next-generation oncolytic virotherapy platforms.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights translational challenges, including safety concerns, viral pathogenicity, host immune responses, and the risk of uncontrolled viral dissemination, which remain unresolved for DENV-based therapies.
Research and practice implications
Future research should prioritize genetic engineering to enhance therapeutic efficacy and biosafety of DENV-based oncolytic agents, alongside clinical translation and regulatory pathway development.
